
===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_long_tail_scheduling.py =====
"""Queue behavior with waiting provider calls; no network or fake provider results."""
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models import ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeDispatchItem
from app.runtime_queue import ClaimRequest, claim_runtime_item
from app.workflow_runtime import ProductionRunRequest, create_production_run
from test_workflow_runtime import _engine, _seed_active_workflow
from test_model_quota import _pool
from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota, release_model_quota

NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 10, 22, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('sla_enabled', ['0', '1'])
def test_multiple_workflows_share_wait_slots_instead_of_old_backlog_monopolizing(tmp_path, monkeypatch, sla_enabled):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', sla_enabled)
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    _assert_workflow_fairness(engine)
    engine.dispose()


def _assert_workflow_fairness(engine):
    with Session(engine) as db:
        a = _seed_active_workflow(db, workflow_key='batch.large')
        b = _seed_active_workflow(db, workflow_key='batch.small')
        old = [ready(db, a, f'large-{i}', age=600)[0].id for i in range(10)]
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == old[0]
        newcomer, _ = ready(db, b, 'small-1', age=1)
        # One old workflow already has a long provider wait. A new category
        # gets the next same-priority slot, even with ten older FIFO items.
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == newcomer.id
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == old[1]


@pytest.mark.parametrize('sla_enabled', ['0', '1'])
def test_mysql_workflow_fairness_uses_current_shared_occupancy(mysql_engine, monkeypatch, sla_enabled):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', sla_enabled)
    from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
    with mysql_engine.begin() as conn:
        create_mysql_baseline(conn)
    _assert_workflow_fairness(mysql_engine)


def ready(db, version, key, *, kind="model_call", sequence=0, age=30):
    run = create_production_run(db, ProductionRunRequest(
        workflow_version_id=version.id, idempotency_key=key,
        category_key="model_3d_su", queue_class="validation",
        runtime_context={}, input_manifest={}, owner="platform"), actor="test")
    attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id))
    # This is a queue-only fixture: no model executor sees the identity script.
    attempt.step_type, attempt.sequence = kind, sequence
    dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id))
    dispatch.available_at = NOW - timedelta(seconds=age)
    db.flush()
    return attempt, dispatch


def claim(db, now=NOW):
    return claim_runtime_item(db, ClaimRequest(worker_id="test", global_limit=2000, now=now))


def hint(attempt, dispatch, pool, *, headroom=0):
    attempt.status = "retryable"
    attempt.checkpoint_json = json.dumps({"keep_me": "correction", "_model_quota_wait": {
        "pool_id": pool.id, "headroom": headroom,
        "recheck_at": (NOW + timedelta(seconds=60)).timestamp()}})
    dispatch.last_error_code = "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"


def test_ready_rule_bypasses_old_prompt_backlog_but_fifo_gets_a_turn(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        first, _ = ready(db, version, "old-a", age=45)
        local_ids = {ready(db, version, f"rule-{i}", kind="rule_eval", sequence=3)[0].id for i in range(8)}
        for _ in range(4):
            assert claim(db).attempt_id in local_ids
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == first.id
    engine.dispose()


def test_b_continuation_precedes_new_a(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        first, _ = ready(db, version, "new-a", age=45)
        b, _ = ready(db, version, "b", sequence=6)
        prior = ProductionStepAttempt(run_id=b.run_id, step_key="completed-a", step_type="model_call",
            sequence=2, script_version_id=b.script_version_id, status="succeeded", attempt_no=1,
            idempotency_key="completed-a", input_manifest_json="{}", input_hash="a"*64,
            output_manifest_json="{}", checkpoint_json="{}")
        db.add(prior)
        db.flush()
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == b.id
    engine.dispose()


def test_old_b_continuation_still_precedes_old_a_backlog(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        ready(db, version, "old-a", age=1800)
        b, _ = ready(db, version, "old-b", sequence=6, age=900)
        db.add(ProductionStepAttempt(run_id=b.run_id, step_key="completed-a",
            step_type="model_call", sequence=2, script_version_id=b.script_version_id,
            status="succeeded", attempt_no=1, idempotency_key="prior-a",
            input_manifest_json="{}", input_hash="a"*64, output_manifest_json="{}", checkpoint_json="{}"))
        db.flush()
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == b.id
    engine.dispose()


def test_pacing_wait_does_not_wake_just_because_concurrency_is_free(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        pool = _pool(db, key="paced", concurrency=24)
        attempt, dispatch = ready(db, version, "paced")
        hint(attempt, dispatch, pool)
        dispatch.last_error_code = "model_batch_submission_wait"
        dispatch.available_at = NOW + timedelta(seconds=45)
        db.flush()
        assert claim(db) is None
        assert claim(db, NOW + timedelta(seconds=44)) is None
        assert claim(db, NOW + timedelta(seconds=45)).attempt_id == attempt.id
    engine.dispose()


def test_saturated_pool_skips_waiters_and_wakes_on_release(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        pool = _pool(db, key="long-tail", concurrency=1)
        pool.lease_seconds = 86400
        lease = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="long-tail", now=NOW)
        blocked, dispatch = ready(db, version, "blocked", age=1000)
        hint(blocked, dispatch, pool)
        rule, _ = ready(db, version, "local", kind="rule_eval", sequence=3)
        db.flush()
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == rule.id
        assert claim(db) is None  # Not another image preparation / quota rejection.
        assert blocked.attempt_no == 1
        assert json.loads(blocked.checkpoint_json)["keep_me"] == "correction"
        release_model_quota(db, lease_token=lease.lease_token, now=NOW)
        # Releasing capacity wakes without waiting for the retry timer.
        dispatch.available_at = NOW + timedelta(seconds=5)
        db.flush()
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == blocked.id
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("cooldown,headroom", [(True, 0), (False, 1)])
def test_hint_respects_cooldown_and_online_headroom(tmp_path, cooldown, headroom):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        pool = _pool(db, key="protected", concurrency=2)
        acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="one", now=NOW)
        if cooldown:
            pool.cooldown_until = NOW + timedelta(seconds=30)
        blocked, dispatch = ready(db, version, "blocked")
        hint(blocked, dispatch, pool, headroom=headroom)
        db.flush()
        assert claim(db) is None
        # Stale hints must never permanently hide work after route/pool changes.
        assert claim(db, NOW + timedelta(seconds=61)).attempt_id == blocked.id
    engine.dispose()


def test_saturation_stops_before_media_preparation_and_preserves_frozen_run(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    from app.models import ProductionRun, WorkflowModelInvocation
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
    with sessions() as db:
        run, attempt, node, model, _ = _case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        pool = _pool(db, key="saturated-before-image", concurrency=1)
        pool.lease_seconds = 86400
        model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
        acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="existing-call")
        attempt.checkpoint_json = '{"operator_note":"keep"}'
        db.commit()
        run_id, attempt_id = run.id, attempt.id
        frozen = run.snapshot_json
        token, owner = attempt.lease_token, attempt.lease_owner

    def forbidden_preparation(*args, **kwargs):
        pytest.fail("A saturated model pool must not read/prepare images again")
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "prepare_prompt_invocation", forbidden_preparation)
    operation = executor._real_prompt_attempt_steps(
        attempt_id=attempt_id, node=node, run_snapshot=json.loads(frozen),
        input_manifest={}, lease_token=token, worker_id=owner, model_client_type=None)
    with pytest.raises(executor.FixtureExecutionError) as error:
        next(operation)
    assert error.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).snapshot_json == frozen
        checkpoint = json.loads(db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id).checkpoint_json)
        assert checkpoint["operator_note"] == "keep"
        assert checkpoint["_model_quota_wait"]["pool_id"] == pool.id
        assert db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)).all() == []
    engine.dispose()


def test_thousand_waiters_do_not_delay_ready_output_or_spend_model_attempts(tmp_path):
    from app.workflow_runtime import complete_runtime_step
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        pool = _pool(db, key="thousand-long-tails", concurrency=24)
        pool.lease_seconds = 86400
        leases = [acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id,
                  owner_key=f"already-in-flight:{i}", now=NOW) for i in range(22)]
        for i in range(1000):
            attempt, dispatch = ready(db, version, f"backlog-{i}", age=1000)
            hint(attempt, dispatch, pool, headroom=2)
        local, _ = ready(db, version, "ready-output", kind="projection", sequence=12)
        online, online_dispatch = ready(db, version, "online-fallback")
        hint(online, online_dispatch, pool, headroom=0)
        db.flush()
        claimed = claim(db)
        assert claimed.attempt_id == local.id
        complete_runtime_step(db, local.id, claimed.lease_token, {"ok": True}, now=NOW)
        assert local.status == "succeeded"
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == online.id
        assert claim(db) is None
        # 1000 queued items never start preparation or burn a model retry.
        assert db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
                         .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc()).limit(1)) == 1
        assert db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)).all() == []
        release_model_quota(db, lease_token=leases[0].lease_token, now=NOW)
        assert claim(db) is not None
    engine.dispose()


def test_preflight_does_not_block_authoritative_replay_of_own_reservation(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case
    from app.runtime_admission import preflight_model_pool
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run, attempt, node, model, _ = _case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        pool = _pool(db, key="own-reservation", concurrency=1)
        model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
        db.flush()
        reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=attempt.id, node=node,
            run_snapshot=json.loads(run.snapshot_json), invocation_key="root")
        db.commit()
        preflight_model_pool(db, run=run, attempt=attempt,
            lease_token=attempt.lease_token, worker_id=attempt.lease_owner)
        assert "_model_quota_wait" not in json.loads(attempt.checkpoint_json)
    engine.dispose()


def test_online_quota_wait_does_not_reuse_expired_batch_deadline(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case
    from app.runtime_admission import preflight_model_pool
    from app.workflow_model_budget import WorkflowModelBudgetError

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run, attempt, _, model, _ = _case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        limits = snapshot["runtime_context"]["real_execution_node_limits"][attempt.step_key]
        limits["online_fallback_limits"] = dict(limits)
        run.snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot)
        pool = _pool(db, key="online-full", concurrency=1)
        model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
        acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key="existing")
        db.commit()
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError):
            preflight_model_pool(db, run=run, attempt=attempt,
                lease_token=attempt.lease_token, worker_id=attempt.lease_owner,
                transport_mode="online", transport_deadline=now-timedelta(seconds=1))
        wait = json.loads(attempt.checkpoint_json)["_model_quota_wait"]
        assert wait["recheck_at"] >= (now+timedelta(seconds=30)).timestamp()
    engine.dispose()


def test_postgres_text_checkpoint_is_cast_before_json_operators():
    from sqlalchemy.dialects import postgresql
    from app.runtime_admission import ready_dispatch_predicate
    sql = str(select(ProductionStepAttempt.id).where(
        ready_dispatch_predicate(NOW, dialect="postgresql")).compile(dialect=postgresql.dialect()))
    assert "CAST(production_step_attempts.checkpoint_json AS JSON)" in sql
    assert "production_step_attempts.checkpoint_json ->" not in sql


def test_prior_attempt_same_key_does_not_bypass_preflight(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case
    from app.runtime_admission import preflight_model_pool
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation, WorkflowModelBudgetError

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run, old, node, model, _ = _case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        pool = _pool(db, key="prior-attempt", concurrency=1)
        model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
        db.flush()
        reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=old.id, node=node,
            run_snapshot=json.loads(run.snapshot_json), invocation_key="root")
        attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(run_id=old.run_id, step_key=old.step_key, step_type=old.step_type,
            sequence=old.sequence, script_version_id=old.script_version_id, status="running", attempt_no=2,
            idempotency_key="new-attempt", input_manifest_json=old.input_manifest_json, input_hash=old.input_hash,
            output_manifest_json="{}", checkpoint_json="{}", lease_token=old.lease_token, lease_owner=old.lease_owner)
        db.add(attempt)
        db.commit()
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as error:
            preflight_model_pool(db, run=run, attempt=attempt,
                lease_token=attempt.lease_token, worker_id=attempt.lease_owner)
        assert error.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
    engine.dispose()


def test_aged_fifo_survives_cross_queue_phase_aliasing(tmp_path):
    from app.models import QueueSchedulerState
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        first, _ = ready(db, version, "old-a", age=180)
        for i in range(5):
            ready(db, version, f"old-continuation-{i}", kind="rule_eval", sequence=3, age=150)
        # This queue may never land on the global fifth-dispatch FIFO turn.
        db.add(QueueSchedulerState(id=1, global_limit=2000, policy_version="queue-policy-v1", dispatch_count=6))
        db.flush()
        # Four continuation turns belong to this queue even when other queue
        # classes advance the global dispatch phase between each claim.
        for _ in range(4):
            assert claim(db).attempt_id != first.id
            state = db.get(QueueSchedulerState, 1)
            state.dispatch_count += 7
            db.commit()
        assert claim(db).attempt_id == first.id
    engine.dispose()


def test_unscoped_claim_rechecks_global_limit_after_scheduler_lock(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """无模块池时，锁前的在飞计数是旧快照；锁后必须复核，不能突破全局上限。"""

    import app.runtime_queue as rq
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        for i in range(3):
            ready(db, version, f'cap-{i}', age=60)
        first = claim_runtime_item(db, ClaimRequest(worker_id="a", global_limit=1, now=NOW))
        assert first is not None
        real_counts = rq._queue_counts

        def stale_counts(db_, *, now, predicate=()):
            # 另一个领取者在我们读完计数、拿到调度锁之间提交了领取。
            pending, _running = real_counts(db_, now=now, predicate=predicate)
            return pending, {queue: 0 for queue in rq.QUEUE_CLASSES}

        monkeypatch.setattr(rq, "_queue_counts", stale_counts)
        second = claim_runtime_item(db, ClaimRequest(worker_id="b", global_limit=1, now=NOW))
        assert second is None
        leased = db.scalars(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "leased")).all()
        assert len(leased) == 1
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_outbox.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from datetime import timedelta
from threading import Lock

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.postgres_baseline import create_postgres_baseline
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
from app.models import (
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.runtime_outbox import (
    DeliveryReceipt,
    RuntimeOutboxError,
    RuntimeOutboxRequest,
    claim_outbox_event,
    complete_outbox_event,
    enqueue_runtime_outbox,
    fail_outbox_event,
)
from app.runtime_queue import RuntimeFailureClassifier
from app.workflow_composer_runtime import ensure_composer_fixture_version
from app.workflow_runtime import (
    ProductionRunRequest,
    claim_next_runtime_step,
    complete_runtime_step,
    create_production_run,
)
from app.workflow_snapshot_store import load_snapshot_blob


def _hash(value: object) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _mechanism_version(db: Session, *, suffix: str = "unit") -> WorkflowVersion:
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key=f"runtime.outbox.{suffix}",
        name="Runtime outbox test",
        description="",
        owner="test",
        allowed_categories_json='["space_image"]',
        status="active",
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    version = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version="1",
        status="active",
        workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
        step_manifest_json="[]",
        edge_manifest_json="[]",
        input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "category_key": "space_image",
                "lane_key": "incremental",
                "run_modes": ["incremental"],
                "queue_class": "production_batch",
                "layout": {},
            }
        ),
        canonical_hash="a" * 64,
        validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(version)
    db.flush()
    return version


def _request(
    version: WorkflowVersion,
    *,
    event_key: str = "event:asset-42:v1",
    payload: dict | None = None,
    max_attempts: int = 3,
    revision: int = 1,
    review_state: str = "auto",
) -> RuntimeOutboxRequest:
    return RuntimeOutboxRequest(
        event_key=event_key,
        target_contract_key="logical.space_image.output",
        business_idempotency_key="space_image:asset:42:node:projection",
        mechanism_version_id=version.id,
        payload=payload or {"asset_id": 42, "grade": "A"},
        max_attempts=max_attempts,
        revision=revision,
        review_state=review_state,
    )


def test_same_outbox_identity_reuses_one_event_and_detects_drift(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'outbox-idempotency.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism_version(db)
            request = _request(version)
            first = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, request)
            second = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, request)
            db.commit()

            assert first.id == second.id
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            ) == 1
            assert first.payload_blob.snapshot_hash == first.payload_hash
            assert load_snapshot_blob(first.payload_blob) == dict(request.payload)

            with pytest.raises(
                RuntimeOutboxError,
                match="outbox_idempotency_conflict",
            ):
                enqueue_runtime_outbox(
                    db,
                    _request(version, payload={"asset_id": 42, "grade": "B"}),
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_outbox_keeps_multiple_revisions_per_business_identity(tmp_path) -> None:
    """ADR-0064：同一业务身份按 revision 并存；同 revision 不同内容仍 fail-closed。"""

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'outbox-revision.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism_version(db)
            first = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, _request(version))
            second = enqueue_runtime_outbox(
                db,
                _request(
                    version,
                    event_key="runtime-outbox:revision-2",
                    payload={"asset_id": 42, "grade": "B"},
                    revision=2,
                    review_state="reviewed",
                ),
            )
            db.commit()
            assert first.id != second.id
            assert (first.revision, first.review_state) == (1, "auto")
            assert (second.revision, second.review_state) == (2, "reviewed")
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            ) == 2
            with pytest.raises(
                RuntimeOutboxError,
                match="outbox_idempotency_conflict",
            ):
                enqueue_runtime_outbox(
                    db,
                    _request(
                        version,
                        event_key="runtime-outbox:revision-2-drift",
                        payload={"asset_id": 42, "grade": "C"},
                        revision=2,
                        review_state="reviewed",
                    ),
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_delivery_requires_matching_read_back_receipt(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'outbox-receipt.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism_version(db)
            event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, _request(version))
            db.commit()
            claimed = claim_outbox_event(db, "delivery-worker")
            assert claimed is not None and claimed.lease_token
            lease_token = claimed.lease_token
            db.commit()

            with pytest.raises(
                RuntimeOutboxError,
                match="outbox_receipt_mismatch",
            ):
                complete_outbox_event(
                    db,
                    claimed.id,
                    lease_token,
                    DeliveryReceipt(
                        business_idempotency_key=claimed.business_idempotency_key,
                        mechanism_version_id=claimed.mechanism_version_id,
                        payload_hash="f" * 64,
                        external_record_key="shadow-row-42",
                    ),
                )
            db.rollback()
            persisted = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, claimed.id)
            assert persisted is not None
            assert persisted.status == "leased"

            delivered = complete_outbox_event(
                db,
                claimed.id,
                lease_token,
                DeliveryReceipt(
                    business_idempotency_key=claimed.business_idempotency_key,
                    mechanism_version_id=claimed.mechanism_version_id,
                    payload_hash=claimed.payload_hash,
                    external_record_key="shadow-row-42",
                ),
            )
            db.commit()
            assert delivered.status == "delivered"
            assert delivered.delivered_at is not None
            assert delivered.lease_token is None
            assert "shadow-row-42" in delivered.delivery_receipt_json

            repeated = complete_outbox_event(
                db,
                delivered.id,
                "worker-did-not-observe-commit",
                DeliveryReceipt(
                    business_idempotency_key=delivered.business_idempotency_key,
                    mechanism_version_id=delivered.mechanism_version_id,
                    payload_hash=delivered.payload_hash,
                    external_record_key="shadow-row-42",
                ),
            )
            assert repeated.id == delivered.id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_outbox_failure_retries_then_dead_letters(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'outbox-failure.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism_version(db)
            event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(
                db,
                _request(version, max_attempts=2),
            )
            db.commit()
            first = claim_outbox_event(db, "delivery-worker")
            assert first is not None and first.lease_token
            first_token = first.lease_token
            failed_at = first.leased_at + timedelta(seconds=1)
            transient = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
                TimeoutError("downstream timed out before acknowledgement"),
                error_code="downstream_timeout",
            )
            failed = fail_outbox_event(
                db,
                first.id,
                first_token,
                transient,
                now=failed_at,
            )
            assert failed.status == "queued"
            assert failed.available_at > failed_at
            retry_at = failed.available_at
            db.commit()

            second = claim_outbox_event(
                db,
                "delivery-worker-2",
                now=retry_at,
            )
            assert second is not None and second.lease_token
            contract = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
                ValueError("downstream field contract rejected"),
                error_code="downstream_contract_invalid",
                contract_error=True,
            )
            terminal = fail_outbox_event(
                db,
                second.id,
                second.lease_token,
                contract,
                now=retry_at + timedelta(seconds=1),
            )
            db.commit()
            assert terminal.status == "dead_lettered"
            assert terminal.dead_lettered_at is not None
            assert terminal.last_error_code == "downstream_contract_invalid"
            assert terminal.lease_token is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_expired_outbox_lease_is_reclaimed_once_then_dead_lettered(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'outbox-expired.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _mechanism_version(db)
            enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, _request(version, max_attempts=2))
            db.commit()
            first = claim_outbox_event(
                db,
                "delivery-worker-1",
                lease_seconds=1,
            )
            assert first is not None and first.lease_token
            first_token = first.lease_token
            first_expiry = first.lease_expires_at
            db.commit()

            second = claim_outbox_event(
                db,
                "delivery-worker-2",
                now=first_expiry + timedelta(seconds=1),
                lease_seconds=1,
            )
            assert second is not None
            assert second.id == first.id
            assert second.attempt_count == 2
            assert second.lease_token != first_token
            second_expiry = second.lease_expires_at
            db.commit()

            assert claim_outbox_event(
                db,
                "delivery-worker-3",
                now=second_expiry + timedelta(seconds=1),
            ) is None
            db.commit()
            terminal = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, first.id)
            assert terminal is not None
            assert terminal.status == "dead_lettered"
            assert terminal.last_error_code == (
                "outbox_delivery_lease_exhausted"
            )
            assert terminal.lease_token is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _incremental_projection_run(
    db: Session,
    *,
    run_mode: str = "incremental",
    node_ids: tuple[str, ...] = ("downstream",),
) -> tuple[ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt]:
    target = create_connector_contract_version(
        db,
        contract_key="space-image-output",
        direction="downstream",
        category_key="space_image",
        logical_locator="logical.space_image.output",
        adapter_key="api-json-v1",
        schema_fingerprint="d" * 64,
        field_mapping={"asset_id": "asset_id"},
        cursor_contract={},
        idempotency_contract={"field": "business_idempotency_key"},
        batch_size=100,
        timeout_seconds=30,
        owner="test",
        status="active",
        created_by="test",
    )
    script = ensure_composer_fixture_version(db, "downstream_projection")
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key=(
            f"runtime.outbox.integration.{run_mode}.{'-'.join(node_ids)}"
        ),
        name="Runtime outbox integration",
        description="",
        owner="test",
        allowed_categories_json='["space_image"]',
        status="active",
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    nodes = [
        {
            "node_instance_id": node_id,
            "kind": "downstream_projection",
            "display_name": f"写入下游 {node_id}",
            "config": {"connector_key": target.contract_key},
            "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
            "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 2},
            "evidence_fields": [],
        }
        for node_id in node_ids
    ]
    edges = [
        {"from": source, "to": target_node}
        for source, target_node in zip(node_ids, node_ids[1:], strict=False)
    ]
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": "space_image",
        "lane_key": "incremental",
        "run_modes": ["incremental", "stock_regression"],
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": edges,
        "queue_class": "production_batch",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {},
        "layout": {},
    }
    version = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version="1",
        status="active",
        workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
        step_manifest_json=canonical_json(nodes),
        edge_manifest_json=canonical_json(edges),
        input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "category_key": "space_image",
                "lane_key": "incremental",
                "run_modes": ["incremental", "stock_regression"],
                "queue_class": "production_batch",
                "layout": {},
            }
        ),
        canonical_hash=_hash(manifest),
        validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(version)
    db.flush()
    run = create_production_run(
        db,
        ProductionRunRequest(
            workflow_version_id=version.id,
            idempotency_key=(
                f"outbox-integration-{run_mode}-{'-'.join(node_ids)}"
            ),
            category_key="space_image",
            queue_class="production_batch",
            runtime_context={"run_mode": run_mode},
            input_manifest={"content_key": "space-image:asset:42", "asset_id": 42},
            owner="test",
            source_type="incremental_material" if run_mode == "incremental" else None,
            source_id=42 if run_mode == "incremental" else None,
        ),
        actor="test",
    )
    db.commit()
    attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(db, "projection-worker", global_limit=1)
    assert attempt_id is not None
    db.commit()
    attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
    assert attempt is not None and attempt.lease_token
    assert attempt.script_version_id == script.id
    return run, attempt


def _projection_result(node_id: str = "downstream") -> dict:
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
        "node_instance_id": node_id,
        "node_kind": "downstream_projection",
        "status": "succeeded",
        "output": {"asset_id": 42, "grade": "A"},
    }


def test_repeated_downstream_nodes_have_distinct_node_scoped_outbox_events(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'outbox-node-scope.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _run, first = _incremental_projection_run(
                db,
                node_ids=("write-a", "write-b"),
            )
            first_token = first.lease_token
            assert first_token
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                first.id,
                first_token,
                _projection_result("write-a"),
            )
            db.commit()

            second_id = claim_next_runtime_step(
                db,
                "projection-worker-2",
                global_limit=1,
            )
            assert second_id is not None
            db.commit()
            second = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, second_id)
            assert second is not None and second.lease_token
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                second.id,
                second.lease_token,
                _projection_result("write-b"),
            )
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                second.id,
                second.lease_token,
                _projection_result("write-b"),
            )
            db.commit()

            events = db.scalars(
                select(RuntimeOutboxEvent).order_by(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id)
            ).all()
            assert [event.node_instance_id for event in events] == [
                "write-a",
                "write-b",
            ]
            assert len({event.event_key for event in events}) == 2
            assert len({event.business_idempotency_key for event in events}) == 2
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_incremental_projection_completion_and_outbox_are_atomic(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.workflow_runtime as runtime

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'outbox-atomic.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt = _incremental_projection_run(db)
            run_id = run.id
            attempt_id = attempt.id
            lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            real_enqueue = enqueue_runtime_outbox

            class InjectedCommitFailure(RuntimeError):
                pass

            def fail_after_enqueue(session, request):
                real_enqueue(session, request)
                raise InjectedCommitFailure("fail before completion commit")

            monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, "enqueue_runtime_outbox", fail_after_enqueue)
            with pytest.raises(InjectedCommitFailure):
                complete_runtime_step(
                    db,
                    attempt_id,
                    lease_token,
                    _projection_result(),
                )
            db.rollback()

        with Session(engine) as verify_db:
            persisted_attempt = verify_db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
            persisted_run = verify_db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert persisted_attempt is not None
            assert persisted_attempt.status in {"leased", "running"}
            assert persisted_run is not None and persisted_run.status == "running"
            assert verify_db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            ) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_a_new_run_copies_the_upstream_task_identity_into_columns() -> None:
    from app.workflow_runtime import _upstream_identity_columns

    task = {"task_id": 2588, "res_type": 6, "ll_id": 34015031, "img_id": 46272219, "class_id_1": 2124}
    assert _upstream_identity_columns({"factory_task": task}) == {
        "upstream_class_id": 2124, "upstream_task_id": 2588, "upstream_img_id": 46272219, "upstream_ll_id": 34015031}
    # 没有工厂任务（存量回归、其他上游）或字段不是正整数：列留空，不猜。
    assert _upstream_identity_columns({}) == {
        "upstream_class_id": None, "upstream_task_id": None, "upstream_img_id": None, "upstream_ll_id": None}
    assert _upstream_identity_columns({"factory_task": {"class_id_1": "2124", "task_id": True}})["upstream_class_id"] is None
    assert _upstream_identity_columns({"factory_task": {"class_id_1": "2124", "task_id": True}})["upstream_task_id"] is None


def test_a_new_run_records_its_run_mode_in_the_column(tmp_path) -> None:
    # 增量纠偏页靠这一列过滤（迁移 118）；建运行时不写，新行就得回到 14 秒一页的快照解析。
    for run_mode in ("incremental", "stock_regression"):
        engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'outbox-run-mode-column-{run_mode}.db'}")
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        try:
            with Session(engine) as db:
                run, _ = _incremental_projection_run(db, run_mode=run_mode)
                assert run.run_mode == run_mode
        finally:
            engine.dispose()


def test_incremental_projection_enqueues_but_stock_regression_does_not(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'outbox-run-mode.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            incremental_run, incremental_attempt = _incremental_projection_run(db)
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                incremental_attempt.id,
                incremental_attempt.lease_token,
                _projection_result(),
            )
            db.commit()
            event = db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent))
            assert event is not None
            assert event.mechanism_version_id == incremental_run.workflow_version_id
            assert event.target_contract_key == "space-image-output"
            assert "space-image:asset:42" in event.business_idempotency_key

        with Session(engine) as db:
            _stock_run, stock_attempt = _incremental_projection_run(
                db,
                run_mode="stock_regression",
            )
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                stock_attempt.id,
                stock_attempt.lease_token,
                _projection_result(),
            )
            db.commit()
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            ) == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_postgres_workers_claim_each_outbox_event_once(pg_engine) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        version = _mechanism_version(db, suffix="postgres")
        for index in range(50):
            enqueue_runtime_outbox(
                db,
                RuntimeOutboxRequest(
                    event_key=f"event:{index}",
                    target_contract_key="logical.space_image.output",
                    business_idempotency_key=f"asset:{index}:mechanism:{version.id}",
                    mechanism_version_id=version.id,
                    payload={"asset_id": index},
                ),
            )
        db.commit()

    claimed_ids: list[int] = []
    claimed_lock = Lock()

    def claim_all(worker_number: int) -> None:
            while True:
                with Session(pg_engine) as db:
                    claimed = claim_outbox_event(db, f"outbox-worker-{worker_number}")
                    claimed_id = claimed.id if claimed is not None else None
                    db.commit()
                if claimed is None:
                    return
                with claimed_lock:
                    claimed_ids.append(claimed_id)

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
        list(pool.map(claim_all, range(8)))

    assert len(claimed_ids) == 50
    assert len(set(claimed_ids)) == 50


def test_postgres_concurrent_enqueue_reuses_one_business_event(pg_engine) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        version = _mechanism_version(db, suffix="concurrent-enqueue")
        version_id = version.id
        db.commit()

    event_ids: list[int] = []
    event_lock = Lock()

    def enqueue_once(_worker_number: int) -> None:
        with Session(pg_engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
            version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, version_id)
            assert version is not None
            event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, _request(version))
            db.commit()
            event_id = event.id
        with event_lock:
            event_ids.append(event_id)

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
        list(pool.map(enqueue_once, range(8)))

    assert len(event_ids) == 8
    assert len(set(event_ids)) == 1
    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
        ) == 1


def test_mysql_workers_claim_each_outbox_event_once(mysql_engine) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        version = _mechanism_version(db, suffix="mysql")
        for index in range(50):
            enqueue_runtime_outbox(
                db,
                RuntimeOutboxRequest(
                    event_key=f"mysql-event:{index}",
                    target_contract_key="logical.space_image.output",
                    business_idempotency_key=(
                        f"mysql-asset:{index}:mechanism:{version.id}"
                    ),
                    mechanism_version_id=version.id,
                    payload={"asset_id": index},
                ),
            )
        db.commit()

    claimed_ids: list[int] = []
    claimed_lock = Lock()

    def claim_all(worker_number: int) -> None:
        while True:
            with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
                claimed = claim_outbox_event(
                    db,
                    f"mysql-outbox-worker-{worker_number}",
                )
                claimed_id = claimed.id if claimed is not None else None
                db.commit()
            if claimed is None:
                return
            with claimed_lock:
                claimed_ids.append(claimed_id)

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
        list(pool.map(claim_all, range(8)))

    assert len(claimed_ids) == 50
    assert len(set(claimed_ids)) == 50

def test_mysql_outbox_claim_does_not_lock_the_other_pending_events(mysql_engine) -> None:
    """A small pending tail must fill independent slots before any commit.

    The production status-index + filesort plan locked the whole pending tail
    before LIMIT 1, so the other delivery transactions found no claimable row.
    """
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    from sqlalchemy import event, insert, text
    from app.database import configure_mysql_dbapi_connection
    from app.runtime_outbox import eligible_outbox_events

    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        version = _mechanism_version(db, suffix="mysql-pending-tail")
        seed = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, _request(version))
        db.flush()
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        db.execute(insert(RuntimeOutboxEvent), [dict(
            event_key=f"pending-tail:{i}",
            target_contract_key=seed.target_contract_key,
            business_idempotency_key=f"pending-tail:{i}",
            mechanism_version_id=version.id, payload_blob_id=seed.payload_blob_id,
            payload_hash=seed.payload_hash, status="queued" if i >= 5000 else "delivered",
            available_at=now, attempt_count=0, max_attempts=3,
        ) for i in range(5040)])
        seed.status = "delivered"
        db.commit()
    with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
        connection.execute(text("ANALYZE TABLE runtime_outbox_events"))

    probe_engine = create_engine(mysql_engine.url, pool_size=20, max_overflow=0)
    def configure_probe(connection, _record):
        configure_mysql_dbapi_connection(connection)
        # Reproduce the production status-index/filesort plan deterministically
        # without relying on table-size heuristics of this small fixture.
        with connection.cursor() as cursor:
            cursor.execute("SET SESSION optimizer_switch='prefer_ordering_index=off'")
    event.listen(probe_engine, "connect", configure_probe)
    sessions = []
    claimed = []
    try:
        for slot in range(20):
            db = Session(probe_engine)
            sessions.append(db)
            candidates = eligible_outbox_events(db, limit=1,
                target_contract_key="logical.space_image.output")
            assert len(candidates) == 1, f"slot {slot} lost all other pending rows"
            event = claim_outbox_event(db, f"pending-tail-worker:{slot}",
                only_event_ids={candidates[0].id})
            assert event is not None
            claimed.append(event.id)
        assert len(set(claimed)) == 20
    finally:
        for db in reversed(sessions):
            db.rollback()
            db.close()
        probe_engine.dispose()

    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
                         .where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.status == "queued")) == 40
        assert db.scalar(select(func.sum(RuntimeOutboxEvent.attempt_count))) == 0


def test_mysql_outbox_identity_drift_fails_closed(mysql_engine) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        version = _mechanism_version(db, suffix="mysql-drift")
        enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, _request(version))
        db.commit()

        with pytest.raises(
            RuntimeOutboxError,
            match="outbox_idempotency_conflict",
        ):
            enqueue_runtime_outbox(
                db,
                _request(version, payload={"asset_id": 42, "grade": "B"}),
            )


def test_mysql_stale_outbox_lease_cannot_complete(mysql_engine) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        version = _mechanism_version(db, suffix="mysql-stale-lease")
        enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, _request(version))
        db.commit()
        claimed = claim_outbox_event(db, "mysql-stale-worker", lease_seconds=1)
        assert claimed is not None and claimed.lease_token
        claimed_id = claimed.id
        lease_token = claimed.lease_token
        expires_at = claimed.lease_expires_at
        receipt = DeliveryReceipt(
            business_idempotency_key=claimed.business_idempotency_key,
            mechanism_version_id=claimed.mechanism_version_id,
            payload_hash=claimed.payload_hash,
            external_record_key="mysql-shadow-row-42",
        )
        db.commit()
        assert expires_at is not None

        with pytest.raises(RuntimeOutboxError, match="outbox_lease_stale"):
            complete_outbox_event(
                db,
                claimed_id,
                lease_token,
                receipt,
                now=expires_at,
            )


def test_incremental_projection_writes_publish_ledger_with_change_record(tmp_path) -> None:
    """ADR-0064：每次发布在平台侧追加一行变更日志（触发方、上一版指纹、字段级变更）。"""

    from app.downstream_publish_ledger import diff_result_paths
    from app.models import DownstreamPublishLedger

    assert diff_result_paths({"a": 1, "b": {"c": 2}}, {"a": 1, "b": {"c": 3}, "d": 4}) == [
        "b.c",
        "d",
    ]
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'outbox-ledger.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt = _incremental_projection_run(db)
            complete_runtime_step(db, attempt.id, attempt.lease_token, _projection_result())
            db.commit()
            rows = db.scalars(select(DownstreamPublishLedger)).all()
            assert len(rows) == 1
            row = rows[0]
            event = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, row.outbox_event_id)
            assert event is not None
            assert (row.revision, row.review_state, row.trigger_kind) == (1, "auto", "auto_run")
            assert row.previous_revision is None
            assert row.business_idempotency_key == event.business_idempotency_key
            assert ":mechanism:" not in row.business_idempotency_key
            assert row.delivery_status == "pending"
            payload = load_snapshot_blob(event.payload_blob)
            assert payload["change"]["trigger_kind"] == "auto_run"
            assert payload["revision"] == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def _load_fault_matrix():
    path = (
        Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
        / "scripts"
        / "runtime-queue-fault-matrix.py"
    )
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("runtime_queue_fault_matrix", path)
    assert spec is not None and spec.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[spec.name] = module
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def _assert_fault_report(report: dict[str, object], dialect: str) -> None:
    assert report["schema_version"] == "runtime-queue-fault-matrix-v2"
    assert report["dialect"] == dialect
    assert isinstance(report["server_version"], str)
    assert report["server_version"]
    assert report["database"]["dialect"] == dialect
    assert report["database"]["server_version"] == report["server_version"]
    assert report["external_model_calls"] == 0
    assert report["external_writes"] == 0
    assert {case["key"] for case in report["cases"]} == {
        "worker_kill",
        "expired_lease",
        "stale_completion",
        "provider_429",
        "schema_error",
        "unknown_external_state",
        "outbox_target_pause",
        "read_back_mismatch",
        "dead_letter_replay",
        "application_restart",
    }
    assert all(case["passed"] for case in report["cases"]), report["cases"]
    assert report["all_passed"] is True


def test_postgres_fault_matrix_fails_closed_and_preserves_recovery(pg_engine) -> None:
    matrix = _load_fault_matrix()
    _assert_fault_report(
        matrix.run_fault_matrix(pg_engine, run_label="pytest-postgres-faults"),
        "postgresql",

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine


def _load_harness():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "runtime-queue-load.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("runtime_queue_load", path)
    assert spec is not None and spec.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[spec.name] = module
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def _assert_zero_loss_report(report: dict[str, object], dialect: str) -> None:
    assert report["schema_version"] == "runtime-queue-load-report-v2"
    assert report["dialect"] == dialect
    assert isinstance(report["server_version"], str)
    assert report["server_version"]
    assert report["asset_count"] == 1_000
    assert report["worker_count"] == 8
    assert report["capacity_scope"] == {
        "proof_kind": "database_queue_only",
        "end_to_end": False,
        "target_100k_per_day_status": "not_proven",
        "reason": "external_model_calls_and_connectors_are_disabled",
    }
    assert report["missing_items"] == 0
    assert report["duplicate_claims"] == 0
    assert report["outbox_counts"] == {
        "queued": 0,
        "leased": 0,
        "delivered": 0,
        "dead_lettered": 0,
        "canceled": 0,
    }
    assert report["workload"]["asset_count"] == 1_000
    assert report["workload"]["model_node_count"] == 1_800
    assert report["workload"]["peak_multiplier"] == 1
    assert report["throughput"]["required_assets_per_second"] == 11.6
    assert report["counts"] == {
        "seeded": 1_000,
        "claimed": 1_000,
        "completed": 1_000,
        "queued": 0,
        "leased": 0,
        "dead_lettered": 0,
        "canceled": 0,
        "duplicate_claims": 0,
        "missing_items": 0,
        "lease_recoveries": 0,
    }
    assert report["latency_ms"]["claim_p50"] >= 0
    assert report["latency_ms"]["claim_p95"] >= report["latency_ms"]["claim_p50"]
    assert report["latency_ms"]["claim_p99"] >= report["latency_ms"]["claim_p95"]
    assert report["throughput"]["claim_assets_per_second"] > 0
    assert report["database"]["dialect"] == dialect
    assert report["database"]["server_version"] == report["server_version"]
    assert report["database"]["bytes"] > 0
    assert report["database"]["growth_bytes"] > 0
    assert report["resources"]["process_cpu_seconds"] > 0
    assert report["resources"]["peak_rss_bytes"] > 0
    assert report["connections"]["pool_size"] == 8
    assert report["connections"]["max_overflow"] == 5
    assert report["connections"]["pool_capacity"] == 13
    assert 1 <= report["connections"]["peak_checked_out"] <= 13
    assert report["connections"]["checked_out_at_end"] == 0
    assert report["transaction_conflicts"] == {
        "deadlocks": 0,
        "retries": 0,
    }
    assert report["snapshot_reuse"] == {
        "blob_rows": 1,
        "runs_with_blob": 1_000,
        "unique_blob_ids": 1,
        "reused_runs": 999,
    }
    assert report["acceptance"]["duplicate_free"] is True
    assert report["acceptance"]["zero_loss"] is True
    assert report["acceptance"]["terminal_counts_reconcile"] is True
    assert report["estimated_drain_seconds"] == 0


def _run_small_load(harness, engine, run_label: str) -> dict[str, object]:
    runtime_engine = harness.create_transactional_runtime_engine(
        engine.url,
        workers=8,
    )
    try:
        return harness.run_load_test(
            runtime_engine,
            asset_count=1_000,
            workers=8,
            claim_batch_size=32,
            workload_manifest={
                "one_model": 0.4,
                "two_model": 0.4,
                "three_model": 0.2,
            },
            run_label=run_label,
        )
    finally:
        runtime_engine.dispose()


def test_small_postgres_load_report_reconciles_every_item(pg_engine) -> None:
    harness = _load_harness()
    _assert_zero_loss_report(
        _run_small_load(harness, pg_engine, "pytest-postgres-small"),
        "postgresql",
    )

        _run_small_load(harness, mysql_engine, "pytest-mysql-small"),

    )


def test_transactional_harness_rejects_sqlite() -> None:
    harness = _load_harness()
    engine = create_engine("sqlite+pysqlite:///:memory:")
    try:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="PostgreSQL|MySQL 8.4"):
            harness.transactional_database_kind(engine)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

    harness = _load_harness()
    parser = harness._parser()
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        parser.parse_args(
            [
                "--maintenance-url",
                "postgresql://localhost/postgres",
                "--mysql-config",
                "/tmp/accounts.json",
            ]
        )

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_queue_mysql.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from datetime import timedelta
from threading import Event, Lock

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
from app.models import (
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeDispatchItem,
    ScriptDefinition,
    ScriptVersion,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.runtime_queue import (
    ClaimRequest,
    RuntimeFailureClassifier,
    RuntimeQueueError,
    claim_runtime_item,
    fail_runtime_item,
    replay_dead_letter,

from app.workflow_runtime import recover_expired_runtime_steps


def _seed_dispatch_items(mysql_engine: Engine, *, count: int) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        script_definition = ScriptDefinition(
            script_key="runtime.queue.mysql.fixture",
            name="MySQL queue concurrency fixture",
            description="",
            owner="test",
            allowed_categories_json="[]",
            step_types_json='["identity"]',
            status="active",
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(script_definition)
        db.flush()
        script_version = ScriptVersion(
            script_definition_id=script_definition.id,
            version="1",
            display_name="MySQL queue concurrency fixture v1",
            executor_kind="deterministic_fixture",
            artifact_sha256="a" * 64,
            manifest_json='{"fixture":"identity"}',
            input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
            output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
            required_permissions_json="[]",
            idempotency_template="{run_key}:{step_key}:{input_hash}",
            timeout_seconds=60,
            max_attempts=3,
            retry_policy_json='{"kind":"fixed","delay_seconds":1}',
            concurrency_limit=count,
            estimated_cost_json="{}",
            status="active",
            validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
            blocked_reason="",
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(script_version)
        workflow_definition = WorkflowDefinition(
            workflow_key="runtime.queue.mysql.workflow",
            name="MySQL queue concurrency workflow",
            description="",
            owner="test",
            allowed_categories_json="[]",
            status="active",
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(workflow_definition)
        db.flush()
        workflow_version = WorkflowVersion(
            workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition.id,
            version="1",
            status="active",
            workflow_schema_version="workflow-v1",
            step_manifest_json="[]",
            edge_manifest_json="[]",
            input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
            output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
            resource_policy_json="{}",
            canonical_hash="b" * 64,
            validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(workflow_version)
        db.flush()

        snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(b"{}").hexdigest()
        for index in range(count):
            run = ProductionRun(
                run_key=f"mysql-queue-run-{index}",
                idempotency_key=f"mysql-queue-idempotency-{index}",
                workflow_definition_id=workflow_definition.id,
                workflow_version_id=workflow_version.id,
                snapshot_json="{}",
                snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                queue_class="production_batch",
                status="queued",
                blockers_json="[]",
                requested_by="test",
                owner="test",
                reason="MySQL concurrent queue claim test",
                environment="dry_run",
                model_execution_mode="simulated",
                total_steps=1,
                completed_steps=0,
                failed_steps=0,
                attempt_count=0,
                error_code="",
                error_message="",
            )
            db.add(run)
            db.flush()
            attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
                run_id=run.id,
                step_key="identity",
                step_type="identity",
                sequence=0,
                script_version_id=script_version.id,
                status="pending",
                attempt_no=1,
                idempotency_key=f"mysql-queue-attempt-{index}",
                input_manifest_json="{}",
                input_hash=snapshot_hash,
                output_manifest_json="{}",
                checkpoint_json="{}",
                last_error_code="",
                last_error_message="",
            )
            db.add(attempt)
            db.flush()
            db.add(
                RuntimeDispatchItem(
                    step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
                    queue_class="production_batch",
                    priority=50,
                    status="queued",
                    max_attempts=3,
                )
            )
        db.commit()


def test_eight_mysql_workers_claim_each_item_once(mysql_engine: Engine) -> None:
    _seed_dispatch_items(mysql_engine, count=100)
    claimed_ids: list[int] = []
    claimed_lock = Lock()

    def claim_until_empty(worker_number: int) -> None:
        while True:
            with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
                claimed = claim_runtime_item(
                    db,
                    ClaimRequest(
                        worker_id=f"mysql-worker-{worker_number}",
                        global_limit=100,
                        lease_seconds=300,
                    ),
                )
                db.commit()
            if claimed is None:
                return
            with claimed_lock:
                claimed_ids.append(claimed.item_id)

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
        list(pool.map(claim_until_empty, range(8)))

    assert len(claimed_ids) == 100
    assert len(set(claimed_ids)) == 100
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(RuntimeDispatchItem)
            .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "leased")
        ) == 100
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(RuntimeDispatchItem)
            .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.attempt_count == 1)
        ) == 100


def test_mysql_claim_preserves_run_first_lock_order(mysql_engine: Engine) -> None:
    _seed_dispatch_items(mysql_engine, count=2)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        attempts = db.scalars(
            select(ProductionStepAttempt).order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.id)
        ).all()
        assert len(attempts) == 2
        run_id = attempts[0].run_id
        attempts[1].run_id = run_id
        attempts[1].step_key = "identity-sibling"
        db.commit()

    run_locked = Event()

    def transition_run_first() -> int:
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            db.connection().exec_driver_sql(
                "SET SESSION innodb_lock_wait_timeout = 5"
            )
            run = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionRun)
                .where(ProductionRun.id == run_id)
                .with_for_update()
            )
            assert run is not None
            run_locked.set()
            time.sleep(0.25)
            dispatches = db.scalars(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem)
                .join(
                    ProductionStepAttempt,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.id
                    == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id,
                )
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
                .with_for_update()
            ).all()
            db.commit()
            return len(dispatches)

    def claim_sibling() -> int | None:
        assert run_locked.wait(timeout=5)
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            db.connection().exec_driver_sql(
                "SET SESSION innodb_lock_wait_timeout = 5"
            )
            claimed = claim_runtime_item(
                db,
                ClaimRequest(
                    worker_id="mysql-run-first-worker",
                    global_limit=2,
                ),
            )
            db.commit()
            return claimed.item_id if claimed is not None else None

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        transition = pool.submit(transition_run_first)
        claim = pool.submit(claim_sibling)
        assert transition.result(timeout=10) == 2
        assert claim.result(timeout=10) is not None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("new_status", ["paused", "canceled"])
def test_mysql_run_state_change_wins_over_a_concurrent_claim(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    new_status: str,
) -> None:
    _seed_dispatch_items(mysql_engine, count=1)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        run_id = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun.id))
        assert run_id is not None

    run_locked = Event()

    def change_status() -> None:
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            run = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionRun)
                .where(ProductionRun.id == run_id)
                .with_for_update()
            )
            assert run is not None
            run_locked.set()
            time.sleep(0.2)
            run.status = new_status
            db.commit()

    def try_claim() -> int | None:
        assert run_locked.wait(timeout=5)
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            claimed = claim_runtime_item(
                db,
                ClaimRequest(
                    worker_id=f"mysql-{new_status}-worker",
                    global_limit=1,
                ),
            )
            db.commit()
            return claimed.item_id if claimed is not None else None

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        status_change = pool.submit(change_status)
        claim = pool.submit(try_claim)
        status_change.result(timeout=10)
        assert claim.result(timeout=10) is None


def _claim_one(mysql_engine: Engine):
    _seed_dispatch_items(mysql_engine, count=1)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        claimed = claim_runtime_item(
            db,
            ClaimRequest(
                worker_id="mysql-failure-worker",
                global_limit=1,
                lease_seconds=1,
            ),
        )
        db.commit()
        assert claimed is not None
        return claimed


def test_mysql_expired_lease_cannot_fail_the_current_item(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    claimed = _claim_one(mysql_engine)
    failure = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
        TimeoutError("late worker"),
        error_code="provider_timeout",
    )

    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeQueueError, match="runtime_lease_stale"):
            fail_runtime_item(
                db,
                claimed.item_id,
                claimed.lease_token,
                failure,
                now=claimed.lease_expires_at,
            )
        db.rollback()


def test_mysql_max_attempt_dead_letter_can_be_replayed(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    claimed = _claim_one(mysql_engine)
    failure = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
        TimeoutError("provider unavailable"),
        error_code="provider_timeout",
    )
    current = claimed.leased_at + timedelta(milliseconds=100)
    current_claim = claimed

    for attempt_number in range(1, 4):
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            dispatch = fail_runtime_item(
                db,
                current_claim.item_id,
                current_claim.lease_token,
                failure,
                now=current,
            )
            db.commit()
            expected = "dead_lettered" if attempt_number == 3 else "queued"
            assert dispatch.status == expected
            available_at = dispatch.available_at
        if attempt_number < 3:
            with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
                next_claim = claim_runtime_item(
                    db,
                    ClaimRequest(
                        worker_id=f"mysql-retry-{attempt_number}",
                        global_limit=1,
                        lease_seconds=10,
                        now=available_at,
                    ),
                )
                db.commit()
                assert next_claim is not None
                current_claim = next_claim
                current = next_claim.leased_at + timedelta(milliseconds=100)

    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        replayed = replay_dead_letter(
            db,
            claimed.item_id,
            actor="mysql-operator",
            reason="供应商恢复，人工确认重放",
            now=current + timedelta(seconds=1),
        )
        db.commit()
        assert replayed.status == "queued"
        assert replayed.max_attempts == 4


def test_mysql_expired_runtime_lease_is_recovered_once(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    claimed = _claim_one(mysql_engine)
    expired_at = claimed.lease_expires_at + timedelta(seconds=1)

    def recover(_worker_number: int) -> int:
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            recovered = recover_expired_runtime_steps(db, now=expired_at)
            db.commit()
            return recovered

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        recovered_counts = list(pool.map(recover, range(2)))

    assert sorted(recovered_counts) == [0, 1]
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        attempts = db.scalars(
            select(ProductionStepAttempt)
            .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == claimed.run_id)
            .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
        ).all()
        assert [(item.attempt_no, item.status) for item in attempts] == [
            (1, "failed"),
            (2, "retryable"),
        ]
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(RuntimeDispatchItem)
            .where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempts[1].id,
                RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "queued",
                RuntimeDispatchItem.queue_class == "recovery",
            )
        ) == 1
