                queue_class="production_batch",
                status="queued",
                blockers_json="[]",
                requested_by="test",
                owner="test",
                reason="PostgreSQL concurrent queue claim test",
                environment="dry_run",
                model_execution_mode="simulated",
                total_steps=1,
                completed_steps=0,
                failed_steps=0,
                attempt_count=0,
                error_code="",
                error_message="",
            )
            db.add(run)
            db.flush()
            attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
                run_id=run.id,
                step_key="identity",
                step_type="identity",
                sequence=0,
                script_version_id=script_version.id,
                status="pending",
                attempt_no=1,
                idempotency_key=f"queue-concurrency-attempt-{index}",
                input_manifest_json="{}",
                input_hash=snapshot_hash,
                output_manifest_json="{}",
                checkpoint_json="{}",
                last_error_code="",
                last_error_message="",
            )
            db.add(attempt)
            db.flush()
            db.add(
                RuntimeDispatchItem(
                    step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
                    queue_class="production_batch",
                    priority=50,
                    status="queued",
                    max_attempts=3,
                )
            )
        db.commit()


def test_two_postgres_workers_never_claim_the_same_item(pg_engine) -> None:
    _seed_dispatch_items(pg_engine, count=100)
    claimed_ids: list[int] = []
    claimed_lock = Lock()

    def claim_until_empty(worker_number: int) -> None:
        while True:
            with Session(pg_engine) as db:
                claimed = claim_runtime_item(
                    db,
                    ClaimRequest(
                        worker_id=f"worker-{worker_number}",
                        global_limit=100,
                        lease_seconds=300,
                    ),
                )
                db.commit()
            if claimed is None:
                return
            with claimed_lock:
                claimed_ids.append(claimed.item_id)

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
        list(pool.map(claim_until_empty, range(8)))

    assert len(claimed_ids) == 100
    assert len(set(claimed_ids)) == 100
    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(RuntimeDispatchItem)
            .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "leased")
        ) == 100
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(RuntimeDispatchItem)
            .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.lease_token.is_not(None))
        ) == 100
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count())
            .select_from(RuntimeDispatchItem)
            .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.attempt_count == 1)
        ) == 100


def test_claim_does_not_deadlock_with_run_first_runtime_transition(
    pg_engine,
) -> None:
    _seed_dispatch_items(pg_engine, count=2)
    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        attempts = db.scalars(
            select(ProductionStepAttempt).order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.id)
        ).all()
        assert len(attempts) == 2
        run_id = attempts[0].run_id
        attempts[1].run_id = run_id
        attempts[1].step_key = "identity-sibling"
        db.commit()

    run_locked = Event()

    def hold_run_then_lock_dispatches() -> int:
        with Session(pg_engine) as db:
            db.connection().exec_driver_sql("SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s'")
            run = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionRun)
                .where(ProductionRun.id == run_id)
                .with_for_update()
            )
            assert run is not None
            run_locked.set()
            # Give the competing claim enough time to select and lock its row.
            time.sleep(0.25)
            rows = db.scalars(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem)
                .join(
                    ProductionStepAttempt,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.id
                    == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id,
                )
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
                .with_for_update()
            ).all()
            db.commit()
            return len(rows)

    def claim_sibling() -> int | None:
        assert run_locked.wait(timeout=5)
        with Session(pg_engine) as db:
            db.connection().exec_driver_sql("SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s'")
            claimed = claim_runtime_item(
                db,
                ClaimRequest(
                    worker_id="deadlock-regression-worker",
                    global_limit=2,
                ),
            )
            db.commit()
            return claimed.item_id if claimed is not None else None

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        transition = pool.submit(hold_run_then_lock_dispatches)
        claim = pool.submit(claim_sibling)
        assert transition.result(timeout=10) == 2
        assert claim.result(timeout=10) is not None


def _claim_one(pg_engine):
    _seed_dispatch_items(pg_engine, count=1)
    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        claimed = claim_runtime_item(
            db,
            ClaimRequest(
                worker_id="failure-worker",
                global_limit=1,
                lease_seconds=300,
            ),
        )
        db.commit()
        assert claimed is not None
        return claimed


def test_transient_failure_schedules_a_bounded_retry(pg_engine) -> None:
    claimed = _claim_one(pg_engine)
    failed_at = claimed.leased_at + timedelta(seconds=1)
    failure = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
        TimeoutError("provider timed out"),
        error_code="provider_timeout",
    )

    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        dispatch = fail_runtime_item(
            db,
            claimed.item_id,
            claimed.lease_token,
            failure,
            now=failed_at,
        )
        db.commit()
        assert dispatch.status == "queued"
        assert dispatch.available_at > failed_at
        assert dispatch.available_at <= failed_at + timedelta(seconds=60)
        assert dispatch.lease_owner is None
        assert dispatch.lease_token is None
        assert dispatch.last_error_code == "provider_timeout"
        assert db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, claimed.attempt_id).status == (
            "retryable"
        )
        assert db.get(ProductionRun, claimed.run_id).status == "retryable"


def test_contract_failure_immediately_enters_dead_letter(pg_engine) -> None:
    claimed = _claim_one(pg_engine)
    failed_at = claimed.leased_at + timedelta(seconds=1)
    failure = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
        ValueError("output contract mismatch"),
        error_code="output_contract_invalid",
        contract_error=True,
    )

    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        dispatch = fail_runtime_item(
            db,
            claimed.item_id,
            claimed.lease_token,
            failure,
            now=failed_at,
        )
        db.commit()
        assert failure.classification == "contract"
        assert failure.retryable is False
        assert dispatch.status == "dead_lettered"
        assert dispatch.dead_lettered_at == failed_at
        assert db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, claimed.attempt_id).status == "failed"
        assert db.get(ProductionRun, claimed.run_id).status == "failed"


def test_rate_limit_failure_preserves_retry_after(pg_engine) -> None:
    claimed = _claim_one(pg_engine)
    failed_at = claimed.leased_at + timedelta(seconds=1)
    failure = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
        RuntimeError("rate limited"),
        error_code="provider_rate_limited",
        status_code=429,
        retry_after_seconds=11,
    )

    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        dispatch = fail_runtime_item(
            db,
            claimed.item_id,
            claimed.lease_token,
            failure,
            now=failed_at,
        )
        db.commit()
        assert failure.classification == "rate_limited"
        assert failure.retry_after_seconds == 11
        assert dispatch.status == "queued"
        assert dispatch.available_at == failed_at + timedelta(seconds=11)


def test_unknown_external_state_is_never_blindly_retried(pg_engine) -> None:
    claimed = _claim_one(pg_engine)
    failed_at = claimed.leased_at + timedelta(seconds=1)
    failure = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
        TimeoutError("provider outcome is unknown"),
        error_code="model_invocation_outcome_unknown",
        unknown_external_state=True,
    )

    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        dispatch = fail_runtime_item(
            db,
            claimed.item_id,
            claimed.lease_token,
            failure,
            now=failed_at,
        )
        db.commit()
        assert failure.classification == "unknown_external_state"
        assert failure.retryable is False
        assert dispatch.status == "dead_lettered"


def test_stale_failure_token_cannot_change_the_current_lease(pg_engine) -> None:
    claimed = _claim_one(pg_engine)
    failure = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
        TimeoutError("late worker"),
        error_code="provider_timeout",
    )

    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeQueueError, match="runtime_lease_stale"):
            fail_runtime_item(
                db,
                claimed.item_id,
                "stale-token",
                failure,
                now=claimed.leased_at + timedelta(seconds=1),
            )
        db.rollback()
        dispatch = db.get(RuntimeDispatchItem, claimed.item_id)
        assert dispatch.status == "leased"
        assert dispatch.lease_token == claimed.lease_token


def test_dead_letter_replay_is_audited_and_runnable(pg_engine) -> None:
    claimed = _claim_one(pg_engine)
    failed_at = claimed.leased_at + timedelta(seconds=1)
    failure = RuntimeFailureClassifier.classify(
        ValueError("operator-correctable contract"),
        error_code="output_contract_invalid",
        contract_error=True,
    )
    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        fail_runtime_item(
            db,
            claimed.item_id,
            claimed.lease_token,
            failure,
            now=failed_at,
        )
        db.commit()

    replayed_at = failed_at + timedelta(minutes=1)
    with Session(pg_engine) as db:
        replayed = replay_dead_letter(
            db,
            claimed.item_id,
            actor="queue-operator",
            reason="合同配置已修复并由运营确认",
            now=replayed_at,
        )
        db.commit()
        assert replayed.status == "queued"
        assert replayed.available_at == replayed_at
        assert replayed.dead_lettered_at is None
        assert replayed.last_error_code == ""
        assert db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, claimed.attempt_id).status == (
            "retryable"
        )
        assert db.get(ProductionRun, claimed.run_id).status == "retryable"
        audit = db.scalar(
            select(RuntimeAuditEvent).where(
                RuntimeAuditEvent.entity_type == "runtime_dispatch_item",
                RuntimeAuditEvent.entity_key == str(claimed.item_id),
                RuntimeAuditEvent.action == "dead_letter_replayed",
            )
        )
        assert audit is not None
        assert audit.actor == "queue-operator"
        assert "合同配置已修复" in audit.details_json

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_recovery_accounting_isolation.py =====
"""Accounting poison must not starve unrelated expired leases or erase charges."""
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeDispatchItem, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app import workflow_runtime as runtime
from app.workflow_model_budget import WorkflowModelBudgetError
from tests.test_workflow_runtime import _seed_active_workflow


def test_accounting_poison_isolated_without_erasing_unknown_charge(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'accounting-isolation.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    expired_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=1)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
            attempts = []
            for index in range(2):
                run = runtime.create_production_run(
                    db,
                    runtime.ProductionRunRequest(
                        workflow_version_id=version.id,
                        idempotency_key=f"accounting-isolation-{index}",
                        category_key="model_3d_su", queue_class="validation",
                        runtime_context={}, input_manifest={"content_key": f"audit:{index}"},
                        owner="platform",
                    ),
                    actor="audit",
                )
                attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id,
                ).order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.sequence))
                attempt.status = "running"
                attempt.lease_owner = "dead-worker"
                attempt.lease_token = f"expired-{index}"
                attempt.lease_expires_at = expired_at
                dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id,
                ))
                assert dispatch is not None
                dispatch.status = "leased"
                attempts.append(attempt)
            poison, healthy = attempts
            invocation = WorkflowModelInvocation(
                production_run_id=poison.run_id, step_attempt_id=poison.id,
                node_instance_id=poison.step_key, status="reserved",
                provider="doubao", model_id="audit-no-provider",
                reserved_input_tokens=100, reserved_output_tokens=50,
                estimated_cost_micros=20,
                evidence_json='{"schema_version":"workflow-model-invocation-v1"}',
            )
            db.add(invocation)
            db.commit()
            charge_before = (
                invocation.reserved_input_tokens, invocation.reserved_output_tokens,
                invocation.estimated_cost_micros, invocation.actual_cost_micros,
                invocation.input_tokens, invocation.output_tokens, invocation.evidence_json,
            )
            calls = []

            def accounting_fault(*args, **kwargs):
                calls.append(kwargs["invocation_id"])
                raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                    "model_quota_evidence_invalid", "Injected accounting evidence conflict",
                )

            monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, "fail_model_invocation", accounting_fault)
            now = expired_at + timedelta(seconds=1)
            assert runtime.recover_expired_runtime_steps(db, now=now) == 1
            db.commit()
            assert healthy.status == "failed"
            assert healthy.lease_expires_at is None
            assert poison.lease_expires_at is None
            assert poison.lease_owner is None and poison.lease_token is None
            # Quarantine terminalizes the step and run so the release gate can drain.
            assert poison.status == "failed"
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, poison.run_id).status == "failed"
            dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == poison.id,
            ))
            assert dispatch.status == "dead_lettered"
            assert dispatch.last_error_code == "model_quota_evidence_invalid"
            # Failed settlement must retain its unknown reservation, never invent actuals.
            assert invocation.status == "reserved"
            assert (
                invocation.reserved_input_tokens, invocation.reserved_output_tokens,
                invocation.estimated_cost_micros, invocation.actual_cost_micros,
                invocation.input_tokens, invocation.output_tokens, invocation.evidence_json,
            ) == charge_before
            assert runtime.recover_expired_runtime_steps(db, now=now + timedelta(seconds=10)) == 0
            assert calls == [invocation.id]
            poison_attempts = db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == poison.run_id,
                ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == poison.step_key,
            )).all()
            assert len(poison_attempts) == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_route_database_wait.py =====
"""A pre-node ownership lock timeout must not lose a paid run's local step."""
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from pymysql.err import OperationalError as MysqlError
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app import incremental_routing, workflow_fixture_executor as executor
from app.models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeDispatchItem, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.workflow_runtime import ProductionRunRequest, WorkflowRuntimeError, create_production_run
from test_workflow_runtime import _engine, _seed_active_workflow


@pytest.mark.parametrize('ownership_lost', [False, True])
def test_route_lock_wait_preserves_attempt_and_paid_history(tmp_path, monkeypatch, ownership_lost):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False, autoflush=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, 'SessionLocal', sessions)
    class NoProvider:
        def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
            raise AssertionError('A local continuation must not repeat a model call')
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            version = _seed_active_workflow(db, two_steps=True)
            run = create_production_run(db, ProductionRunRequest(
                workflow_version_id=version.id, idempotency_key='route-wait',
                category_key='model_3d_su', queue_class='validation', runtime_context={},
                input_manifest={'content_key':'known-paid-result'}, owner='platform'), actor='test')
            run_id = run.id; frozen = (run.snapshot_json, run.snapshot_hash)
            db.commit()
        assert executor.process_runtime_step_once('test', global_limit=4, model_client_type=NoProvider)
        with sessions() as db:
            first = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id==run_id, ProductionStepAttempt.step_key=='identity'))
            assert first.status=='succeeded'
            paid = WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run_id, step_attempt_id=first.id,
                node_instance_id='prior_paid_a', invocation_key='root', status='succeeded',
                provider='fixture', model_id='fixture', actual_cost_micros=123,
                evidence_json=json.dumps({'result':{'accepted':True}}),
                finished_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc))
            db.add(paid); db.commit()
        with sessions() as db:
            paid_before = {c.name:getattr(db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, paid.id), c.name)
                           for c in WorkflowModelInvocation.__table__.columns}
            local = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id==run_id, ProductionStepAttempt.step_key=='finish'))
            local_id = local.id
        real_validation = incremental_routing.validate_run_route
        def busy(db, run):
            raise OperationalError('controlled ownership fence', {},
                MysqlError(1205, 'Lock wait timeout exceeded; try restarting transaction'))
        monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_routing, 'validate_run_route', busy)
        for _ in range(6):
            assert executor.process_runtime_step_once('test', global_limit=4, model_client_type=NoProvider)
            with sessions() as db:
                local = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, local_id)
                dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id==local_id))
                assert local.status=='retryable' and local.attempt_no==1
                assert local.last_error_code=='runtime_route_database_wait'
                assert local.lease_token is None and dispatch.attempt_count==0
                assert dispatch.status=='queued' and dispatch.available_at>datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)
                assert (db.get(ProductionRun,run_id).snapshot_json,db.get(ProductionRun,run_id).snapshot_hash)==frozen
                assert {c.name:getattr(db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation,paid.id),c.name)
                        for c in WorkflowModelInvocation.__table__.columns}==paid_before
                dispatch.available_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc)-timedelta(seconds=1)
                db.commit()
        def resumed(db, run):
            if ownership_lost:
                raise WorkflowRuntimeError('incremental_ownership_lost', 'ownership transferred', status_code=409)
            return real_validation(db, run)
        monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_routing, 'validate_run_route', resumed)
        assert executor.process_runtime_step_once('test', global_limit=4, model_client_type=NoProvider)
        with sessions() as db:
            run=db.get(ProductionRun,run_id)
            assert run.status==('blocked' if ownership_lost else 'succeeded')
            assert len(db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)).all())==1
            assert db.get(ProductionStepAttempt,local_id).attempt_no==1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_rule_conflict_billing.py =====
"""run417: a known provider response survives a deterministic rule conflict."""
import hashlib
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base
from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
from app.models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowModelInvocation, WorkflowVersion
from app.workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError
from test_workflow_fixture_executor import _convert_to_incremental_run, _fixture_module
from test_workflow_model_executor import _case


@pytest.mark.parametrize('partial_mutation', [False, True])
def test_rule_failure_preserves_paid_response_and_rolls_back_parent(monkeypatch, tmp_path, partial_mutation):
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'rule-settlement.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _, _ = _case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
            node['config']['short_circuit_rules'] = [
                {'rule_key': key, 'when': {'path': 'output.score', 'op': 'eq', 'value': 88},
                 'target_level': level, 'priority': 900, 'skip_roles': []}
                for key, level in [('track', 'L3'), ('hard-defect', 'L4')]
            ]
            version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, run.workflow_version_id)
            version.step_manifest_json = canonical_json([node])
            snapshot = _convert_to_incremental_run(run)
            snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes'] = [node]
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(run.snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest()
            run_id, attempt_id, lease_token = run.id, attempt.id, attempt.lease_token
            inputs = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            db.commit()
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, 'SessionLocal', sessions)
        if partial_mutation:
            def incomplete_parent(db, *_args, **_kwargs):
                db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).completed_steps = 99
                db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id).output_manifest_json = '{"partial":true}'
                db.flush()
                raise WorkflowRuntimeError('rule_after_mutation', '规则失败，不得提交部分状态')
            monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, 'complete_runtime_step', incomplete_parent)

        class Client:
            calls = 0
            def __init__(self, config): self.config = config
            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                Client.calls += 1
                return DoubaoResponse(parsed={'score':88,'label':'已知响应'}, raw_text='{}',
                    raw_payload={'id':'known-rule-conflict'}, upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id='known-rule-conflict', attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options['output_budget'], reasoning_effort=options['reasoning_effort'],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode, input_tokens=90, output_tokens=30, total_tokens=120)

        with pytest.raises(fixture.FixtureExecutionError) as caught:
            fixture._real_prompt_result(worker_id='worker-1', attempt_id=attempt_id,
                lease_token=lease_token, run_snapshot=snapshot, node=node,
                input_manifest=inputs, model_client_type=Client)
        assert caught.value.code == ('rule_after_mutation' if partial_mutation else 'short_circuit_conflict')
        assert caught.value.failure_phase == 'post_settlement'
        assert caught.value.retryable is False
        assert Client.calls == 1
        with sessions() as db:
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            assert invocation.status == 'succeeded'
            assert invocation.upstream_status_code == 200
            assert (invocation.input_tokens, invocation.output_tokens) == (90,30)
            assert invocation.actual_cost_micros > 0
            assert json.loads(invocation.evidence_json)['result']['parsed_output'] == {'score':88,'label':'已知响应'}
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).completed_steps == 0
            assert db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id).output_manifest_json == '{}'
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_runs_paging.py =====
"""Run lists need a page beyond the first, and a picture of what is running.

The operations centre showed the newest 50 runs and nothing else, identified
only by a random run key - so nobody could tell what was actually being
processed, or look past the top of the list.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import ProductionRun
from app.workflow_runtime_api import _frozen_asset_id


def _run(index: int, *, manifest: object = None, snapshot: str | None = None) -> ProductionRun:
    body = snapshot if snapshot is not None else json.dumps(
        {"runtime_context": {"input_manifest": manifest if manifest is not None else {"asset_id": 500 + index}}}
    )
    return ProductionRun(
        run_key=f"run-{index:04d}", idempotency_key=f"idem-{index}", source_type="content_ingress_event",
        source_id=index, workflow_definition_id=1, workflow_version_id=1, snapshot_hash="a" * 64,
        snapshot_json=body, category_key="model_3d_su", queue_class="production_batch",
        status="succeeded", blockers_json="[]", requested_by="test", owner="test", reason="",
        environment="dry_run", total_steps=1, completed_steps=1, failed_steps=0,
    )


def test_the_asset_behind_a_run_is_exposed_so_a_list_can_show_it():
    assert _frozen_asset_id(_run(1)) == 501


@pytest.mark.parametrize("manifest", [
    {},                      # frozen before the field existed
    {"asset_id": None},
    {"asset_id": 0},
    {"asset_id": -3},
    {"asset_id": "10963"},   # a string is not an id
    {"asset_id": True},      # bool is an int subclass; not an id either
    [],                      # manifest is not even an object
])
def test_a_run_without_a_usable_asset_simply_has_no_thumbnail(manifest):
    assert _frozen_asset_id(_run(1, manifest=manifest)) is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("snapshot", ["", "not json", "[]", '{"runtime_context": {}}', "null"])
def test_an_unreadable_snapshot_never_breaks_the_listing(snapshot):
    assert _frozen_asset_id(_run(1, snapshot=snapshot)) is None


def _client(tmp_path, name, runs):
    """A real app around the real router - the paging contract is HTTP-shaped."""
    from fastapi import FastAPI
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

    from app.database import get_db
    from app.workflow_runtime_api import build_workflow_runtime_router

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / name}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        for row in runs:
            db.add(row)
        db.commit()

    def _db():
        with Session(engine) as session:
            yield session

    application = FastAPI()
    application.include_router(build_workflow_runtime_router(require_user=lambda: None))
    application.dependency_overrides[get_db] = _db
    return TestClient(application)


def test_paging_walks_the_whole_list_without_repeating_or_skipping(tmp_path):
    client = _client(tmp_path, "runs.db", [_run(index) for index in range(1, 13)])

    seen: list[int] = []
    offset, pages = 0, 0
    while True:
        body = client.get(f"/api/runtime/runs?limit=5&offset={offset}").json()
        assert body["total"] == 12 and body["limit"] == 5 and body["offset"] == offset
        seen.extend(item["id"] for item in body["items"])
        pages += 1
        if not body["has_more"]:
            break
        offset += len(body["items"])
        assert pages < 10, "paging did not terminate"

    assert pages == 3 and len(seen) == 12 and len(set(seen)) == 12
    # Newest first, and the newest row carries its picture.
    assert seen == sorted(seen, reverse=True)
    assert client.get("/api/runtime/runs?limit=1").json()["items"][0]["asset_id"] == 512


def test_the_last_page_reports_no_more_even_when_it_is_exactly_full(tmp_path):
    client = _client(tmp_path, "exact.db", [_run(index) for index in range(1, 7)])
    second = client.get("/api/runtime/runs?limit=3&offset=3").json()
    assert len(second["items"]) == 3 and second["has_more"] is False
    beyond = client.get("/api/runtime/runs?limit=3&offset=6").json()
    assert beyond["items"] == [] and beyond["has_more"] is False and beyond["total"] == 6


def test_the_count_respects_the_same_filters_as_the_page(tmp_path):
    rows = [_run(index) for index in range(1, 5)]
    failed = _run(99)
    failed.status = "failed"
    client = _client(tmp_path, "filter.db", [*rows, failed])
    assert client.get("/api/runtime/runs?limit=50").json()["total"] == 5
    only_failed = client.get("/api/runtime/runs?status=failed&limit=50").json()
    assert only_failed["total"] == 1 and len(only_failed["items"]) == 1
    assert only_failed["items"][0]["asset_id"] == 599


def test_a_negative_offset_is_refused_rather_than_wrapping_around(tmp_path):
    client = _client(tmp_path, "negative.db", [_run(1)])
    assert client.get("/api/runtime/runs?offset=-1").status_code == 422

===== FILE backend/tests/test_runtime_scheduler_adapter.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import importlib.util
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    QueueSchedulerState,
    RuntimeDispatchItem,
)
from tests.test_workflow_runtime import _seed_active_workflow


def _runtime_module():
    assert importlib.util.find_spec("app.workflow_runtime") is not None
    return importlib.import_module("app.workflow_runtime")


def test_runtime_dispatch_persists_existing_scheduler_state(tmp_path) -> None:
    runtime = _runtime_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'scheduler.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            first_version = _seed_active_workflow(
                db,
                workflow_key="label.runtime.validation",
                queue_class="validation",
            )
            second_version = _seed_active_workflow(
                db,
                workflow_key="label.runtime.interactive",
                queue_class="interactive",
            )
            for version, key, queue in (
                (first_version, "idem-validation", "validation"),
                (second_version, "idem-interactive", "interactive"),
            ):
                runtime.create_production_run(
                    db,
                    runtime.ProductionRunRequest(
                        workflow_version_id=version.id,
                        idempotency_key=key,
                        category_key="model_3d_su",
                        queue_class=queue,
                        runtime_context={
                            "queue_policy_version": "queue-policy-v1"
                        },
                        input_manifest={"content_key": key},
                        owner="platform",
                        reason="scheduler",
                    ),
                    actor="admin",
                )
            db.commit()

            assert runtime.claim_next_runtime_step(
                db, "worker-1", global_limit=2
            ) is not None
            db.commit()
            assert runtime.claim_next_runtime_step(
                db, "worker-2", global_limit=2
            ) is not None
            db.commit()
            state = db.get(QueueSchedulerState, 1)
            assert state is not None
            assert state.dispatch_count == 2
            assert state.policy_version == "queue-policy-v1"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_expired_runtime_lease_moves_to_recovery_attempt(tmp_path) -> None:
    runtime = _runtime_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'recovery.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
            run = runtime.create_production_run(
                db,
                runtime.ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=version.id,
                    idempotency_key="idem-recovery",
                    category_key="model_3d_su",
                    queue_class="validation",
                    runtime_context={"queue_policy_version": "queue-policy-v1"},
                    input_manifest={"content_key": "recovery"},
                    owner="platform",
                    reason="recovery",
                ),
                actor="admin",
            )
            db.commit()
            first_dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem))
            assert first_dispatch is not None
            claim_time = first_dispatch.available_at
            attempt_id = runtime.claim_next_runtime_step(
                db,
                "worker-dead",
                global_limit=1,
                lease_seconds=30,
                now=claim_time,
            )
            db.commit()
            assert attempt_id is not None

            recovered = runtime.recover_expired_runtime_steps(
                db,
                claim_time + timedelta(seconds=60),
            )
            db.commit()
            assert recovered == 1
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
            ).all()
            assert [item.status for item in attempts] == ["failed", "retryable"]
            assert attempts[1].attempt_no == 2
            dispatch = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempts[1].id
                )
            )
            assert dispatch is not None
            assert dispatch.queue_class == "recovery"
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, run.id).status == "retryable"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_sample_sets.py =====
import json

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import _add_to_category_golden_set, app, current_user
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    EvaluationJob,
    EvaluationResult,
    SampleSet,
    SampleSetItem,
    SampleTruthRevision,
    User,
)


def test_system_golden_set_revises_truth_for_repeated_asset_correction() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        asset = Asset(
            original_name="repeat.jpg",
            stored_name="repeat.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=100,
            sha256="f" * 64,
            category_key="space_image",
        )
        db.add(asset)
        db.flush()
        results = []
        for index in (1, 2):
            job = EvaluationJob(
                asset_id=asset.id,
                category_key="space_image",
                status="completed",
                stage="done",
                progress=100,
            )
            db.add(job)
            db.flush()
            result = EvaluationResult(
                asset_id=asset.id,
                job_id=job.id,
                precheck_json='{"classification":{"primary_category":"住宅设计"}}',
                aesthetic_json=None,
                scoring_json="{}",
                raw_response_a="{}",
                score=50 + index,
                level="L3",
                confidence=0.9,
                needs_review=False,
                model_id="repeat-model",
                prompt_a_version="repeat-a",
                prompt_b_version="repeat-b",
                rubric_version="repeat-rubric",
                engine_version="repeat-engine",
            )
            db.add(result)
            db.flush()
            results.append(result)

        first_truth = {"corrected_level": "L3", "marker": "first"}
        second_truth = {"corrected_level": "L2", "marker": "second"}
        _add_to_category_golden_set(
            db, evaluation=results[0], truth=first_truth, actor="reviewer-a"
        )
        db.flush()
        _add_to_category_golden_set(
            db, evaluation=results[0], truth=first_truth, actor="reviewer-a"
        )
        db.flush()
        _add_to_category_golden_set(
            db, evaluation=results[1], truth=second_truth, actor="reviewer-b"
        )
        db.flush()

        sample_set = db.scalar(
            select(SampleSet).where(SampleSet.name == "系统黄金集·space_image")
        )
        item = db.scalar(
            select(SampleSetItem).where(SampleSetItem.sample_set_id == sample_set.id)
        )
        assert item.source_result_id == results[1].id
        assert item.truth_revision == 2
        assert json.loads(item.truth_json)["marker"] == "second"
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count()).select_from(SampleSetItem).where(
                SampleSetItem.sample_set_id == sample_set.id
            )
        ) == 1
        revisions = db.scalars(
            select(SampleTruthRevision)
            .where(SampleTruthRevision.sample_item_id == item.id)
            .order_by(SampleTruthRevision.revision)
        ).all()
        assert [revision.revision for revision in revisions] == [1, 2]
        assert [json.loads(revision.truth_json)["marker"] for revision in revisions] == [
            "first",
            "second",
        ]
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_sample_set_captures_human_final_level() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin", username="tester", password_hash="unused", display_name="测试员")
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="sample.jpg",
        stored_name="sample.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=100,
        width=1200,
        height=800,
        sha256="a" * 64,
        status="evaluated",
    )
    db.add_all([user, asset])
    db.flush()
    job = EvaluationJob(asset_id=asset.id, status="completed", stage="done", progress=100)
    db.add(job)
    db.flush()
    result = EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        job_id=job.id,
        precheck_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "classification": {
                    "primary_category": "住宅设计",
                    "scope_status": "in_scope",
                    "primary_confidence": 0.95,
                },
                "image_quality": {"quality_severity": "good", "confidence": 0.95},
                "media_form": {},
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
        aesthetic_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "dimensions": {
                    key: {"grade": 5 if key == "color_material" else 4}
                    for key in (
                        "composition_viewpoint",
                        "lighting_atmosphere",
                        "color_material",
                        "spatial_design_furnishing",
                        "visual_hierarchy",
                        "detail_completion",
                        "inspiration_reference",
                        "presentation_integrity",
                    )
                },
                "assessment_confidence": 0.9,
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
        scoring_json="{}",
        raw_response_a="{}",
        raw_response_b=None,
        score=65,
        level="L3",
        confidence=0.9,
        needs_review=False,
        model_id="doubao-1.8",
        prompt_a_version="A1",
        prompt_b_version=None,
        rubric_version="R1",
        engine_version="E1",
    )
    db.add(result)
    db.commit()

    def test_db():
        yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = test_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    client = TestClient(app)
    try:
        opened = client.post(
            f"/api/evaluations/{result.id}/review-panel/open",
            json={},
        )
        assert opened.status_code == 200
        reviewed = client.post(
            f"/api/evaluations/{result.id}/review-panel/votes",
            json={
                "reviewer_name": "审核员",
                "decision": "corrected",
                "expected_panel_revision": opened.json()["revision"],
                "note": "色彩与材质评分偏高",
                "corrections": [
                    {
                        "target_type": "dimension",
                        "field_key": "color_material",
                        "model_value": 5,
                        "human_value": 3,
                        "reason_codes": ["photography_as_design"],
                        "note": "统一色调主要来自摄影调色",
                    }
                ],
            },
        )
        assert reviewed.status_code == 200
        asset_detail = client.get(f"/api/assets/{asset.id}").json()
        assert "evaluation" not in asset_detail
        assert "status" not in asset_detail
        evaluation_detail = client.get(f"/api/evaluations/{result.id}").json()
        assert evaluation_detail["evaluation"]["updated_at"]
        assert evaluation_detail["evaluation"]["human_review"]["corrected_score"] is not None
        correction = evaluation_detail["evaluation"]["human_review"]["corrections"][0]
        assert correction["field_key"] == "color_material"
        assert correction["human_value"] == 3

        created = client.post(
            "/api/sample-sets",
            json={"name": "黄金样本", "description": "迁移回归"},
        )
        assert created.status_code == 200
        sample_set_id = created.json()["id"]

        added = client.post(
            f"/api/sample-sets/{sample_set_id}/items",
            json={"asset_ids": [asset.id]},
        )
        assert added.status_code == 200
        assert added.json()["added"] == 1

        detail = client.get(f"/api/sample-sets/{sample_set_id}").json()
        assert detail["summary"]["item_count"] == 1
        assert detail["items"][0]["expected_level"] == "L4"
        assert detail["items"][0]["expected_category"] == "住宅设计"

        overridden = client.post(
            "/api/sample-sets",
            json={"name": "L2 专项样本", "description": "批量等级覆盖"},
        )
        overridden_id = overridden.json()["id"]
        client.post(
            f"/api/sample-sets/{overridden_id}/items",
            json={"asset_ids": [asset.id], "expected_level": "L2"},
        )
        overridden_detail = client.get(f"/api/sample-sets/{overridden_id}").json()
        assert overridden_detail["items"][0]["expected_level"] == "L2"

        second_job = EvaluationJob(asset_id=asset.id, status="completed", stage="done", progress=100)
        db.add(second_job)
        db.flush()
        second_result = EvaluationResult(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            job_id=second_job.id,
            precheck_json=json.dumps(
                {"classification": {"primary_category": "商业空间"}}, ensure_ascii=False
            ),
            aesthetic_json=None,
            scoring_json="{}",
            raw_response_a="{}",
            raw_response_b=None,
            score=52,
            level="L2",
            confidence=0.8,
            needs_review=True,
            model_id="doubao-2.0",
            prompt_a_version="A2",
            prompt_b_version="B2",
            rubric_version="R1",
            engine_version="E1",
        )
        db.add(second_result)
        db.commit()

        evaluation_rows = client.get("/api/evaluations?limit=100").json()["items"]
        matching = [item for item in evaluation_rows if item["id"] == asset.id]
        assert len(matching) == 2
        assert {item["evaluation"]["versions"]["model"] for item in matching} == {
            "doubao-1.8",
            "doubao-2.0",
        }
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_create_sample_set_copies_locked_golden_set_into_editable_draft() -> None:
    """已锁定黄金集的 409 建议"复制形成新草稿版本后再调整"，这条路径必须真实存在。

    在此之前 SampleSetCreateRequest 只有 name/description/kind/category_key，
    没有任何从现有集复制的能力，运营撞上 409 后只能直连 SQL 绕过守卫。
    """
    from datetime import datetime, timezone

    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin", username="tester", password_hash="unused", display_name="测试员")
        asset = Asset(
            original_name="好-inspiration.jpg",
            stored_name="stored-good.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=100,
            width=1200,
            height=800,
            sha256="b" * 64,
            status="evaluated",
        )
        db.add_all([user, asset])
        db.flush()
        job = EvaluationJob(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            category_key="inspiration_image",
            status="completed",
            stage="done",
            progress=100,
        )
        db.add(job)
        db.flush()
        result = EvaluationResult(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            job_id=job.id,
            precheck_json="{}",
            aesthetic_json=None,
            scoring_json="{}",
            raw_response_a="{}",
            score=75,
            level="L2",
            confidence=0.9,
            needs_review=False,
            model_id="copy-model",
            prompt_a_version="copy-a",
            prompt_b_version="copy-b",
            rubric_version="copy-rubric",
            engine_version="copy-engine",
        )
        db.add(result)
        db.flush()
        locked = SampleSet(
            name="灵感图黄金集 v1",
            description="已锁定",
            kind="golden",
            category_key="inspiration_image",
            status="locked",
            created_by="tester",
        )
        db.add(locked)
        db.flush()
        db.add(
            SampleSetItem(
                sample_set_id=locked.id,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                source_result_id=result.id,
                expected_level="L2",
                expected_category="inspiration_image",
                truth_json=json.dumps({"level": "L2", "source": "human_correction"}),
                truth_revision=3,
                truth_updated_by="运营小张",
                truth_updated_at=datetime(2026, 8, 20, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
                note="人工纠偏过",
                added_by="tester",
            )
        )
        db.commit()
        locked_id = locked.id

    def _override_db():
        with Session(engine) as session:
            yield session

    holder = Session(engine)
    acting_user = holder.scalar(select(User).where(User.username == "tester"))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = _override_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: acting_user
    try:
        client = TestClient(app)
        # 锁定集自身不可改（守卫仍在），且提示里给出可用的复制路径
        blocked = client.patch(
            f"/api/sample-sets/{locked_id}/status", json={"status": "draft"}
        )
        assert blocked.status_code == 409
        assert "source_sample_set_id" in blocked.json()["detail"]

        created = client.post(
            "/api/sample-sets",
            json={"name": "灵感图黄金集 v2 草稿", "source_sample_set_id": locked_id},
        )
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        body = created.json()
        assert body["copied_items"] == 1
        assert body["source_sample_set_id"] == locked_id
        new_id = body["id"]
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()

    with Session(engine) as db:
        copy = db.get(SampleSet, new_id)
        assert copy is not None
        # 复制体必须是可编辑的 draft，否则又撞 409，等于没给出路
        assert copy.status == "draft"
        # kind 与 category_key 跟随源集，否则复制出来的集跑不了同一套回归
        assert copy.kind == "golden"
        assert copy.category_key == "inspiration_image"
        items = db.scalars(
            select(SampleSetItem).where(SampleSetItem.sample_set_id == new_id)
        ).all()
        assert len(items) == 1
        item = items[0]
        assert item.expected_level == "L2"
        # 人工纠偏真值连同修订号与署名一起带过去 —— 复制黄金集的意义就在这份结论
        assert json.loads(item.truth_json or "{}")["source"] == "human_correction"
        assert item.truth_revision == 3
        assert item.truth_updated_by == "运营小张"
    engine.dispose()


def test_create_sample_set_rejects_unknown_source() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(User(is_admin=True, role="admin", username="tester", password_hash="unused", display_name="测试员"))
        db.commit()

    def _override_db():
        with Session(engine) as session:
            yield session

    holder = Session(engine)
    acting_user = holder.scalar(select(User).where(User.username == "tester"))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = _override_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: acting_user
    try:
        client = TestClient(app)
        resp = client.post(
            "/api/sample-sets", json={"name": "复制不存在的集", "source_sample_set_id": 9999}
        )
        assert resp.status_code == 404
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        holder.close()
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_schema_adapter.py =====
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base
from app.models import Asset, EvaluationJob, EvaluationResult
from app.schema_adapter import (
    adapt_combined_aesthetic_response,
    is_combined_aesthetic_response,
    normalize_aesthetic_dimensions_for_schema,
    normalize_precheck_business_rules,
    normalize_production_fields,
    repair_combined_aesthetic_results,
    rescore_stored_results,
    validate_production_correction,
)
from app.dimension_schema_registry import space_schema_definition_for_scoring_profile
from app.scoring import ENGINE_VERSION, calculate_score


def combined_payload() -> dict:
    dimensions = {
        "composition_viewpoint": {"grade": 3, "evidence": ["构图证据"]},
        "lighting_atmosphere": {"grade": 3},
        "color_material": {"grade": 3},
        "spatial_design_coherence": {"grade": 5},
        "visual_hierarchy": {"grade": 3},
        "detail_finish": {"grade": 3},
        "contemporary_relevance": {"grade": 3},
        "presentation_integrity": {"grade": 3},
    }
    return {
        "prompt_version": "space_aesthetic_v1.3-draft.2",
        "scope": {"is_in_scope": True, "profile_route": "space"},
        "classification": {
            "primary_category": "住宅设计",
            "category_confidence": 0.95,
        },
        "media_analysis": {
            "media_type": "real_photo",
            "ai_status": "no",
            "shooting_style": "professional",
        },
        "special_flags": {
            "is_collage": False,
            "is_multi_view_layout": False,
            "is_unfinished_site": False,
            "is_pure_white_product": False,
        },
        "quality_analysis": {
            "asset_file_damage": "none",
            "quality_issue_codes": [],
            "observable_evidence": ["画面清晰"],
        },
        "dimensions": dimensions,
        "decision_rules": {
            "hard_gate_triggered": False,
            "level_cap": "none",
            "manual_review_required": False,
        },
        "overall_confidence": 0.9,
    }


def test_combined_response_is_adapted_and_scored_with_declared_weights() -> None:
    payload = combined_payload()
    assert is_combined_aesthetic_response(payload) is True

    precheck, aesthetic = adapt_combined_aesthetic_response(payload)
    assert precheck["classification"]["scope_status"] == "in_scope"
    assert precheck["classification"]["primary_confidence"] == 0.95
    assert aesthetic["dimensions"]["spatial_design_furnishing"]["grade"] == 5
    assert aesthetic["dimensions"]["detail_completion"]["grade"] == 3
    assert aesthetic["dimensions"]["inspiration_reference"]["grade"] == 3

    scoring = calculate_score(precheck, aesthetic)
    assert scoring["formal"] is True
    assert scoring["score"] == 69.8
    assert scoring["level"] == "L3"


def test_combined_response_applies_declared_level_cap() -> None:
    payload = combined_payload()
    payload["dimensions"] = {
        key: {**value, "grade": 5} for key, value in payload["dimensions"].items()
    }
    payload["decision_rules"]["level_cap"] = "L3"
    payload["decision_rules"]["level_cap_reasons"] = ["现场记录图最高 L3"]
    precheck, aesthetic = adapt_combined_aesthetic_response(payload)

    scoring = calculate_score(precheck, aesthetic)
    assert scoring["raw_level"] == "L5"
    assert scoring["level"] == "L3"
    assert scoring["caps"][-1]["reason"] == "现场记录图最高 L3"


def test_startup_rescore_never_rewrites_native_workflow_snapshots() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    payload = combined_payload()
    precheck, aesthetic = adapt_combined_aesthetic_response(payload)

    def add_result(
        index: int,
        *,
        native: bool,
        level: str,
        needs_review: bool,
    ) -> EvaluationResult:
        asset = Asset(
            original_name=f"startup-rescore-{index}.jpg",
            stored_name=f"startup-rescore-{index}.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=10,
            sha256=f"{index:064x}",
        )
        db.add(asset)
        db.flush()
        job = EvaluationJob(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            status="completed",
            stage="done",
            progress=100,
            workflow_version_id=99 if native else None,
            production_run_id=100 + index if native else None,
        )
        db.add(job)
        db.flush()
        result = EvaluationResult(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            job_id=job.id,
            workflow_version_id=job.workflow_version_id,
            production_run_id=job.production_run_id,
            workflow_snapshot_hash="a" * 64 if native else None,
            precheck_json=json.dumps(precheck),
            aesthetic_json=json.dumps(aesthetic),
            scoring_json="{}",
            raw_response_a="{}",
            score=99,
            level=level,
            confidence=0.99,
            needs_review=needs_review,
            model_id="startup-rescore-test",
            prompt_a_version="A1",
            rubric_version="R1",
            engine_version="native-snapshot-v1" if native else "legacy-v0",
        )
        db.add(result)
        db.flush()
        return result

    native = add_result(1, native=True, level="L5", needs_review=True)
    legacy = add_result(2, native=False, level="L1", needs_review=True)
    native_repair = add_result(3, native=True, level="L4", needs_review=True)
    native_repair.precheck_json = json.dumps(payload)
    native_repair.aesthetic_json = None
    db.commit()

    assert repair_combined_aesthetic_results(db) == 0
    assert rescore_stored_results(db) == 1

    db.refresh(native)
    db.refresh(legacy)
    db.refresh(native_repair)
    assert native.engine_version == "native-snapshot-v1"
    assert native.level == "L5"
    assert native.needs_review is True
    assert native_repair.engine_version == "native-snapshot-v1"
    assert native_repair.aesthetic_json is None
    assert native_repair.level == "L4"
    assert native_repair.needs_review is True
    assert legacy.engine_version == ENGINE_VERSION
    assert legacy.level != "L1"

    db.close()
    engine.dispose()


def test_aesthetic_aliases_are_canonicalized_without_dropping_unknown_keys() -> None:
    original = {
        "dimensions": {
            "spatial_design_coherence": {"grade": 3},
            "detail_finish": {"grade": 4},
            "contemporary_relevance": {"grade": 5},
            "future_dimension": {"grade": 2},
        }
    }
    schema = space_schema_definition_for_scoring_profile("space_aesthetic_v1.3")

    normalized = normalize_aesthetic_dimensions_for_schema(original, schema)

    assert normalized is not original
    assert normalized["dimensions"]["spatial_design_furnishing"]["grade"] == 3
    assert normalized["dimensions"]["detail_completion"]["grade"] == 4
    assert normalized["dimensions"]["inspiration_reference"]["grade"] == 5
    assert "spatial_design_coherence" not in normalized["dimensions"]
    assert normalized["dimensions"]["future_dimension"] == {"grade": 2}
    assert "spatial_design_coherence" in original["dimensions"]


def test_partial_space_is_not_normal_or_professional_in_business_rules() -> None:
    precheck = {
        "scene_scope": {"type": "partial_space"},
        "media_form": {
            "real_photo": {"status": "yes"},
            "rendering": {"status": "no"},
            "ai_generated": {"status": "no"},
            "professional_photography": {"status": "yes"},
            "documentary_record": {"status": "no"},
            "casual_snapshot": {"status": "no"},
        },
        "image_quality": {
            "quality_severity": "normal",
            "capture_quality": "good",
            "issues": [],
            "evidence": [],
        },
        "display_flags": {"watermark": False, "decorative_border": False},
    }
    normalized = normalize_precheck_business_rules(precheck)
    assert normalized["image_quality"]["quality_severity"] == "slight"
    assert normalized["image_quality"]["capture_quality"] == "acceptable"
    assert normalized["media_form"]["professional_photography"]["status"] == "no"
    assert normalized["media_form"]["documentary_record"]["status"] == "yes"

    payload = {
        "production_fields": {
            "\t" + "itle": "现代客厅",
            "seotitle": "现代简约客厅空间设计效果图",
            "category": "住宅空间，客厅",
            "style": "现代简约",
            "\t" + "ags": ["客厅", "现代", "简约", "木饰面"],
            "cons": "可见缺点",
            "design": "可见设计",
            "score": 80,
            "reason": [],
            "image_defects": "",
            "trait": "3D数字效果图",
        },
        "image_quality": {"quality_severity": "normal"},
        "media_form": {
            "rendering": {"status": "yes", "confidence": 0.9, "evidence": ["数字渲染"]}
        },
    }
    normalized = normalize_production_fields(payload, required=True)
    assert normalized["production_fields"]["title"] == "现代客厅"
    assert normalized["production_fields"]["tags"] == ["客厅", "现代", "简约", "木饰面"]
