    contract = deepcopy(valid_contract())
    contract["execution_variants"][0]["category_key"] = "unknown_category"
    with pytest.raises(SemanticTagContractError, match="unknown_category.*适用性矩阵"):
        validate_tag_demand_contract(contract)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_semantic_tag_fact_governance.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.label_governance import (
    approve_semantic_facts,
    build_label_snapshot,
    create_release,
)
from app.models import AssetVersion, ReviewPanel, SemanticTagFact, TagDemandContract
from app.semantic_tag_contracts import canonical_contract_hash, validate_tag_demand_contract

from tests.test_unified_label_platform import _database
from tests.test_tag_demand_contract_api import _definition


def _definition_for_test_categories() -> dict[str, object]:
    payload = _definition()
    payload["category_applicability"]["space_image"] = dict(
        payload["category_applicability"]["model_3d_su"]
    )
    variant = dict(payload["execution_variants"][0])
    variant["category_key"] = "space_image"
    payload["execution_variants"].append(variant)
    return payload


def _active_contract(db) -> TagDemandContract:
    definition = validate_tag_demand_contract(_definition_for_test_categories())
    contract = TagDemandContract(
        contract_key="semantic-platform",
        version=1,
        status="active",
        definition_json=json.dumps(definition.model_dump(mode="json"), ensure_ascii=False),
        contract_hash=canonical_contract_hash(definition),
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(contract)
    db.flush()
    return contract


def _candidate_fact(db, *, contract: TagDemandContract, asset_version: AssetVersion, evaluation_id: int, field_key: str = "style") -> SemanticTagFact:
    fact = SemanticTagFact(
        asset_version_id=asset_version.id,
        field_key=field_key,
        fact_version=1,
        field_status="needs_review",
        values_json=json.dumps([{
            "value": "现代简约",
            "entity_id": "style.modern",
            "locale": "zh",
            "rank": 1,
            "weight": 1.0,
            "evidence_ref": "evaluation:1#semantic.style.0",
        }], ensure_ascii=False),
        evidence_json=json.dumps(["evaluation:1#semantic.style.0"], ensure_ascii=False),
        source_evaluation_id=evaluation_id,
        source_review_id=None,
        contract_id=contract.id,
        normalization_version="semantic-normalization-v1",
        mapping_version="style-map-v1",
        status="candidate",
        payload_hash="a" * 64,
    )
    db.add(fact)
    db.flush()
    return fact


def _attach_semantic_route(result, *, contract: TagDemandContract, asset_version: AssetVersion) -> None:
    precheck = json.loads(result.precheck_json or "{}")
    precheck["semantic_route"] = {
        "contract_id": contract.id,
        "contract_version": contract.version,
        "contract_hash": contract.contract_hash,
        "asset_version_id": asset_version.id,
        "site_scope": "domestic",
        "asset_scope": "whole",
        "locale": "zh",
        "category_key": result.job.category_key,
        "prompt_variant": "whole",
        "prompt_version": "prompt-v1",
        "model_version": "model-v1",
    }
    result.precheck_json = canonical_json(precheck)


def test_only_completed_human_truth_can_approve_semantic_facts() -> None:
    engine, db, _user, result = _database()
    try:
        contract = _active_contract(db)
        asset_version = AssetVersion(
            asset_id=result.asset_id,
            version=1,
            asset_sha256="a" * 64,
            source_version="fixture-v1",
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(asset_version)
        db.flush()
        _attach_semantic_route(result, contract=contract, asset_version=asset_version)
        _candidate_fact(db, contract=contract, asset_version=asset_version, evaluation_id=result.id)
        panel = db.scalar(select(ReviewPanel).where(ReviewPanel.evaluation_id == result.id))
        assert panel is not None
        panel.status = "collecting"
        panel.final_review_id = None
        db.commit()

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="人工真值"):
            approve_semantic_facts(db, evaluation_id=result.id, actor="reviewer")
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_published_label_v2_contains_only_approved_structured_facts() -> None:
    engine, db, _user, result = _database()
    try:
        contract = _active_contract(db)
        asset_version = AssetVersion(
            asset_id=result.asset_id,
            version=1,
            asset_sha256="a" * 64,
            source_version="fixture-v1",
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(asset_version)
        db.flush()
        _attach_semantic_route(result, contract=contract, asset_version=asset_version)
        _candidate_fact(db, contract=contract, asset_version=asset_version, evaluation_id=result.id)
        db.commit()

        approved = approve_semantic_facts(db, evaluation_id=result.id, actor="reviewer")
        assert approved[0].status == "approved"
        content_key, _evaluation_id, _review_id, payload = build_label_snapshot(
            db, evaluation_id=result.id, content_key=None
        )
        assert content_key.startswith("asset:")
        assert payload["schema_version"] == "published-label-v2"
        assert payload["semantic"]["style"]["values"][0]["entity_id"] == "style.modern"
        assert payload["provenance"]["asset_version_id"] == asset_version.id
        serialized = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)
        assert "raw_response" not in serialized
        assert "candidate" not in serialized
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_semantic_approval_is_idempotent_for_same_final_review() -> None:
    engine, db, _user, result = _database()
    try:
        contract = _active_contract(db)
        asset_version = AssetVersion(
            asset_id=result.asset_id,
            version=1,
            asset_sha256="a" * 64,
            source_version="fixture-v1",
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(asset_version)
        db.flush()
        _attach_semantic_route(result, contract=contract, asset_version=asset_version)
        _candidate_fact(
            db,
            contract=contract,
            asset_version=asset_version,
            evaluation_id=result.id,
        )
        db.commit()

        first = approve_semantic_facts(db, evaluation_id=result.id, actor="reviewer")
        db.commit()
        second = approve_semantic_facts(db, evaluation_id=result.id, actor="reviewer")
        db.commit()

        assert [row.id for row in second] == [row.id for row in first]
        approved = db.scalars(
            select(SemanticTagFact).where(SemanticTagFact.status == "approved")
        ).all()
        assert len(approved) == 1
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_semantic_approval_rejects_contract_drift() -> None:
    engine, db, _user, result = _database()
    try:
        old_contract = _active_contract(db)
        asset_version = AssetVersion(
            asset_id=result.asset_id,
            version=1,
            asset_sha256="a" * 64,
            source_version="fixture-v1",
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(asset_version)
        db.flush()
        _attach_semantic_route(
            result,
            contract=old_contract,
            asset_version=asset_version,
        )
        _candidate_fact(
            db,
            contract=old_contract,
            asset_version=asset_version,
            evaluation_id=result.id,
        )
        old_contract.status = "retired"
        definition = validate_tag_demand_contract(_definition_for_test_categories())
        current = TagDemandContract(
            contract_key="semantic-platform",
            version=2,
            status="active",
            definition_json=json.dumps(
                definition.model_dump(mode="json"), ensure_ascii=False
            ),
            contract_hash=canonical_contract_hash(definition),
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(current)
        db.commit()

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="合同.*漂移"):
            approve_semantic_facts(db, evaluation_id=result.id, actor="reviewer")
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_semantic_approval_rejects_not_applicable_values() -> None:
    engine, db, _user, result = _database()
    try:
        definition_payload = _definition_for_test_categories()
        for category_key in ("model_3d_su", "space_image"):
            definition_payload["category_applicability"][category_key]["style"] = (
                "not_applicable"
            )
        definition = validate_tag_demand_contract(definition_payload)
        contract = TagDemandContract(
            contract_key="semantic-platform",
            version=1,
            status="active",
            definition_json=json.dumps(
                definition.model_dump(mode="json"), ensure_ascii=False
            ),
            contract_hash=canonical_contract_hash(definition),
            created_by="test",
        )
        asset_version = AssetVersion(
            asset_id=result.asset_id,
            version=1,
            asset_sha256="a" * 64,
            source_version="fixture-v1",
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add_all([contract, asset_version])
        db.flush()
        _attach_semantic_route(result, contract=contract, asset_version=asset_version)
        _candidate_fact(
            db,
            contract=contract,
            asset_version=asset_version,
            evaluation_id=result.id,
        )
        db.commit()

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="values.*必须为空"):
            approve_semantic_facts(db, evaluation_id=result.id, actor="reviewer")
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_semantic_release_metadata_matches_v2_payload() -> None:
    engine, db, _user, result = _database()
    try:
        contract = _active_contract(db)
        asset_version = AssetVersion(
            asset_id=result.asset_id,
            version=1,
            asset_sha256="a" * 64,
            source_version="fixture-v1",
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(asset_version)
        db.flush()
        _attach_semantic_route(result, contract=contract, asset_version=asset_version)
        _candidate_fact(
            db,
            contract=contract,
            asset_version=asset_version,
            evaluation_id=result.id,
        )
        approve_semantic_facts(db, evaluation_id=result.id, actor="reviewer")
        release, _duplicate = create_release(
            db,
            release_key="semantic-v2-release",
            evaluation_id=result.id,
            content_key=None,
            requested_by="reviewer",
        )

        assert json.loads(release.label_payload_json)["schema_version"] == (
            "published-label-v2"
        )
        assert release.label_schema_version == "published-label-v2"
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_semantic_tag_mapping.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.schema_adapter import attach_semantic_candidates
from app.semantic_tag_mapping import (
    SemanticExecutionRoute,
    candidate_bundle,
    candidate,
    map_standard_entities,
    normalize_semantic_candidates,
    semantic_mapping_fact_payload,
    suggest_unmapped_candidates,
)


def fixture_mapping(field_key: str) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "field_key": field_key,
        "values": {
            "现代简约": {
                "entity_id": "style.modern_minimal",
                "names": {"zh": "现代简约", "en": "Modern Minimal"},
                "aliases": ["modern minimal"],
            },
            "沙发": {
                "entity_id": "object.sofa",
                "names": {"zh": "沙发", "en": "Sofa"},
                "aliases": ["sofa"],
            },
        },
    }


def empty_mapping() -> dict[str, object]:
    return {"field_key": "material", "values": {}}


def material_mapping() -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "field_key": "material",
        "values": {
            "原木色": {
                "entity_id": "material.natural_wood",
                "names": {"zh": "原木色", "en": "Natural wood"},
                "aliases": ["天然木色"],
                "synonyms": ["木本色"],
            },
            "胡桃木色": {
                "entity_id": "material.walnut",
                "names": {"zh": "胡桃木色", "en": "Walnut"},
                "aliases": [],
                "synonyms": [],
            },
        },
    }


def test_mapping_keeps_structured_rank_weight_and_bilingual_names() -> None:
    bundle = candidate_bundle(
        field_key="style",
        values=[candidate("现代简约", rank=1, weight=0.7)],
    )
    result = map_standard_entities(
        bundle=bundle,
        mapping_registry=fixture_mapping("style"),
        normalization_version="semantic-normalization-v1",
        mapping_version="style-map-v1",
    )
    assert result.values[0].entity_id == "style.modern_minimal"
    assert result.values[0].localized_names == {"zh": "现代简约", "en": "Modern Minimal"}
    assert result.values[0].rank == 1
    assert result.values[0].weight == 0.7


def test_mapping_marks_unknown_values_without_discarding_evidence() -> None:
    bundle = candidate_bundle(field_key="material", values=[candidate("未知复合面")])
    result = map_standard_entities(
        bundle=bundle,
        mapping_registry=empty_mapping(),
        normalization_version="semantic-normalization-v1",
        mapping_version="material-map-v1",
    )
    assert list(result.unmapped_values) == ["未知复合面"]
    assert result.field_status == "needs_review"
    assert list(result.evidence_refs) == ["evidence:未知复合面"]


def test_approved_synonym_maps_to_same_canonical_entity_after_nfkc() -> None:
    result = map_standard_entities(
        bundle=candidate_bundle(
            field_key="material",
            values=[candidate("\u3000木本色  ")],
        ),
        mapping_registry=material_mapping(),
        normalization_version="semantic-normalization-v1",
        mapping_version="material-map-v1",
    )

    assert [item.entity_id for item in result.values] == [
        "material.natural_wood"
    ]
    assert result.unmapped_values == ()


def test_near_term_is_review_only_and_never_becomes_formal_value() -> None:
    registry = material_mapping()
    suggestions = suggest_unmapped_candidates("原木本色", registry)
    assert suggestions[0].entity_id == "material.natural_wood"
    assert 0 < suggestions[0].score < 1

    result = map_standard_entities(
        bundle=candidate_bundle(
            field_key="material",
            values=[candidate("原木本色", evidence_ref="evaluation:7#material")],
        ),
        mapping_registry=registry,
        normalization_version="semantic-normalization-v1",
        mapping_version="material-map-v1",
    )
    payload = semantic_mapping_fact_payload(result)

    assert result.field_status == "needs_review"
    assert result.values == ()
    assert payload["values"] == []
    assert payload["evidence"][0]["kind"] == "unmapped_candidate"
    assert payload["evidence"][0]["raw_value"] == "原木本色"
    assert payload["evidence"][0]["suggestions"][0]["entity_id"] == (
        "material.natural_wood"
    )


def test_duplicate_entities_merge_deterministically() -> None:
    bundle = candidate_bundle(
        field_key="object",
        values=[
            candidate("沙发", rank=1, weight=0.6),
            candidate("sofa", locale="en", rank=2, weight=0.4),
        ],
    )
    result = map_standard_entities(
        bundle=bundle,
        mapping_registry=fixture_mapping("object"),
        normalization_version="semantic-normalization-v1",
        mapping_version="object-map-v1",
    )
    assert len(result.values) == 1
    assert result.values[0].weight == pytest.approx(0.6)
    assert result.values[0].rank == 1


def test_duplicate_entities_keep_the_highest_relative_importance_level() -> None:
    bundle = candidate_bundle(
        field_key="object",
        values=[
            candidate("沙发", rank=2, weight=0.5),
            candidate("sofa", locale="en", rank=1, weight=0.7),
        ],
    )
    result = map_standard_entities(
        bundle=bundle,
        mapping_registry=fixture_mapping("object"),
        normalization_version="semantic-normalization-v1",
        mapping_version="object-map-v1",
    )
    assert result.values[0].weight == pytest.approx(0.7)
    assert result.values[0].rank == 1


def test_normalization_rejects_string_when_field_requires_array() -> None:
    route = SemanticExecutionRoute(
        contract_id=1,
        contract_version=1,
        contract_hash="a" * 64,
        site_scope="domestic",
        asset_scope="whole",
        locale="zh",
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        prompt_variant="whole",
        prompt_version="prompt-v1",
        model_version="model-v1",
        fields={"style": "optional"},
        asset_version_id=1,
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="style 必须是数组或对象"):
        normalize_semantic_candidates(
            route=route,
            provider_payload={"semantic": {"style": "现代简约"}},
            evidence_prefix="evaluation:1",
        )


def test_schema_adapter_only_attaches_candidates_without_creating_release() -> None:
    route = SemanticExecutionRoute(
        contract_id=1,
        contract_version=1,
        contract_hash="a" * 64,
        site_scope="domestic",
        asset_scope="whole",
        locale="zh",
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        prompt_variant="whole",
        prompt_version="prompt-v1",
        model_version="model-v1",
        fields={"style": "optional"},
        asset_version_id=1,
    )
    precheck: dict[str, object] = {"classification": {"scope_status": "in_scope"}}
    normalized = attach_semantic_candidates(
        precheck,
        route=route,
        provider_payload={"semantic": {"style": [{"value": "现代简约", "rank": 1, "weight": 0.7, "evidence": ["画面"]}]}},
        evidence_prefix="evaluation:1",
    )
    assert normalized["semantic_candidates"]["style"][0]["value"] == "现代简约"
    assert "label_release" not in normalized
    assert "published_label" not in normalized

===== FILE backend/tests/test_semantic_tag_quality.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    SemanticQualityMetricSnapshot,
    StrategyBundle,
    TagDemandContract,
)
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.semantic_tag_quality import (
    build_run_semantic_quality,
    compute_semantic_quality_metrics,
    persist_semantic_quality_snapshot,
)


def _review_stats() -> dict[str, int]:
    return {
        "corrected": 0,
        "reviewed": 1,
        "required": 1,
        "null_truth": 0,
        "null_correct": 0,
        "bilingual": 0,
        "bilingual_consistent": 0,
    }


def test_field_precision_and_recall_use_entity_ids() -> None:
    report = compute_semantic_quality_metrics(
        truth_by_asset={"asset-v1": {"style": {"style.modern", "style.minimal"}}},
        predicted_by_asset={"asset-v1": {"style": {"style.modern", "style.luxury"}}},
        mapping_stats={"style": {"candidate": 2, "mapped": 2, "unmapped": 0, "conflicted": 0, "evaluated": 1}},
        review_stats={"style": _review_stats()},
        reconciliation_stats={"expected": 1, "matched": 1},
    )
    assert report.fields["style"].precision == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert report.fields["style"].recall == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert report.fields["style"].mapping_coverage == pytest.approx(1.0)


def test_platform_macro_and_micro_metrics_are_both_reported() -> None:
    report = compute_semantic_quality_metrics(
        truth_by_asset={
            "a": {"style": {"style.modern"}, "material": {"material.wood"}},
            "b": {"style": {"style.minimal"}},
        },
        predicted_by_asset={
            "a": {"style": {"style.modern"}, "material": set()},
            "b": {"style": {"style.luxury"}},
        },
        mapping_stats={
            "style": {"candidate": 2, "mapped": 2, "unmapped": 0, "conflicted": 0, "evaluated": 2},
            "material": {"candidate": 1, "mapped": 1, "unmapped": 0, "conflicted": 0, "evaluated": 1},
        },
        review_stats={"style": _review_stats(), "material": _review_stats()},
        reconciliation_stats={"expected": 3, "matched": 2},
    )
    assert 0 <= report.macro_precision <= 1
    assert 0 <= report.micro_recall <= 1
    assert report.reconciliation_rate == pytest.approx(2 / 3)


def test_empty_denominator_serializes_as_none() -> None:
    report = compute_semantic_quality_metrics(
        truth_by_asset={},
        predicted_by_asset={},
        mapping_stats={"material": {"candidate": 0, "mapped": 0, "unmapped": 0, "conflicted": 0, "evaluated": 0}},
        review_stats={"material": _review_stats() | {"reviewed": 0, "required": 0}},
        reconciliation_stats={"expected": 0, "matched": 0},
    )
    assert report.fields["material"].precision is None
    assert report.fields["material"].recall is None
    assert report.fields["material"].mapping_coverage is None
    assert report.reconciliation_rate is None


def test_run_quality_uses_only_frozen_truth_and_result_evidence() -> None:
    run = SimpleNamespace(
        id=17,
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        execution_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "semantic_truth_snapshot": {
                    "schema_version": "semantic-truth-snapshot-v1",
                    "assets": {
                        "11": {
                            "sample_set_id": 3,
                            "sample_item_id": 5,
                            "truth_revision": 2,
                            "truth": {
                                "semantic": {
                                    "style": {
                                        "status": "required",
                                        "values": [{"entity_id": "style.modern"}],
                                    }
                                }
                            },
                        }
                    },
                },
                "semantic_quality_context": {
                    "contract_id": 9,
                    "contract_key": "semantic-platform",
                    "contract_version": 4,
                    "contract_hash": "a" * 64,
                    "site_scope": "domestic",
                    "asset_scope": "unknown",
                },
            }
        ),
        items=[
            SimpleNamespace(
                asset_id=11,
                result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
                    {
                        "stage_a": {
                            "semantic": {
                                "style": {
                                    "status": "required",
                                    "values": [{"entity_id": "style.modern"}],
                                }
                            },
                            "semantic_mapping_stats": {
                                "style": {
                                    "candidate": 1,
                                    "mapped": 1,
                                    "unmapped": 0,
                                    "conflicted": 0,
                                }
                            },
                            "semantic_review_stats": {
                                "style": {
                                    "corrected": 0,
                                    "reviewed": 1,
                                    "required": 1,
                                    "null_truth": 0,
                                    "null_correct": 0,
                                    "bilingual": 0,
                                    "bilingual_consistent": 0,
                                }
                            },
                            "semantic_reconciliation_stats": {
                                "expected": 1,
                                "matched": 1,
                            },
                        }
                    }
                ),
            )
        ],
    )

    report, evidence, context = build_run_semantic_quality(run)

    assert report.fields["style"].precision == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert report.fields["style"].review_coverage == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert report.reconciliation_rate == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert evidence["truth_source"] == "frozen_run_snapshot"
    assert evidence["truth_revision_min"] == 2
    assert context["contract_id"] == 9


def test_run_quality_exposes_unmapped_candidates_without_counting_as_predictions() -> None:
    run = SimpleNamespace(
        id=19,
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        execution_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "semantic_truth_snapshot": {
                    "schema_version": "semantic-truth-snapshot-v1",
                    "assets": {
                        "11": {
                            "truth_revision": 1,
                            "truth": {
                                "semantic": {
                                    "material": {
                                        "values": [
                                            {"entity_id": "material.natural_wood"}
                                        ]
                                    }
                                }
                            },
                        }
                    },
                }
            }
        ),
        items=[
            SimpleNamespace(
                asset_id=11,
                result_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
                    {
                        "stage_a": {
                            "semantic": {"material": {"values": []}},
                            "semantic_unmapped_candidates": {
                                "material": [
                                    {
                                        "raw_value": "原木本色",
                                        "evidence_ref": "evaluation:19#material",
                                        "suggestions": [
                                            {
                                                "entity_id": "material.natural_wood",
                                                "canonical_value": "原木色",
                                                "score": 0.8,
                                            }
                                        ],
                                    }
                                ]
                            },
                            "semantic_mapping_stats": {
                                "material": {
                                    "candidate": 1,
                                    "mapped": 0,
                                    "unmapped": 1,
                                    "conflicted": 0,
                                }
                            },
                        }
                    }

    report, evidence, _context = build_run_semantic_quality(run)

    assert report.fields["material"].predicted_count == 0
    assert evidence["unmapped_candidates"][0]["raw_value"] == "原木本色"
    assert evidence["unmapped_candidates"][0]["field_key"] == "material"


def test_quality_snapshot_is_append_only_and_idempotent() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        contract = TagDemandContract(
            contract_key="semantic-platform",
            version=1,
            status="active",
            definition_json="{}",
            contract_hash="a" * 64,
            created_by="test",
        )
        baseline_set = BaselineSet(
            name="semantic-quality-fixture",
            category_key="model_3d_su",
            default_expected_level="L1",
            fingerprint="b" * 64,
            created_by="test",
        )
        bundle = StrategyBundle(
            canonical_hash="c" * 64,
            strategy_schema_version="strategy-bundle-v1",
            model_id="fixture-model",
            model_config_snapshot="{}",
            prompt_a_version="prompt-a",
            prompt_b_version="prompt-b",
            rubric_version="rubric-v1",
            engine_version="engine-v1",
        )
        db.add_all([contract, baseline_set, bundle])
        db.flush()
        run = BaselineRegressionRun(
            baseline_set_id=baseline_set.id,
            sequence_no=1,
            strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
            category_key="model_3d_su",
            strategy_snapshot_json="{}",
            execution_snapshot_json="{}",
            baseline_set_fingerprint=baseline_set.fingerprint,
            status="completed",
            total=1,
            completed=1,
            valid_predictions=1,
            metrics_json="{}",
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(run)
        db.flush()
        report = compute_semantic_quality_metrics(
            truth_by_asset={"a": {"style": {"style.modern"}}},
            predicted_by_asset={"a": {"style": {"style.modern"}}},
            mapping_stats={"style": {"candidate": 1, "mapped": 1, "unmapped": 0, "conflicted": 0, "evaluated": 1}},
            review_stats={"style": _review_stats()},
            reconciliation_stats={"expected": 1, "matched": 1},
        )
        rows = persist_semantic_quality_snapshot(
            db,
            baseline_run_id=run.id,
            contract_id=contract.id,
            category_key="model_3d_su",
            site_scope="domestic",
            asset_scope="whole",
            report=report,
        )
        db.commit()
        assert {row.field_key for row in rows} == {"style", "_aggregate"}
        again = persist_semantic_quality_snapshot(
            db,
            baseline_run_id=run.id,
            contract_id=contract.id,
            category_key="model_3d_su",
            site_scope="domestic",
            asset_scope="whole",
            report=report,
        )
        assert [row.id for row in again] == [row.id for row in rows]
        assert db.query(SemanticQualityMetricSnapshot).count() == 2
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_server_backup.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import json
import subprocess
import sys
import tarfile
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from io import BytesIO, StringIO
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from app.server_backup import (
    ServerBackupError,
    assert_empty_restore_directory,
    build_backup_manifest,
    create_data_archive,
    main as server_backup_main,
    provision_runtime_master_key,
    sanitize_mysql_dump,
    validate_backup_manifest,
    validate_data_archive,
    validate_mysql_client_version,
    write_backup_status,
    write_mysql_client_config,
)


def _valid_master_key() -> bytes:
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(b"k" * 32)


def _backup_files(tmp_path: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path, Path]:
    backup = tmp_path / "backup"
    backup.mkdir()
    dump = backup / "mysql.sql"
    archive = backup / "platform-data.tar.gz"
    dump.write_text("CREATE TABLE fixture (id INT);\n", encoding="utf-8")
    with tarfile.open(archive, "w:gz") as bundle:
        payload = _valid_master_key()
        member = tarfile.TarInfo("secrets/master.key")
        member.size = len(payload)
        member.mode = 0o600
        bundle.addfile(member, BytesIO(payload))
    return backup, dump, archive


def test_runtime_value_allows_only_non_secret_build_revision(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment = tmp_path / "runtime.env"
    environment.write_text(
        f"LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA={'a' * 40}\nINLINE_API_KEY=must-not-leak\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    output = StringIO()

    assert server_backup_main(
        [
            "runtime-value",
            "--env-file",
            str(environment),
            "--field",
            "LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA",
        ],
        output=output,
    ) == 0
    assert output.getvalue().strip() == "a" * 40

    blocked = StringIO()
    assert server_backup_main(
        [
            "runtime-value",
            "--env-file",
            str(environment),
            "--field",
            "INLINE_API_KEY",
        ],
        output=blocked,
    ) == 2
    assert "runtime_field_forbidden" in blocked.getvalue()
    assert "must-not-leak" not in blocked.getvalue()


def test_runtime_value_cli_runs_without_third_party_site_packages(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment = tmp_path / "runtime.env"
    environment.write_text(
        f"LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA={'b' * 40}\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    completed = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            "-S",
            "-X",
            "utf8",
            "-m",
            "app.server_backup",
            "runtime-value",
            "--env-file",
            str(environment),
            "--field",
            "LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA",
        ],
        cwd=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1],
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )

    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stderr
    assert completed.stdout.strip() == "b" * 40

    dsn_file = tmp_path / "database-url"
    dsn_file.write_text(
        "mysql+pymysql://backup:p%40ss@127.0.0.1:3306/labelsystem",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    dsn_file.chmod(0o600)
    client_config = tmp_path / "client.cnf"
    completed = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            "-S",
            "-X",
            "utf8",
            "-m",
            "app.server_backup",
            "write-client-config",
            "--dsn-file",
            str(dsn_file),
            "--expected-schema",
            "labelsystem",
            "--output",
            str(client_config),
        ],
        cwd=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1],
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )

    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stdout + completed.stderr
    assert "p@ss" not in completed.stdout + completed.stderr
    assert client_config.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
    assert 'password="p@ss"' in client_config.read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_backup_manifest_contains_only_relative_hash_evidence(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    backup, _dump, _archive = _backup_files(tmp_path)

    manifest = build_backup_manifest(
        backup,
        source_schema="labelsystem",
        file_names=("mysql.sql", "platform-data.tar.gz"),
    )
    serialized = json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False)

    assert manifest["format"] == "label-system-server-backup-v1"
    assert manifest["source_schema"] == "labelsystem"
    assert [item["path"] for item in manifest["files"]] == [
        "mysql.sql",
        "platform-data.tar.gz",
    ]
    assert all(len(item["sha256"]) == 64 for item in manifest["files"])
    assert str(tmp_path) not in serialized
    assert "password" not in serialized.lower()
    assert "dsn" not in serialized.lower()


def test_mysql_dump_sanitizer_removes_source_definers_and_keeps_logic(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = tmp_path / "raw.sql"
    target = tmp_path / "mysql.sql"
    source.write_bytes(
        b"/*!50003 CREATE*/ /*!50017 DEFINER=`legacy`@`%`*/ "
        b"/*!50003 TRIGGER fixture_trigger BEFORE INSERT ON fixture "
        b"FOR EACH ROW SET NEW.id = 1 */;;\n"
        b"/*!50013 DEFINER=`legacy`@`localhost` SQL SECURITY DEFINER */\n"
        b"CREATE VIEW fixture_view AS SELECT 1;\n"
        b"/*!50003 CREATE*/ /*!50020 DEFINER=`legacy`@`localhost`*/ "
        b"/*!50003 PROCEDURE fixture_procedure() SELECT 1 */;;\n"
        b"/*!50106 CREATE*/ /*!50117 DEFINER=`legacy`@`localhost`*/ "
        b"/*!50106 EVENT fixture_event ON SCHEDULE EVERY 1 DAY DO SELECT 1 */;;\n"
        b"SELECT '/*!50017 DEFINER=`literal`@`data`*/';\n"
    )

    assert sanitize_mysql_dump(source, output=target) == 4

    rendered = target.read_bytes()
    assert b"/*!50017 DEFINER=`legacy`" not in rendered
    assert b"/*!50013 DEFINER=" not in rendered
    assert b"/*!50020 DEFINER=" not in rendered
    assert b"/*!50117 DEFINER=" not in rendered
    assert b"/*!50013 SQL SECURITY DEFINER */" in rendered
    assert b"/*!50003 CREATE*/  /*!50003 TRIGGER fixture_trigger" in rendered
    assert b"CREATE VIEW fixture_view" in rendered
    assert b"/*!50003 PROCEDURE fixture_procedure" in rendered
    assert b"/*!50106 EVENT fixture_event" in rendered
    assert b"SELECT '/*!50017 DEFINER=`literal`@`data`*/'" in rendered
    assert target.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600


def test_mysql_dump_sanitizer_removes_plain_routine_definers(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = tmp_path / "raw.sql"
    target = tmp_path / "mysql.sql"
    source.write_bytes(
        b"DELIMITER ;;\n"
        b"CREATE DEFINER=`legacy`@`localhost` PROCEDURE `fixture_procedure`()\n"
        b"BEGIN SELECT 1; END ;;\n"
        b"CREATE DEFINER=`legacy`@`%` FUNCTION `fixture_function`() "
        b"RETURNS INT RETURN 1 ;;\n"
        b"SELECT 'CREATE DEFINER=`literal`@`data` PROCEDURE kept';\n"
    )

    assert sanitize_mysql_dump(source, output=target) == 2

    rendered = target.read_bytes()
    assert b"CREATE PROCEDURE `fixture_procedure`()" in rendered
    assert b"CREATE FUNCTION `fixture_function`()" in rendered
    assert b"DEFINER=`legacy`" not in rendered
    assert b"SELECT 'CREATE DEFINER=`literal`@`data` PROCEDURE kept'" in rendered


def test_mysql_dump_sanitizer_fails_closed_on_malformed_definer(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = tmp_path / "raw.sql"
    target = tmp_path / "mysql.sql"
    source.write_bytes(b"/*!50017 DEFINER=`broken`@`localhost`\n")

    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        sanitize_mysql_dump(source, output=target)

    assert error.value.code == "mysql_dump_definer_invalid"
    assert not target.exists()


def test_mysql_dump_sanitizer_handles_chunk_boundary(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = tmp_path / "raw.sql"
    target = tmp_path / "mysql.sql"
    prefix = b"x" * (1024 * 1024 - 8) + b"\n"
    source.write_bytes(
        prefix
        + b"/*!50003 CREATE*/ /*!50017 DEFINER=`legacy`@`localhost`*/ "
        + b"/*!50003 TRIGGER boundary_trigger BEFORE INSERT ON fixture "
        + b"FOR EACH ROW SET NEW.id = 1 */;;\n"
    )

    assert sanitize_mysql_dump(source, output=target) == 1
    rendered = target.read_bytes()
    assert rendered.startswith(prefix + b"/*!50003 CREATE*/ ")
    assert b"DEFINER=`legacy`" not in rendered
    assert b"TRIGGER boundary_trigger" in rendered


def test_manifest_validation_rejects_hash_drift_and_wrong_schema(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    backup, dump, _archive = _backup_files(tmp_path)
    manifest = build_backup_manifest(
        backup,
        source_schema="labelsystem",
        file_names=("mysql.sql", "platform-data.tar.gz"),
    )

    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        validate_backup_manifest(
            backup,
            manifest,
            expected_source_schema="other_schema",
        )
    assert error.value.code == "backup_schema_mismatch"

    dump.write_text("X" * dump.stat().st_size, encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        validate_backup_manifest(
            backup,
            manifest,
            expected_source_schema="labelsystem",
        )
    assert error.value.code == "backup_file_hash_mismatch"


def test_compressed_dump_is_valid_zstd_of_the_sanitized_sql(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """备份格式 v2：清理后的 SQL 直接流进 zstd，解压回来与明文清理结果逐字节一致。"""
    source = tmp_path / "raw.sql"
    source.write_bytes(
        b"/*!50003 CREATE*/ /*!50017 DEFINER=`legacy`@`%`*/ "
        b"/*!50003 TRIGGER fixture_trigger BEFORE INSERT ON fixture "
        b"FOR EACH ROW SET NEW.id = 1 */;;\n"
        + b"INSERT INTO fixture VALUES (1,'repeated payload');\n" * 5000
    )
    plain, packed = tmp_path / "mysql.sql", tmp_path / "mysql.sql.zst"

    assert sanitize_mysql_dump(source, output=plain) == 1
    assert sanitize_mysql_dump(source, output=packed) == 1

    assert packed.stat().st_size < plain.stat().st_size / 5
    assert packed.stat().st_mode & 0o077 == 0
    restored = subprocess.run(["zstd", "-dc", str(packed)], check=True, capture_output=True).stdout
    assert restored == plain.read_bytes()


def test_compressed_dump_without_zstd_fails_closed(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    import app.server_backup as module

    source = tmp_path / "raw.sql"
    source.write_bytes(b"SELECT 1;\n")
    monkeypatch.setattr(module.shutil, "which", lambda _name: None)
    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        sanitize_mysql_dump(source, output=tmp_path / "mysql.sql.zst")
    assert error.value.code == "mysql_dump_compressor_missing"
    assert not (tmp_path / "mysql.sql.zst").exists()


def test_manifest_format_follows_the_sql_file_and_must_match_on_validation(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    backup, dump, _archive = _backup_files(tmp_path)
    dump.rename(backup / "mysql.sql.zst")
    manifest = build_backup_manifest(
        backup,
        source_schema="labelsystem",
        file_names=("mysql.sql.zst", "platform-data.tar.gz"),
    )
    assert manifest["format"] == "label-system-server-backup-v2"
    assert validate_backup_manifest(backup, manifest, expected_source_schema="labelsystem")

    output = StringIO()
    (backup / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest), encoding="utf-8")
    assert server_backup_main(["backup-sql-file", "--backup-dir", str(backup)], output=output) == 0
    assert output.getvalue().strip() == "mysql.sql.zst"

    mislabeled = {**manifest, "format": "label-system-server-backup-v1"}
    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        validate_backup_manifest(backup, mislabeled, expected_source_schema="labelsystem")
    assert error.value.code == "backup_file_set_invalid"


def test_manifest_rejects_symlink_and_path_traversal(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    backup, dump, archive = _backup_files(tmp_path)
    outside = tmp_path / "outside.sql"
    outside.write_text("outside", encoding="utf-8")
    dump.unlink()
    dump.symlink_to(outside)

    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        build_backup_manifest(
            backup,
            source_schema="labelsystem",
            file_names=("mysql.sql", "platform-data.tar.gz"),
        )
    assert error.value.code == "backup_file_invalid"

    traversal_manifest = {
        "format": "label-system-server-backup-v1",
        "source_schema": "labelsystem",
        "files": [
            {"path": "../outside.sql", "size": 7, "sha256": "a" * 64},
            {
                "path": archive.name,
                "size": archive.stat().st_size,
                "sha256": "b" * 64,
            },
        ],
    }
    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        validate_backup_manifest(
            backup,
            traversal_manifest,
            expected_source_schema="labelsystem",
        )
    assert error.value.code == "backup_path_invalid"


def test_data_archive_rejects_traversal_links_and_missing_master_key(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    traversal = tmp_path / "traversal.tar.gz"
    with tarfile.open(traversal, "w:gz") as bundle:
        payload = b"unsafe"
        member = tarfile.TarInfo("../outside")
        member.size = len(payload)
        bundle.addfile(member, BytesIO(payload))

    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        validate_data_archive(traversal)
    assert error.value.code == "data_archive_path_invalid"

    missing_key = tmp_path / "missing-key.tar.gz"
    with tarfile.open(missing_key, "w:gz") as bundle:
        payload = b"file"
        member = tarfile.TarInfo("images/a.txt")
        member.size = len(payload)
        bundle.addfile(member, BytesIO(payload))
    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        validate_data_archive(missing_key)
    assert error.value.code == "data_archive_master_key_missing"

    injected_secret = tmp_path / "injected-secret.tar.gz"
    with tarfile.open(injected_secret, "w:gz") as bundle:
        for name, payload in (
            ("secrets/master.key", _valid_master_key()),
            ("secrets/operations-metrics.token", b"rotatable-secret"),
        ):
            member = tarfile.TarInfo(name)
            member.mode = 0o600
            member.size = len(payload)
            bundle.addfile(member, BytesIO(payload))
    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        validate_data_archive(injected_secret)
    assert error.value.code == "data_archive_secret_forbidden"

    invalid_key = tmp_path / "invalid-key.tar.gz"
    with tarfile.open(invalid_key, "w:gz") as bundle:
        payload = b"not-a-valid-master-key"
        member = tarfile.TarInfo("secrets/master.key")
        member.mode = 0o600
        member.size = len(payload)
        bundle.addfile(member, BytesIO(payload))
    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        validate_data_archive(invalid_key)
    assert error.value.code == "data_archive_master_key_invalid"

    public_key = tmp_path / "public-key.tar.gz"
    with tarfile.open(public_key, "w:gz") as bundle:
        payload = _valid_master_key()
        member = tarfile.TarInfo("secrets/master.key")
        member.mode = 0o644
        member.size = len(payload)
        bundle.addfile(member, BytesIO(payload))
    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        validate_data_archive(public_key)
    assert error.value.code == "data_archive_master_key_permissions_invalid"


def test_data_archive_can_normalize_an_external_runtime_master_key(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    data_dir = tmp_path / "data"
    (data_dir / "images").mkdir(parents=True)
    (data_dir / "images" / "fixture.txt").write_text(
        "fixture",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    (data_dir / "secrets").mkdir()
    (data_dir / "secrets" / "operations-metrics.token").write_text(
        "rotatable-metrics-token",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    (data_dir / "secrets" / "content-ingress.token").write_text(
        "rotatable-ingress-token",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    external_key = tmp_path / "runtime-master.key"
    external_key.write_bytes(_valid_master_key())
    external_key.chmod(0o600)
    archive = tmp_path / "platform-data.tar.gz"

    create_data_archive(
        data_dir,
        master_key_file=external_key,
        output=archive,
    )
    validate_data_archive(archive)
    assert archive.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600

    with tarfile.open(archive, "r:gz") as bundle:
        names = bundle.getnames()
        assert "images/fixture.txt" in names
        assert "secrets/master.key" in names
        assert "secrets/operations-metrics.token" not in names
        assert "secrets/content-ingress.token" not in names
        restored = bundle.extractfile("secrets/master.key")
        assert restored is not None
        assert restored.read() == _valid_master_key()


def test_data_archive_excludes_rebuildable_and_runtime_only_roots(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    data_dir = tmp_path / "data"
    roots = ("images", "cache", "logs", "database", "operations")
    for root_name in roots:
        root = data_dir / root_name
        root.mkdir(parents=True)
        (root / "fixture.txt").write_text(root_name, encoding="utf-8")
    external_key = tmp_path / "runtime-master.key"
    external_key.write_bytes(_valid_master_key())
    external_key.chmod(0o600)
    archive = tmp_path / "platform-data.tar.gz"

    create_data_archive(
        data_dir,
        master_key_file=external_key,
        output=archive,
    )

    with tarfile.open(archive, "r:gz") as bundle:
        names = set(bundle.getnames())
    assert "images/fixture.txt" in names
    assert "secrets/master.key" in names
    for root_name in ("cache", "logs", "database", "operations"):
        assert root_name not in names
        assert f"{root_name}/fixture.txt" not in names


def test_data_archive_leaves_out_the_original_store_but_keeps_other_images(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    """增量原图目录挂在机械盘上、有保留期，不进每日备份；其余图片照旧备份。"""
    data_dir = tmp_path / "data"
    originals = data_dir / "images" / "originals"
    originals.mkdir(parents=True)
    (originals / ".label-original-store").write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    (originals / "material-x.jpg").write_bytes(b"jpg")
    (data_dir / "images" / "upload.jpg").write_bytes(b"jpg")
    external_key = tmp_path / "runtime-master.key"
    external_key.write_bytes(_valid_master_key())
    external_key.chmod(0o600)
    archive = tmp_path / "platform-data.tar.gz"

    create_data_archive(data_dir, master_key_file=external_key, output=archive)
    validate_data_archive(archive)

    with tarfile.open(archive, "r:gz") as bundle:
        names = set(bundle.getnames())
    assert "images/upload.jpg" in names
    assert not any(name.startswith("images/originals") for name in names)


def test_data_archive_rejects_an_invalid_source_key_without_partial_output(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    data_dir = tmp_path / "data"
    data_dir.mkdir()
    source = tmp_path / "invalid-master.key"
    source.write_text("not-a-valid-key", encoding="utf-8")
    source.chmod(0o600)
    archive = tmp_path / "platform-data.tar.gz"

    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        create_data_archive(
            data_dir,
            master_key_file=source,
            output=archive,
        )

    assert error.value.code == "platform_master_key_invalid"
    assert not archive.exists()


def test_restore_directory_must_exist_and_be_empty(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    target = tmp_path / "restore"
    target.mkdir()
    assert_empty_restore_directory(target)
    (target / "existing").write_text("occupied", encoding="utf-8")

    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        assert_empty_restore_directory(target)
    assert error.value.code == "restore_data_directory_not_empty"


def test_runtime_master_key_is_provisioned_once_and_never_overwritten(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = tmp_path / "external-master.key"
    source.write_bytes(_valid_master_key())
    source.chmod(0o600)
    data_dir = tmp_path / "data"
    data_dir.mkdir()

    target = provision_runtime_master_key(source, data_dir=data_dir)

    assert target == data_dir / "secrets" / "master.key"
    assert target.read_bytes() == _valid_master_key()
    assert target.stat().st_mode & 0o077 == 0
    assert provision_runtime_master_key(source, data_dir=data_dir) == target

    source.write_bytes(base64.urlsafe_b64encode(b"x" * 32))
    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        provision_runtime_master_key(source, data_dir=data_dir)
    assert error.value.code == "runtime_master_key_mismatch"
    assert target.read_bytes() == _valid_master_key()


def test_existing_runtime_master_key_is_normalized_for_linux_container(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    source = tmp_path / "external-master.key"
    source.write_bytes(_valid_master_key())
    source.chmod(0o600)
    data_dir = tmp_path / "data"
    target = data_dir / "secrets" / "master.key"
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    target.write_bytes(_valid_master_key())
    target.chmod(0o600)
    ownership: list[tuple[Path, int, int]] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr("app.server_backup.sys.platform", "linux")
    monkeypatch.setattr("app.server_backup.os.geteuid", lambda: 0)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "app.server_backup.os.chown",
        lambda path, uid, gid: ownership.append((Path(path), uid, gid)),
    )

    provision_runtime_master_key(source, data_dir=data_dir)

    assert (target, 10001, 10001) in ownership
    assert (target.parent, 10001, 10001) in ownership


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "value",
    [
        "mysql  Ver 8.4.11 for macos15.0 on arm64 (MySQL Community Server - GPL)",
        "/opt/homebrew/opt/mysql@8.4/bin/mysql  Ver 8.4.11 for macos26.6 on arm64 (Homebrew)",
        "mysqldump  Ver 8.4.11 for Linux on x86_64 (MySQL Community Server - GPL)",
    ],
)
def test_backup_accepts_only_oracle_mysql_84_clients(value: str) -> None:
    assert validate_mysql_client_version(value) == "8.4.11"

    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError, match="8.4"):
        validate_mysql_client_version(
            "mysql  Ver 5.7.44 for Linux on x86_64"
        )
    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError, match="Oracle"):
        validate_mysql_client_version(
            "mysqldump  Ver 8.4.11-MariaDB for Linux on x86_64"
        )


def test_mysql_client_config_uses_protected_dsn_file_and_mode(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    dsn_file = tmp_path / "database-url"
    dsn_file.write_text(
        "mysql+pymysql://backup-user:fixture-secret@127.0.0.1:3306/labelsystem",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    dsn_file.chmod(0o600)
    output = tmp_path / "client.cnf"

    write_mysql_client_config(
        dsn_file,
        expected_schema="labelsystem",
        output=output,
    )

    content = output.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert output.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
    assert "user=\"backup-user\"" in content
    assert "password=\"fixture-secret\"" in content
    assert "database=" not in content

    dsn_file.chmod(0o644)
    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        write_mysql_client_config(
            dsn_file,
            expected_schema="labelsystem",
            output=tmp_path / "other.cnf",
        )
    assert error.value.code == "database_url_file_permissions_invalid"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("dsn", "expected_code"),
    [
        (
            "mysql+pymysql://user:p@ss@prod.example/labelsystem",
            "database_url_contract_invalid",
        ),
        (
            "mysql+pymysql://user:secret@prod.example/labelsystem#ignored",
            "database_url_contract_invalid",
        ),
        (
            "mysql+pymysql://user:secret@prod.example/labelsystem#",
            "database_url_contract_invalid",
        ),
        (
            "mysql+pymysql://user:secret@prod\texample/labelsystem",
            "database_url_invalid",
        ),
        (
            "mysql+pymysql://user:sec\x7fret@prod.example/labelsystem",
            "database_url_invalid",
        ),
        (
            "mysql+pymysql://user:@prod.example/labelsystem",
            "database_url_contract_invalid",
        ),
        (
            "mysql+pymysql://user:secret@prod.example:0/labelsystem",
            "database_url_contract_invalid",
        ),
        (
            "mysql+pymysql://user:secret@prod example/labelsystem",
            "database_url_invalid",
        ),
        (
            "mysql+pymysql://user:secret@prod%example/labelsystem",
            "database_url_contract_invalid",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_mysql_client_config_rejects_ambiguous_or_invalid_dsn(
    tmp_path: Path,
    dsn: str,
    expected_code: str,
) -> None:
    dsn_file = tmp_path / "database-url"
    dsn_file.write_text(dsn, encoding="utf-8")
    dsn_file.chmod(0o600)

    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        write_mysql_client_config(
            dsn_file,
            expected_schema="labelsystem",
            output=tmp_path / "client.cnf",
        )

    assert error.value.code == expected_code
    assert not (tmp_path / "client.cnf").exists()


def test_mysql_client_host_override_is_loopback_only(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    dsn_file = tmp_path / "database-url"
    dsn_file.write_text(
        "mysql+pymysql://backup:secret@host.docker.internal:33084/labelsystem",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    dsn_file.chmod(0o600)

    output = tmp_path / "client.cnf"
    write_mysql_client_config(
        dsn_file,
        expected_schema="labelsystem",
        output=output,
        host_override="127.0.0.1",
    )
    assert 'host="127.0.0.1"' in output.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    with pytest.raises(ServerBackupError) as error:
        write_mysql_client_config(
            dsn_file,
            expected_schema="labelsystem",
            output=tmp_path / "forbidden.cnf",
            host_override="db.external.example",
        )
    assert error.value.code == "mysql_client_host_override_forbidden"


def test_successful_backup_writes_an_atomic_redacted_status_record(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    backup, _dump, _archive = _backup_files(tmp_path)
    manifest = build_backup_manifest(
        backup,
        source_schema="labelsystem",
        file_names=("mysql.sql", "platform-data.tar.gz"),
    )
    (backup / "manifest.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    status_file = tmp_path / "data" / "operations" / "last-successful-backup.json"
    completed_at = datetime(2026, 8, 30, 7, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    write_backup_status(
        backup,
        status_file=status_file,
        completed_at=completed_at,
    )

    payload = json.loads(status_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert payload == {
        "completed_at": "2026-08-30T07:00:00+00:00",
        "format": "label-system-backup-status-v1",
        "manifest_sha256": payload["manifest_sha256"],
        "source_schema": "labelsystem",
    }
    assert len(payload["manifest_sha256"]) == 64
    assert status_file.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
    serialized = json.dumps(payload).lower()
    assert str(tmp_path).lower() not in serialized
    assert "password" not in serialized
    assert "dsn" not in serialized

===== FILE backend/tests/test_server_backup_scripts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import tarfile
from pathlib import Path

import pytest


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def _valid_master_key_text() -> str:
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(b"k" * 32).decode("ascii")


def _runtime_fixture(tmp_path: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path, str]:
    source_schema = "source_schema"
    secret_root = tmp_path / "secrets"
    secret_root.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    app_dsn = secret_root / "database-url"
    migration_dsn = secret_root / "migration-database-url"
    backup_dsn = secret_root / "backup-database-url"
    for path, role in (
        (app_dsn, "app"),
        (migration_dsn, "migration"),
        (backup_dsn, "backup"),
    ):
        path.write_text(
            f"mysql+pymysql://{role}:fixture-secret@127.0.0.1:3306/{source_schema}",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        path.chmod(0o600)
    nas = tmp_path / "nas"
    snapshot_source = tmp_path / "migration" / "source"
    checkpoints = tmp_path / "migration" / "checkpoints"
    nas.mkdir()
    snapshot_source.mkdir(parents=True)
    checkpoints.mkdir(parents=True)
    (snapshot_source / "platform.sqlite3").write_bytes(b"snapshot")
    environment = {
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_IMAGE_TAG": "server-fixture",
        "LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA": "a" * 40,
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_BIND_ADDRESS": "127.0.0.1",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_PORT": "8081",
        "SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE": "true",
        "ALLOW_DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD": "false",
        "DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": str(app_dsn),
        "MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": str(migration_dsn),
        "BACKUP_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": str(backup_dsn),
        "MYSQL_PLATFORM_SCHEMA": source_schema,
        "NAS_MAPS_SOURCE": str(nas),
        "MIGRATION_SNAPSHOT_SOURCE_DIR": str(snapshot_source),
        "MIGRATION_CHECKPOINT_DIR": str(checkpoints),
        "WORKER_GROUP_SIZE": "32",
        "WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS": "1",
        "WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT": "33",
    }
    environment_file = tmp_path / "runtime env"
    environment_file.write_text(
        "\n".join(f"{key}={value}" for key, value in environment.items())
        + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    return environment_file, backup_dsn, source_schema


def _fake_mysql_clients(tmp_path: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path, Path]:
    log = tmp_path / "mysql-client.log"
    mysqldump = tmp_path / "mysqldump"
    mysql = tmp_path / "mysql"
    mysqldump.write_text(
        "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n"
        "set -euo pipefail\n"
        "printf 'mysqldump %s\\n' \"$*\" >> \"$BACKUP_TEST_LOG\"\n"
        "if [[ \"${1:-}\" == '--version' ]]; then\n"
        "  echo 'mysqldump  Ver 8.4.11 for fixture (MySQL Community Server - GPL)'\n"
        "  exit 0\n"
        "fi\n"
        "result=''\n"
        "for arg in \"$@\"; do\n"
        "  case \"$arg\" in --result-file=*) result=${arg#--result-file=} ;; esac\n"
        "done\n"
        "test -n \"$result\"\n"
        "if [[ \"${MALFORMED_DUMP:-}\" == '1' ]]; then\n"
        "  printf '%s\\n' '/*!50017 DEFINER=`broken`@`localhost`' > \"$result\"\n"
        "  exit 0\n"
        "fi\n"
        "printf '%s\\n' '/*!50003 CREATE*/ "
        "/*!50017 DEFINER=`legacy`@`%`*/ "
        "/*!50003 TRIGGER restored_fixture BEFORE INSERT ON fixture "
        "FOR EACH ROW SET NEW.id = 1 */;;' > \"$result\"\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    mysql.write_text(
        "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n"
        "set -euo pipefail\n"
        "printf 'mysql %s\\n' \"$*\" >> \"$BACKUP_TEST_LOG\"\n"
        "if [[ \"${1:-}\" == '--version' ]]; then\n"
        "  echo 'mysql  Ver 8.4.11 for fixture (MySQL Community Server - GPL)'\n"
        "  exit 0\n"
        "fi\n"
        "for arg in \"$@\"; do\n"
        "  if [[ \"$arg\" == '-e' ]]; then\n"
        "    counter_file=\"${BACKUP_TEST_LOG}.table-count\"\n"
        "    count=0\n"
        "    if [[ -f \"$counter_file\" ]]; then count=$(<\"$counter_file\"); fi\n"
        "    if [[ \"$count\" == '0' ]]; then echo 0; else echo 120; fi\n"
        "    echo $((count + 1)) > \"$counter_file\"\n"
        "    exit 0\n"
        "  fi\n"
        "done\n"
        "cat >> \"${BACKUP_TEST_LOG}.stdin\"\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    mysqldump.chmod(0o755)
    mysql.chmod(0o755)
    return mysqldump, mysql, log


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("compression", "sql_name", "backup_format"),
    [
        ("zstd", "mysql.sql.zst", "label-system-server-backup-v2"),
        ("none", "mysql.sql", "label-system-server-backup-v1"),
    ],
)
def test_backup_and_restore_scripts_keep_credentials_out_of_argv(
    tmp_path: Path,
    compression: str,
    sql_name: str,
    backup_format: str,
) -> None:
    environment_file, _backup_dsn, source_schema = _runtime_fixture(tmp_path)
    mysqldump, mysql, log = _fake_mysql_clients(tmp_path)
    data_dir = tmp_path / "platform-data"
    (data_dir / "secrets").mkdir(parents=True)
    (data_dir / "secrets" / "master.key").write_text(
        _valid_master_key_text(),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    (data_dir / "secrets" / "master.key").chmod(0o600)
    (data_dir / "images").mkdir()
    (data_dir / "images" / "fixture.txt").write_text("image", encoding="utf-8")
    backup_dir = tmp_path / "backup-output"
    command_environment = {
        **os.environ,
        "PYTHON_BIN": sys.executable,
        "MYSQLDUMP_BIN": str(mysqldump),
        "MYSQL_BIN": str(mysql),
        "BACKUP_TEST_LOG": str(log),
        "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT / "backend"),
        "BACKUP_SQL_COMPRESSION": compression,
    }

    backup = subprocess.run(
        [
            str(ROOT / "deploy" / "server" / "backup.sh"),
            str(environment_file),
            str(data_dir),
            str(backup_dir),
        ],
        cwd=ROOT,
        env=command_environment,
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    assert backup.returncode == 0, backup.stderr
    manifest = json.loads(
        (backup_dir / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    assert manifest["source_schema"] == source_schema
    assert manifest["format"] == backup_format
    assert {item["path"] for item in manifest["files"]} == {sql_name, "platform-data.tar.gz"}
    assert sorted(path.name for path in backup_dir.iterdir()) == sorted(
        ["manifest.json", sql_name, "platform-data.tar.gz"]
    )
    with tarfile.open(backup_dir / "platform-data.tar.gz", "r:gz") as archive:
        assert "secrets/master.key" in archive.getnames()
    client_log = log.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "--single-transaction" in client_log
    assert "--routines" in client_log
    assert "--triggers" in client_log
    assert "--events" in client_log
    assert "mysqldump --version" in client_log
    assert f"--result-file={backup_dir / 'mysql.raw.sql'}" in client_log
    if compression == "zstd":
        sanitized_dump = subprocess.run(
            ["zstd", "-dc", str(backup_dir / sql_name)],
            check=True,
            capture_output=True,
        ).stdout.decode("utf-8")
    else:
        sanitized_dump = (backup_dir / sql_name).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "DEFINER=`legacy`" not in sanitized_dump
    assert "/*!50003 TRIGGER restored_fixture" in sanitized_dump
    assert not (backup_dir / "mysql.raw.sql").exists()
    assert "fixture-secret" not in client_log + backup.stdout + backup.stderr

    target_dsn = tmp_path / "target-database-url"
    target_schema = "restore_schema"
    target_dsn.write_text(
        f"mysql+pymysql://restore:target-secret@127.0.0.1:3306/{target_schema}",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    target_dsn.chmod(0o600)
    target_data = tmp_path / "restored-data"
    target_data.mkdir()
    uname = tmp_path / "uname-fixture"
    identity = tmp_path / "id-fixture"
    chown = tmp_path / "chown-fixture"
    uname.write_text("#!/usr/bin/env bash\necho Linux\n", encoding="utf-8")
    identity.write_text(
        "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n"
        "case \"${1:-}\" in -u|-g) echo 0 ;; *) exit 64 ;; esac\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    chown.write_text(
        "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n"
        "printf 'chown %s\\n' \"$*\" >> \"$BACKUP_TEST_LOG\"\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    for executable in (uname, identity, chown):
        executable.chmod(0o755)
    restore_environment = {
        **command_environment,
        "UNAME_BIN": str(uname),
        "ID_BIN": str(identity),
        "CHOWN_BIN": str(chown),
    }
    restore = subprocess.run(
        [
            str(ROOT / "deploy" / "server" / "restore.sh"),
            str(backup_dir),
            source_schema,
            str(target_dsn),
            target_schema,
            str(target_data),
        ],
        cwd=ROOT,
        env=restore_environment,
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    assert restore.returncode == 0, restore.stderr
    # 恢复时灌进 mysql 的必须是解压后的清理版 SQL，而不是压缩字节
    assert (log.parent / (log.name + ".stdin")).read_text(encoding="utf-8") == sanitized_dump
    restore_receipt = json.loads(
        (target_data / "operations" / "last-successful-restore.json").read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )
    assert restore_receipt["format"] == "label-system-restore-status-v1"
    assert restore_receipt["completed"] is True
    assert restore_receipt["source_schema"] == source_schema
    assert restore_receipt["target_schema"] == target_schema
    assert restore_receipt["restored_tables"] == 120
    assert restore_receipt["manifest_sha256"]
    assert (target_data / "secrets" / "master.key").read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) == _valid_master_key_text()
    assert target_data.joinpath("secrets").stat().st_mode & 0o077 == 0
    assert target_data.joinpath("secrets", "master.key").stat().st_mode & 0o077 == 0
    serialized = log.read_text(encoding="utf-8") + restore.stdout + restore.stderr
    assert "mysql --version" in serialized
    assert f"chown -R 10001:10001 -- {target_data}" in serialized
    assert "fixture-secret" not in serialized
    assert "target-secret" not in serialized

    )

    assert completed.returncode != 0


def test_backup_cleans_raw_dump_when_definer_sanitizing_fails(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment_file, _backup_dsn, _source_schema = _runtime_fixture(tmp_path)
    mysqldump, _mysql, log = _fake_mysql_clients(tmp_path)
    data_dir = tmp_path / "platform-data"
    data_dir.mkdir()
    master_key = tmp_path / "master.key"
    master_key.write_text(_valid_master_key_text(), encoding="utf-8")
    master_key.chmod(0o600)
    runtime = environment_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    runtime += f"API_KEY_MASTER_KEY_SECRET_FILE={master_key}\n"
    environment_file.write_text(runtime, encoding="utf-8")
    backup_dir = tmp_path / "failed-backup"
    environment = {
        **os.environ,
        "PYTHON_BIN": sys.executable,
        "MYSQLDUMP_BIN": str(mysqldump),
        "BACKUP_TEST_LOG": str(log),
        "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT / "backend"),
        "MALFORMED_DUMP": "1",
    }

    completed = subprocess.run(
        [
            str(ROOT / "deploy" / "server" / "backup.sh"),
            str(environment_file),
            str(data_dir),
            str(backup_dir),
        ],
        cwd=ROOT,
        env=environment,
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )

    assert completed.returncode != 0
    assert backup_dir.is_dir()
    assert not (backup_dir / "mysql.raw.sql").exists()
    assert not (backup_dir / "mysql.sql").exists()
    assert not (backup_dir / "manifest.json").exists()

def _daily_backup_quarantine_block() -> str:
    source = (ROOT / "deploy/server/daily-backup.sh").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    start = source.index("backup_verified=0\n")
    end = source.index("trap quarantine_failed_backup EXIT\n") + len("trap quarantine_failed_backup EXIT\n")
    return source[start:end]


def _run_quarantine(
    tmp_path: Path, tail: str, sql_name: str = "mysql.sql"
) -> tuple[subprocess.CompletedProcess[str], Path]:
    daily_root = tmp_path / "daily"
    stamp = "20260926T200000Z"
    target = daily_root / f"daily-{stamp}"
    target.mkdir(parents=True)
    (target / sql_name).write_text("dump", encoding="utf-8")
    (target / "platform-data.tar.gz").write_text("data", encoding="utf-8")
    (target / "backup.log").write_text("log", encoding="utf-8")
    harness = (
        "set -euo pipefail\n"
        f"stamp={stamp}\n"
        f"daily_root='{daily_root}'\n"
        'target="$daily_root/daily-$stamp"\n'
        + _daily_backup_quarantine_block()
        + tail
    )
    completed = subprocess.run(["bash", "-c", harness], capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)
    return completed, daily_root


def test_daily_backup_quarantines_failed_directory_and_drops_large_files(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    completed, daily_root = _run_quarantine(tmp_path, "false\n")
    assert completed.returncode == 1
    failed = daily_root / "failed-20260926T200000Z"
    assert not (daily_root / "daily-20260926T200000Z").exists()
    assert sorted(p.name for p in failed.iterdir()) == ["backup.log"]
    assert "已隔离" in completed.stderr


def test_daily_backup_quarantine_also_drops_a_compressed_dump(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    completed, daily_root = _run_quarantine(tmp_path, "false\n", sql_name="mysql.sql.zst")
    assert completed.returncode == 1
    assert sorted(p.name for p in (daily_root / "failed-20260926T200000Z").iterdir()) == ["backup.log"]


def _daily_backup_verify(target: Path) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """跑 daily-backup.sh 里真实的那段完整性校验（宿主脚本先于写 v2 的 backup.sh 上线，两种格式都得认）。"""
    source = (ROOT / "deploy/server/daily-backup.sh").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    start = source.index("import json, pathlib, sys\n")
    end = source.index("PY\nbackup_verified=1")
    return subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-", str(target)], input=source[start:end],
                          capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("backup_format", "sql_name"),
    [("label-system-server-backup-v1", "mysql.sql"), ("label-system-server-backup-v2", "mysql.sql.zst")],
)
def test_daily_backup_verification_accepts_both_formats_and_rejects_mismatch(
    tmp_path: Path, backup_format: str, sql_name: str
) -> None:
    target = tmp_path / "daily-20260926T200000Z"
    target.mkdir()
    (target / sql_name).write_bytes(b"dump")
    (target / "platform-data.tar.gz").write_bytes(b"data")
    files = [{"path": name, "size": 4, "sha256": "0" * 64} for name in (sql_name, "platform-data.tar.gz")]
    (target / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps({"format": backup_format, "files": files}), encoding="utf-8")
    completed = _daily_backup_verify(target)
    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stderr
    assert f"verified daily-20260926T200000Z {sql_name} 4 bytes" in completed.stdout

    other = {"label-system-server-backup-v1": "label-system-server-backup-v2",
             "label-system-server-backup-v2": "label-system-server-backup-v1"}[backup_format]
    (target / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps({"format": other, "files": files}), encoding="utf-8")
    assert _daily_backup_verify(target).returncode != 0


def test_daily_backup_keeps_a_verified_backup_even_if_queueing_fails(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    completed, daily_root = _run_quarantine(tmp_path, "backup_verified=1\nexit 3\n")
    assert completed.returncode == 3
    kept = daily_root / "daily-20260926T200000Z"
    assert (kept / "mysql.sql").exists()
    assert not (daily_root / "failed-20260926T200000Z").exists()


def test_daily_backup_success_leaves_directory_untouched(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    completed, daily_root = _run_quarantine(tmp_path, "true\n")
    assert completed.returncode == 0
    assert (daily_root / "daily-20260926T200000Z" / "mysql.sql").exists()


def test_daily_backup_script_parses() -> None:
    completed = subprocess.run(
        ["bash", "-n", str(ROOT / "deploy/server/daily-backup.sh")],
        capture_output=True, text=True, check=False,
    )
    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stderr

===== FILE backend/tests/test_server_deployment_contract.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import yaml


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def _default_from_interpolation(value: str) -> int:
    return int(value.rsplit(":-", 1)[1].removesuffix("}"))


def _example_environment(
    filename: str = "runtime.env.example",
) -> dict[str, str]:
    result: dict[str, str] = {}
    for raw_line in (
        ROOT / "deploy" / "server" / filename
    ).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        line = raw_line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
            continue
        key, value = line.split("=", 1)
        result[key] = value
    return result


def test_server_compose_provides_100k_worker_topology_without_inline_secrets() -> None:
    compose = yaml.safe_load(
        (ROOT / "docker-compose.server.yml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    app = compose["services"]["app"]
    worker_group = compose["services"]["worker-group"]
    migration = compose["services"]["migration"]
    app_environment = app["environment"]
    worker_environment = worker_group["environment"]

    assert app_environment["LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV"] == (
        "${LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV:-production}"
    )
    assert app_environment["SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE"] == (
        "${SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE:-true}"
    )
    assert app_environment["SESSION_COOKIE_NAME"] == (
        "${SESSION_COOKIE_NAME:-3d66_session}"
    )
    assert app_environment["ALLOW_INSECURE_SESSION_COOKIE"] == (
        "${ALLOW_INSECURE_SESSION_COOKIE:-false}"
    )
    assert app_environment["ALLOW_DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD"] == (
        "${ALLOW_DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD:-false}"
    )
    assert app["build"]["dockerfile"] == "Dockerfile"
    assert app_environment["DATABASE_URL"] == ""
    assert app_environment["DATABASE_URL_FILE"] == "/run/secrets/database-url"
    assert worker_environment["DATABASE_URL"] == ""
    assert worker_environment["DATABASE_URL_FILE"] == "/run/secrets/database-url"
    app_database_mount = next(
        item
        for item in app["volumes"]
        if isinstance(item, dict) and item["target"] == "/run/secrets/database-url"
    )
    assert app_database_mount == {
        "type": "bind",
        "source": "${DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE:?set DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE}",
        "target": "/run/secrets/database-url",
        "read_only": True,
        "bind": {"create_host_path": False},
    }
    assert app_database_mount in worker_group["volumes"]
    assert migration["profiles"] == ["migration"]
    assert migration["environment"]["DATABASE_URL_FILE"] == (
        "/run/secrets/migration-database-url"
    )
    assert migration["environment"]["MYSQL_PLATFORM_SCHEMA"] == (
        "${MYSQL_PLATFORM_SCHEMA:-labelsystem}"
    )
    assert {
        "type": "bind",
        "source": (
            "${MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE:?set "
            "MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE}"
        ),
        "target": "/run/secrets/migration-database-url",
        "read_only": True,
        "bind": {"create_host_path": False},
    } in migration["volumes"]
    assert "prepare-import-target" in migration["command"][-1]
    assert "seed_defaults" not in migration["command"][-1]
    assert "secrets" not in compose

    embedded_workers = int(app_environment["WORKER_PROCESS_COUNT"])
    group_size = _default_from_interpolation(
        worker_environment["WORKER_PROCESS_COUNT"]
    )
    group_replicas = _default_from_interpolation(
        worker_group["deploy"]["replicas"]
    )
    expected_workers = _default_from_interpolation(
        app_environment["WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT"]
    )
    assert embedded_workers + group_size * group_replicas == 193
    assert expected_workers == 193
    assert _default_from_interpolation(
        worker_environment["DATABASE_POOL_SIZE"]
    ) == 2
    assert worker_environment["DATABASE_MAX_OVERFLOW"] == "0"
    assert worker_group["command"][-1] == "app.worker_group"
    assert worker_group["depends_on"]["app"]["condition"] == (
        "service_healthy"
    )
    assert worker_group["stop_grace_period"] == "30s"
    assert "/api/health/live'" in app["healthcheck"]["test"][-1]
    assert "/api/health/ready" not in app["healthcheck"]["test"][-1]
    for service in (app, worker_group, migration):
        assert service["extra_hosts"] == [
            "host.docker.internal:${LABEL_SYSTEM_NETWORK_GATEWAY:-172.30.66.1}"
        ]

    serialized = json.dumps(compose, ensure_ascii=False).lower()
    assert "mysql+pymysql://" not in serialized
    assert "password=" not in serialized
    assert "api_key=" not in serialized
