def test_release_scripts_resolve_environment_network_and_app_container() -> None:
    preflight = (ROOT / "deploy/server/preflight.sh").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    deploy = (ROOT / "deploy/server/deploy.sh").read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    assert "field LABEL_SYSTEM_NETWORK_NAME" in preflight
    assert "field LABEL_SYSTEM_NETWORK_SUBNET" in preflight
    assert "field LABEL_SYSTEM_NETWORK_GATEWAY" in preflight
    assert "docker network inspect 3d66-label-system" not in preflight
    assert "docker exec -w /app/backend 3d66-label-system-server" not in deploy
    prepare_test = (
        ROOT / "deploy/server/prepare-test-environment.sh"
    ).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert (
        'runtime_value "$test_env" MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE'
        in prepare_test
    )
    assert 'runtime_value "$test_env" LABEL_SYSTEM_NETWORK_SUBNET' in prepare_test
    assert 'runtime_value "$test_env" BACKUP_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE' in prepare_test
    assert 'ufw allow from "$test_network_subnet"' in prepare_test
    assert 'chown 10001:10001 "$service_secret"' in prepare_test
    assert 'test_compose=(docker compose --env-file "$test_env"' in prepare_test
    assert '"${test_compose[@]}" --profile migration run --rm' in prepare_test
    assert deploy.count("GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0=safe.directory") == 2
    assert 'git -C "$repo_root" rev-parse HEAD' in deploy
    assert 'git -C "$repo_root" status' in deploy


def test_environment_isolation_checker_rejects_shared_runtime_identity(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    fields = {
        "COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME": "production-project",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV": "production",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_CONTAINER_NAME": "production-container",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_NETWORK_NAME": "production-network",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_PORT": "8081",
        "MYSQL_PLATFORM_SCHEMA": "production_schema",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT": "/var/lib/production",
        "DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": "/etc/production/app",
        "MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": "/etc/production/migration",
        "BACKUP_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": "/etc/production/backup",
        "API_KEY_MASTER_KEY_SECRET_FILE": "/etc/production/master",
    }
    production = tmp_path / "production.env"
    test = tmp_path / "test.env"
    production.write_text(
        "\n".join(f"{key}={value}" for key, value in fields.items()) + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    test_fields = {
        key: ("test" if key == "LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV" else f"test-{value}")
        for key, value in fields.items()
    }
    test.write_text(
        "\n".join(f"{key}={value}" for key, value in test_fields.items()) + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    script = ROOT / "deploy/server/verify-environment-isolation.sh"
    environment = {**os.environ, "PYTHON_BIN": sys.executable}

    accepted = subprocess.run(
        [str(script), str(production), str(test)],
        cwd=ROOT,
        env=environment,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )
    assert accepted.returncode == 0, accepted.stderr
    assert accepted.stdout.strip() == '{"status":"ready","checks":10}'

    shared = production.read_text(encoding="utf-8").replace(
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV=production",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV=test",
    )
    test.write_text(shared, encoding="utf-8")
    rejected = subprocess.run(
        [str(script), str(production), str(test)],
        cwd=ROOT,
        env=environment,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )
    assert rejected.returncode == 65
    assert "COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME" in rejected.stderr


def test_prometheus_scrape_and_alert_contract_covers_production_bottlenecks() -> None:
    prometheus = yaml.safe_load(
        (ROOT / "deploy" / "server" / "prometheus.yml").read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )
    rule_document = yaml.safe_load(
        (ROOT / "deploy" / "server" / "alerts.yml").read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )

    scrape = next(
        item
        for item in prometheus["scrape_configs"]
        if item["job_name"] == "label-system"
    )
    assert scrape["metrics_path"] == "/metrics"
    assert scrape["authorization"]["credentials_file"] == (
        "/etc/prometheus/secrets/label-system-operations.token"
    )
    assert scrape["static_configs"] == [
        {"targets": ["127.0.0.1:8081"]}
    ]

    rules = {
        item["alert"]: item
        for group in rule_document["groups"]
        for item in group["rules"]
    }
    expected_alerts = {
        "LabelSystemScrapeDown",
        "LabelSystemRuntimeCritical",
        "LabelSystemWorkerMissing",
        "LabelSystemWorkerLoopStale",
        "LabelSystemQueueOldestAgeHigh",
        "LabelSystemDeadLetter",
        "LabelSystemOutboxBacklog",
        "LabelSystemProviderRateLimited",
        "LabelSystemHumanReviewCapacity",
        "LabelSystemMysqlConnectionsHigh",
        "LabelSystemMysqlRedoCheckpointPressure",
        "LabelSystemMysqlRedoWaits",
        "LabelSystemMysqlBufferPoolWaits",
        "LabelSystemMysqlMetricsUnavailable",
        "LabelSystemDataDiskHigh",
        "LabelSystemDataDiskMetricsUnavailable",
        "LabelSystemBackupMissing",
        "LabelSystemBackupStale",
    }
    assert expected_alerts <= set(rules)
    assert "label_system_queue_blocking_dead_lettered" in str(
        rules["LabelSystemDeadLetter"]["expr"]
    )
    assert str(rules["LabelSystemScrapeDown"]["expr"]) == (
        'up{job="label-system"} == 0'
    )
    assert str(rules["LabelSystemRuntimeCritical"]["expr"]) == (
        "label_system_alerts_critical > 0"
    )
    assert "increase(label_system_mysql_innodb_log_waits_total[5m])" in str(
        rules["LabelSystemMysqlRedoWaits"]["expr"]
    )
    assert (
        "label_system_mysql_innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_utilization_ratio"
        in str(rules["LabelSystemMysqlRedoCheckpointPressure"]["expr"])
    )
    serialized = json.dumps(
        {"prometheus": prometheus, "alerts": rule_document},
        ensure_ascii=False,
    ).lower()
    assert "bearer_token:" not in serialized
    assert "password" not in serialized


def test_backup_script_records_success_only_after_manifest_is_written() -> None:

        encoding="utf-8"
    )
    assert "write-backup-status" in script
    assert script.index("write-manifest") < script.index("write-backup-status")


def test_server_runtime_dependencies_are_exactly_locked_without_test_tools() -> None:
    lock_path = ROOT / "backend" / "requirements.lock.txt"
    assert lock_path.is_file()
    lock_text = lock_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    matches = list(re.finditer(
        r"(?m)^([A-Za-z0-9_.-]+)==([^\s\\]+)\s*\\$",
        lock_text,
    ))
    assert matches
    requirements = {
        f"{match.group(1).lower().replace('_', '-')}=={match.group(2)}"
        for match in matches
    }
    expected = {
        "annotated-doc==0.0.5",
        "annotated-types==0.8.0",
        "anyio==4.14.2",
        "certifi==2026.7.22",
        "cffi==2.1.1",
        "click==8.5.0",
        "cryptography==50.0.0",
        "fastapi==0.141.1",
        "greenlet==3.5.5",
        "h11==0.16.0",
        "httpcore==1.0.9",
        "httptools==0.8.0",
        "httpx==0.28.1",
        "idna==3.19",
        "packaging==26.3",
        "pillow==12.3.0",
        "psycopg==3.3.4",
        "psycopg-binary==3.3.4",
        "pycparser==3.0",
        "pydantic==2.13.5",
        "pydantic-core==2.46.5",
        "pymupdf==1.28.2",
        "pymysql==1.2.0",
        "pypdf==6.16.2",
        "pytesseract==0.3.13",
        "python-dotenv==1.2.3",
        "python-multipart==0.0.32",
        "pyyaml==6.0.3",
        "sqlalchemy==2.0.52",
        "starlette==1.6.0",
        "typing-inspection==0.4.4",
        "typing-extensions==4.16.0",
        "uvicorn==0.52.4",
        "uvloop==0.22.1",
        "watchfiles==1.2.0",
        "websockets==17.1",
    }
    assert requirements == expected
    for index, match in enumerate(matches):
        block_end = (
            matches[index + 1].start()
            if index + 1 < len(matches)
            else len(lock_text)
        )
        block = lock_text[match.start():block_end]
        assert re.search(r"--hash=sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}(?:\s|$)", block)

    bootstrap_lock = (
        ROOT / "backend" / "pip-bootstrap.lock.txt"
    ).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert bootstrap_lock == (
        "pip==26.2.1 \\\n"
        "    --hash=sha256:"
        "71138adf1f4ca900cdb7d289c21b7494329f2332b6d85f0e1c42108c0384ed3e\n"
    )

    dockerfile = (ROOT / "Dockerfile").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "pip-bootstrap.lock.txt" in dockerfile
    assert (
        "python -m pip install --no-cache-dir --require-hashes --upgrade"
        in dockerfile
    )
    assert "-r /tmp/pip-bootstrap.lock.txt" in dockerfile
    assert "pip install --no-cache-dir --require-hashes" in dockerfile


def test_server_image_is_digest_pinned_minimal_and_non_root() -> None:
    dockerfile = (ROOT / "Dockerfile").read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    from_lines = [
        line.strip()
        for line in dockerfile.splitlines()
        if line.strip().startswith("FROM ")
    ]
    assert len(from_lines) == 2
    assert all("@sha256:" in line for line in from_lines)
    assert "requirements.lock.txt" in dockerfile
    assert "COPY . /app" not in dockerfile
    assert "backend/app/ /app/backend/app/" in dockerfile
    assert "backend/prompts/ /app/backend/prompts/" in dockerfile
    assert "backend/fixtures/ /app/backend/fixtures/" in dockerfile
    assert "prompts/ /app/prompts/" in dockerfile
    assert "scripts/mysql-integration.py /app/scripts/mysql-integration.py" in dockerfile
    assert "HOME=/home/labelsystem" in dockerfile
    assert dockerfile.count("ARG LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA=dev") == 1
    assert dockerfile.splitlines()[0] == "ARG LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA=dev"
    assert dockerfile.count("ARG LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA\n") == 2
    first_stage_arg = dockerfile.index("ARG LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA\n")
    second_stage_arg = dockerfile.index("ARG LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA\n", first_stage_arg + 1)
    assert dockerfile.index("FROM node:") < first_stage_arg < dockerfile.index("RUN npm run build")
    assert dockerfile.index("FROM python:") < second_stage_arg < dockerfile.index("ENV LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA=$LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA")
    assert "LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA=$LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA" in dockerfile
    assert (
        "LABEL org.opencontainers.image.revision=$LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA"
        in dockerfile
    )
    assert dockerfile.index(
        "RUN pip install --no-cache-dir --require-hashes"
    ) < second_stage_arg
    assert "USER 10001:10001" in dockerfile


def test_server_compose_uses_explicit_data_bind_and_runtime_identity() -> None:
    compose = yaml.safe_load(
        (ROOT / "docker-compose.server.yml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    environment = _example_environment()

    for service_name in ("app", "worker-group"):
        service = compose["services"][service_name]
        assert service["user"] == "10001:10001"
        assert any(
            item == "${LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT:?set LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT}:/data"
            for item in service["volumes"]
        )
        assert "secrets" not in service
    assert "volumes" not in compose
    assert environment["LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT"] == (
        "/var/lib/3d66-label-system/data"
    )


def test_server_compose_uses_stable_private_network_for_host_mysql() -> None:
    compose = yaml.safe_load(
        (ROOT / "docker-compose.server.yml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )

    assert compose["networks"]["label-system"] == {
        "external": True,
        "name": "${LABEL_SYSTEM_NETWORK_NAME:-3d66-label-system}",
    }
    for service_name in ("app", "worker-group", "migration"):
        service = compose["services"][service_name]
        assert service["networks"] == ["label-system"]
        assert service["extra_hosts"] == [

        ]

        (ROOT / "docker-compose.server.yml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )

        service = compose["services"][service_name]

        }


def test_worker_receives_the_same_pool_the_deploy_gate_validates() -> None:
    # server_runtime validates DATABASE_POOL_SIZE from runtime.env, but the
    # preparation threads live in worker-group. A hardcoded worker pool would
    # let a validated phase_threads=4 run against a pool of 2.
    compose = yaml.safe_load(
        (ROOT / "docker-compose.server.yml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    worker_environment = compose["services"]["worker-group"]["environment"]
    app_environment = compose["services"]["app"]["environment"]

    worker_pool = worker_environment["DATABASE_POOL_SIZE"]
    app_pool = app_environment["DATABASE_POOL_SIZE"]
    assert worker_pool.startswith("${DATABASE_POOL_SIZE:-")
    assert app_pool.startswith("${DATABASE_POOL_SIZE:-")
    # Defaults keep today's behaviour for anyone who does not set the variable.
    assert _default_from_interpolation(worker_pool) == 2
    assert _default_from_interpolation(app_pool) == 4

    # The capacity profile is a deploy-gate token; the worker must still be able
    # to report which profile it is running under, and split admission must be
    # explicit rather than an implicit default divergence from the gate.
    assert worker_environment["WORKER_RUNTIME_CAPACITY_PROFILE"] == (
        "${WORKER_RUNTIME_CAPACITY_PROFILE:-}"
    )
    assert worker_environment["WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION"] == (
        "${WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION:-0}"
    )

===== FILE backend/tests/test_server_image_archive.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import io
import json
import tarfile
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest

from app.server_image_archive import (
    ImageArchiveValidationError,
    validate_docker_save_archive,
)


EXPECTED_TAG = "3d66-label-system:server-bbbbbbbbbbbb"


def _json_bytes(value: Any) -> bytes:
    return json.dumps(value, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")


def _digest(payload: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()


def _add_file(archive: tarfile.TarFile, name: str, payload: bytes) -> None:
    info = tarfile.TarInfo(name)
    info.size = len(payload)
    archive.addfile(info, io.BytesIO(payload))


def _write_containerd_archive(
    path: Path,
    *,
    legacy_manifest: Any | None = None,
    index_name: str | None = None,
    extra_top_descriptor: bool = False,
    alias_manifest: bool = False,
    link_member: bool = False,
    disguised_attestation: bool = False,
    valid_attestation: bool = False,
    top_index_alias: bool = False,
    duplicate_top_index_key: bool = False,
) -> None:
    config = b'{"architecture":"amd64","os":"linux"}'
    layer = b"layer payload"
    config_path = f"blobs/sha256/{_digest(config)}"
    layer_path = f"blobs/sha256/{_digest(layer)}"
    image_manifest = _json_bytes(
        {
            "schemaVersion": 2,
            "mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.manifest.v1+json",
            "config": {
                "mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.config.v1+json",
                "digest": f"sha256:{_digest(config)}",
                "size": len(config),
            },
            "layers": [
                {
                    "mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.layer.v1.tar+gzip",
                    "digest": f"sha256:{_digest(layer)}",
                    "size": len(layer),
                }
            ],
        }
    )
    image_manifest_digest = _digest(image_manifest)
    runnable_descriptor = {
        "mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.manifest.v1+json",
        "digest": f"sha256:{image_manifest_digest}",
        "size": len(image_manifest),
        "platform": {"architecture": "amd64", "os": "linux"},
    }
    nested_descriptors = [runnable_descriptor]
    attestation_config = b'{"architecture":"unknown","os":"unknown"}'
    attestation_layer = b'{"predicateType":"https://slsa.dev/provenance/v1"}'
    attestation_manifest = _json_bytes(
        {
            "schemaVersion": 2,
            "mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.manifest.v1+json",
            "config": {
                "mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.config.v1+json",
                "digest": f"sha256:{_digest(attestation_config)}",
                "size": len(attestation_config),
            },
            "layers": [
                {
                    "mediaType": "application/vnd.in-toto+json",
                    "digest": f"sha256:{_digest(attestation_layer)}",
                    "size": len(attestation_layer),
                    "annotations": {
                        "in-toto.io/predicate-type": (
                            "https://slsa.dev/provenance/v1"
                        )
                    },
                }
            ],
        }
    )
    attestation_manifest_digest = _digest(attestation_manifest)
    if valid_attestation:
        nested_descriptors.append(
            {
                "mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.manifest.v1+json",
                "digest": f"sha256:{attestation_manifest_digest}",
                "size": len(attestation_manifest),
                "annotations": {
                    "vnd.docker.reference.digest": (
                        f"sha256:{image_manifest_digest}"
                    ),
                    "vnd.docker.reference.type": "attestation-manifest",
                },
                "platform": {"architecture": "unknown", "os": "unknown"},
            }
        )
    disguised_document = _json_bytes(
        {
            "schemaVersion": 2,
            "mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.index.v1+json",
            "manifests": [],
        }
    )
    disguised_digest = _digest(disguised_document)
    if disguised_attestation:
        nested_descriptors.append(
            {
                "mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.index.v1+json",
                "digest": f"sha256:{disguised_digest}",
                "size": len(disguised_document),
                "annotations": {
                    "vnd.docker.reference.digest": (
                        f"sha256:{image_manifest_digest}"
                    ),
                    "vnd.docker.reference.type": "attestation-manifest",
                },
                "platform": {"architecture": "unknown", "os": "unknown"},
            }
        )
    nested_index = _json_bytes(
        {
            "schemaVersion": 2,
            "mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.index.v1+json",
            "manifests": nested_descriptors,
        }
    )
    nested_index_digest = _digest(nested_index)
    image_name = index_name or f"docker.io/library/{EXPECTED_TAG}"
    top_descriptor = {
        "mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.index.v1+json",
        "digest": f"sha256:{nested_index_digest}",
        "size": len(nested_index),
        "annotations": {
            "io.containerd.image.name": image_name,
            "org.opencontainers.image.ref.name": EXPECTED_TAG.rsplit(":", 1)[1],
        },
    }
    top_descriptors = [top_descriptor]
    if extra_top_descriptor:
        top_descriptors.append(
            {
                **top_descriptor,
                "annotations": {
                    "io.containerd.image.name": (
                        "docker.io/library/3d66-label-system:previous-running"
                    ),
                    "org.opencontainers.image.ref.name": "previous-running",
                },
            }
        )
    index_document = {
        "schemaVersion": 2,
        "mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.index.v1+json",
        "manifests": top_descriptors,
    }
    index = _json_bytes(index_document)
    poisoned_descriptor = {
        **top_descriptor,
        "annotations": {
            "io.containerd.image.name": (
                "docker.io/library/3d66-label-system:previous-running"
            ),
            "org.opencontainers.image.ref.name": "previous-running",
        },
    }
    if top_index_alias:
        index = (
            b'{"schemaVersion":2,"mediaType":'
            + _json_bytes("application/vnd.oci.image.index.v1+json")
            + b',"manifests":'
            + _json_bytes([top_descriptor])
            + b',"Manifests":'
            + _json_bytes([top_descriptor, poisoned_descriptor])
            + b"}"
        )
    if duplicate_top_index_key:
        index = (
            b'{"schemaVersion":2,"mediaType":'
            + _json_bytes("application/vnd.oci.image.index.v1+json")
            + b',"manifests":'
            + _json_bytes([top_descriptor, poisoned_descriptor])
            + b',"manifests":'
            + _json_bytes([top_descriptor])
            + b"}"
        )
    if legacy_manifest is None:
        legacy_manifest = [
            {
                "Config": config_path,
                "RepoTags": [EXPECTED_TAG],
                "Layers": [layer_path],
            }
        ]
    legacy_payload = _json_bytes(legacy_manifest)

    with tarfile.open(path, "w") as archive:
        _add_file(archive, "manifest.json", legacy_payload)
        _add_file(archive, "index.json", index)
        _add_file(archive, "oci-layout", b'{"imageLayoutVersion":"1.0.0"}')
        _add_file(archive, config_path, config)
        _add_file(archive, layer_path, layer)
        _add_file(
            archive,
            f"blobs/sha256/{image_manifest_digest}",
            image_manifest,
        )
        _add_file(
            archive,
            f"blobs/sha256/{nested_index_digest}",
            nested_index,
        )
        if disguised_attestation:
            _add_file(
                archive,
                f"blobs/sha256/{disguised_digest}",
                disguised_document,
            )
        if valid_attestation:
            _add_file(
                archive,
                f"blobs/sha256/{attestation_manifest_digest}",
                attestation_manifest,
            )
            _add_file(
                archive,
                f"blobs/sha256/{_digest(attestation_config)}",
                attestation_config,
            )
            _add_file(
                archive,
                f"blobs/sha256/{_digest(attestation_layer)}",
                attestation_layer,
            )
        if alias_manifest:
            _add_file(
                archive,
                "./manifest.json",
                _json_bytes(
                    [
                        {
                            "Config": config_path,
                            "RepoTags": [
                                "3d66-label-system:previous-running"
                            ],
                            "Layers": [layer_path],
                        }
                    ]
                ),
            )
        if link_member:
            info = tarfile.TarInfo("legacy-manifest-link")
            info.type = tarfile.SYMTYPE
            info.linkname = "manifest.json"
            archive.addfile(info)


def test_validate_docker_save_archive_accepts_docker29_containerd_shape(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    archive = tmp_path / "image.tar"
    _write_containerd_archive(archive)

    identity = validate_docker_save_archive(archive, expected_tag=EXPECTED_TAG)

    assert identity.repo_tag == EXPECTED_TAG
    assert identity.config.startswith("blobs/sha256/")
    assert identity.layer_count == 1


def test_validate_docker_save_archive_accepts_bound_attestation(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    archive = tmp_path / "image.tar"
    _write_containerd_archive(archive, valid_attestation=True)

    identity = validate_docker_save_archive(archive, expected_tag=EXPECTED_TAG)

    assert identity.repo_tag == EXPECTED_TAG


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "legacy_manifest",
    [
        [
            {
                "Config": "config.json",
                "RepoTags": [
                    EXPECTED_TAG,
                    "3d66-label-system:previous-running",
                ],
                "Layers": ["layer/layer.tar"],
            }
        ],
        [
            {
                "Config": "config.json",
                "RepoTags": [EXPECTED_TAG],
                "Layers": ["layer/layer.tar"],
            },
            {
                "Config": "other.json",
                "RepoTags": ["3d66-label-system:other"],
                "Layers": ["other/layer.tar"],
            },
        ],
        [
            {
                "Config": "config.json",
                "RepoTags": ["3d66-label-system:other"],
                "Layers": ["layer/layer.tar"],
            }
        ],
        [],
        {"RepoTags": [EXPECTED_TAG]},
    ],
)
def test_validate_docker_save_archive_rejects_bad_legacy_mapping(
    tmp_path: Path,
    legacy_manifest: Any,
) -> None:
    archive = tmp_path / "image.tar"

    with pytest.raises(ImageArchiveValidationError):
        validate_docker_save_archive(archive, expected_tag=EXPECTED_TAG)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_server_image_archive_layersources.py =====
"""归档校验必须接受标准 `docker save` 的可选 LayerSources 字段。

服务器本机(新版 docker)构建导出的镜像归档,其 manifest.json 会带 LayerSources
(层来源映射,与镜像身份无关、加载器忽略)。此前 allowed 仅覆盖旧 docker 输出,
导致本机构建的归档被误拒。放宽后仍必须拒绝任何真正未知的字段。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.server_image_archive import (
    ImageArchiveValidationError,
    _legacy_identity,
)


_BLOB = "blobs/sha256/" + "a" * 64
_TAG = "3d66-label-system:server-f5ade2202bb2"


def _manifest(**extra) -> list:
    entry = {"Config": _BLOB, "RepoTags": [_TAG], "Layers": [_BLOB]}
    entry.update(extra)
    return [entry]


def test_accepts_layersources_field() -> None:
    identity = _legacy_identity(
        _manifest(LayerSources={_BLOB: {"mediaType": "application/vnd.oci.image.layer.v1.tar"}}),
        _TAG,
    )
    assert identity.repo_tag == _TAG
    assert identity.layer_count == 1


def test_accepts_manifest_without_layersources() -> None:
    identity = _legacy_identity(_manifest(), _TAG)
    assert identity.repo_tag == _TAG


def test_still_rejects_truly_unknown_key() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ImageArchiveValidationError):
        _legacy_identity(_manifest(Bogus=1), _TAG)


def test_still_requires_expected_tag() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ImageArchiveValidationError):
        _legacy_identity(_manifest(), "3d66-label-system:server-other")

===== FILE backend/tests/test_server_production_acceptance.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest


def _acceptance_script() -> Any:
    script = (
        Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
        / "scripts"
        / "server-production-acceptance.py"
    )
    specification = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
        "labelsystem_server_production_acceptance_script",
        script,
    )
    assert specification is not None
    assert specification.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(specification)
    specification.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def _passing_evidence(*, workers: int = 33) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "schema": "server-production-evidence-v1",
        "build": {
            "expected_sha": "a" * 40,
            "backend_sha": "a" * 40,
            "frontend_sha": "a" * 40,
            "oci_revision": "a" * 40,
        },
        "health": {
            "ready": True,
            "database_product": "Oracle MySQL",
            "database_version": "8.4.6",
            "active_workers": workers,
            "expected_workers": workers,
        },
        "runtime": {
            "proof_kind": "observed_end_to_end",
            "end_to_end": True,
            "completed_assets": 500,
            "observed_seconds": 300,
            "queue_depth_start": 600,
            "queue_depth_end": 100,
            "error_count": 0,
        },
        "provider": {
            "confirmed_slots": 8,
            "model_calls": 900,
            "http_429": 0,
            "timeouts": 0,
            "other_errors": 0,
            "actual_cost_micros": 8_000_000,
        },
        "recovery": {
            "backup_manifest_sha256": "b" * 64,
            "restore_completed": True,
            "reconciliation_accepted": True,
            "restored_tables": 119,
            "restore_seconds": 20,
        },
        "system": {
            "mysql_connection_utilization_ratio": 0.4,
            "cpu_utilization_ratio": 0.5,
            "memory_utilization_ratio": 0.6,
            "worker_oom_count": 0,
        },
    }


def test_database_only_throughput_cannot_prove_end_to_end_capacity() -> None:
    module = _acceptance_script()
    evidence = _passing_evidence()
    evidence["runtime"].update(
        {
            "proof_kind": "database_queue_only",
            "end_to_end": False,
            "completed_assets_per_second": 1000,
        }
    )

    receipt = module.evaluate_acceptance(evidence)

    assert receipt["decision"]["accepted"] is False
    assert receipt["decision"]["capacity_status"] == "not_proven"
    assert "end_to_end_capacity_not_proven" in receipt["decision"]["blockers"]


def test_missing_restore_evidence_blocks_acceptance() -> None:
    module = _acceptance_script()
    evidence = _passing_evidence()
    evidence["recovery"] = {}

    receipt = module.evaluate_acceptance(evidence)

    assert receipt["decision"]["accepted"] is False
    assert "backup_restore_not_proven" in receipt["decision"]["blockers"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("field", "value", "blocker"),
    [
        ("http_429", 11, "provider_429_rate_exceeded"),
        ("timeouts", 11, "provider_timeout_rate_exceeded"),
    ],
)
def test_provider_error_thresholds_block_tier_promotion(
    field: str,
    value: int,
    blocker: str,
) -> None:
    module = _acceptance_script()
    evidence = _passing_evidence()
    evidence["provider"][field] = value

    receipt = module.evaluate_acceptance(evidence)

    assert receipt["decision"]["accepted"] is False
    assert receipt["decision"]["promote_next_tier"] is False
    assert blocker in receipt["decision"]["blockers"]


def test_other_provider_errors_block_acceptance() -> None:
    module = _acceptance_script()
    evidence = _passing_evidence()
    evidence["provider"]["other_errors"] = 1

    receipt = module.evaluate_acceptance(evidence)

    assert receipt["decision"]["accepted"] is False
    assert "provider_errors_present" in receipt["decision"]["blockers"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("field", "value", "blocker"),
    [
        ("model_calls", 10_001, "paid_call_limit_exceeded"),
        ("actual_cost_micros", 1_000_000_001, "paid_cost_limit_exceeded"),
    ],
)
def test_paid_acceptance_ceiling_stops_the_run(
    field: str,
    value: int,
    blocker: str,
) -> None:
    module = _acceptance_script()
    evidence = _passing_evidence()
    evidence["provider"][field] = value

    receipt = module.evaluate_acceptance(evidence)

    assert receipt["decision"]["accepted"] is False
    assert blocker in receipt["decision"]["blockers"]


def test_theoretical_extrapolation_is_labelled_not_proven() -> None:
    module = _acceptance_script()
    evidence = _passing_evidence()
    evidence["runtime"]["proof_kind"] = "theoretical_extrapolation"

    receipt = module.evaluate_acceptance(evidence)

    assert receipt["decision"]["capacity_status"] == "not_proven"
    assert receipt["decision"]["accepted"] is False


def test_observed_end_to_end_evidence_can_pass_first_tier() -> None:
    module = _acceptance_script()

    receipt = module.evaluate_acceptance(_passing_evidence())

    assert receipt["metrics"]["completed_assets_per_second"] == pytest.approx(
        500 / 300
    )
    assert receipt["metrics"]["queue_slope_assets_per_second"] == pytest.approx(
        -500 / 300
    )
    assert receipt["decision"] == {
        "accepted": True,
        "blockers": [],
        "capacity_status": "proven",
        "promote_next_tier": True,
        "sustainable_worker_tier": 33,
    }
    serialized = json.dumps(receipt, ensure_ascii=False)
    assert "mysql+pymysql://" not in serialized
    assert "password" not in serialized.lower()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_server_release_scripts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import os
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import pytest


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def _line_position(lines: list[str], prefix: str) -> int:
    return next(index for index, line in enumerate(lines) if line.startswith(prefix))


def _fake_executable(path: Path, label: str) -> None:
    git_output = (
        "if [[ \"${1:-}\" == '-C' && \"$*\" == *'rev-parse HEAD'* ]]; then\n"
        "  printf '%s\\n' \"${RELEASE_SOURCE_SHA:-}\"\n"
        "elif [[ \"${1:-}\" == '-C' && \"$*\" == *'status --porcelain'* ]]; then\n"
        "  printf '%s' \"${DIRTY_WORKTREE_OUTPUT:-}\"\n"
        "fi\n"
        if label == "git"
        else ""
    )
    container_inspect_output = (
        "if [[ \"${1:-}\" == 'inspect' "
        "&& \"$*\" == *'--format'* ]]; then\n"
        "  if [[ \"$*\" == *'{{.Image}}'* ]]; then\n"
        "    printf '%s\\n' \"${PREVIOUS_IMAGE_ID:-}\"\n"
        "  else\n"
        "    printf '%s\\n' \"${PREVIOUS_IMAGE_REFERENCE:-}\"\n"
        "  fi\n"
        "fi\n"
        if label == "docker"
        else ""
    )
    inspect_output = (
        "if [[ \"${1:-}\" == 'image' && \"${2:-}\" == 'inspect' "
        "&& \"$*\" == *'--format'* ]]; then\n"
        "  if [[ \"${3:-}\" == \"3d66-label-system:${RELEASE_IMAGE_TAG:-}\" ]]; then\n"
        "    if [[ -e \"$RELEASE_TEST_LOG.image-loaded\" ]]; then\n"
        "      if [[ \"${LOADED_IMAGE_MISSING:-}\" == '1' ]]; then exit 1; fi\n"
        "      printf '%s\\n' \"${LOADED_IMAGE_CONTRACT:-}\"\n"
        "    else\n"
        "      if [[ \"${TARGET_IMAGE_EXISTS:-}\" != '1' ]]; then exit 1; fi\n"
        "      printf '%s\\n' \"${TARGET_IMAGE_REVISION:-}\"\n"
        "    fi\n"
        "  elif [[ \"$*\" == *'{{.Id}}'* ]]; then\n"
        "    printf '%s\\n' \"${PREVIOUS_IMAGE_ID:-}\"\n"
        "  else\n"
        "    printf '%s\\n' \"${IMAGE_REVISION:-}\"\n"
        "  fi\n"
        "fi\n"
        if label == "docker"
        else ""
    )
    load_output = (
        "if [[ \"${1:-}\" == 'image' && \"${2:-}\" == 'load' ]]; then\n"
        "  : > \"$RELEASE_TEST_LOG.image-loaded\"\n"
        "fi\n"
        if label == "docker"
        else ""
    )
    failure_output = (
        "if [[ -n \"${FAIL_DOCKER_MATCH:-}\" && "
        "\"$*\" == *\"$FAIL_DOCKER_MATCH\"* ]]; then\n"
        "  if [[ \"${FAIL_DOCKER_MATCH_ONCE:-}\" != '1' ]]; then exit 23; fi\n"
        "  if [[ ! -e \"$RELEASE_TEST_LOG.fail-once\" ]]; then\n"
        "    : > \"$RELEASE_TEST_LOG.fail-once\"\n"
        "    exit 23\n"
        "  fi\n"
        "fi\n"
        if label == "docker"
        else ""
    )
    running_services_output = (
        "if [[ \"$*\" == *'ps -q --status running app worker-group'* ]]; then\n"
        "  printf '%s\\n' \"${RUNNING_SERVICE_IDS:-}\"\n"
        "fi\n"
        if label == "docker"
        else ""
    )
    network_inspect_output = (
        "if [[ \"${1:-}\" == 'network' && \"${2:-}\" == 'inspect' ]]; then\n"
        "  if [[ \"${FAIL_DOCKER_NETWORK_INSPECT:-}\" == '1' ]]; then exit 28; fi\n"
        "  printf '%s\\n' \"${DOCKER_NETWORK_CONTRACT:-bridge|172.30.66.0/24|172.30.66.1}\"\n"
        "fi\n"
        if label == "docker"
        else ""
    )
    runtime_value_output = (
        "if [[ \"$*\" == *'app.server_backup runtime-value'* "
        "&& \"$*\" == *'LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT'* ]]; then\n"
        "  printf '%s\\n' \"$RELEASE_DATA_ROOT\"\n"
        "elif [[ \"$*\" == *'app.server_backup runtime-value'* "
        "&& \"$*\" == *'LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA'* ]]; then\n"
        "  printf '%s\\n' \"$RELEASE_SOURCE_SHA\"\n"
        "elif [[ \"$*\" == *'app.server_backup runtime-value'* "
        "&& \"$*\" == *'LABEL_SYSTEM_IMAGE_TAG'* ]]; then\n"
        "  printf '%s\\n' \"$RELEASE_IMAGE_TAG\"\n"
        "fi\n"
        if label == "python"
        else ""
    )
    backup_failure_output = (
        "if [[ \"${FAIL_BACKUP:-}\" == '1' ]]; then exit 24; fi\n"
        if label == "backup"
        else ""
    )
    python_failure_output = (
        "if [[ -n \"${FAIL_PYTHON_MATCH:-}\" && "
        "\"$*\" == *\"$FAIL_PYTHON_MATCH\"* ]]; then\n"
        "  exit 29\n"
        "fi\n"
        if label == "python"
        else ""
    )
    schema_probe_output = (
        "if [[ \"$*\" == *'select max(version) from schema_migrations'* ]]; then\n"
        "  if [[ \"${FAIL_SCHEMA_PROBE:-}\" == '1' ]]; then exit 27; fi\n"
        "  printf '%s\\n' \"${CURRENT_SCHEMA:-111}\"\n"
        "  if [[ \"${FAIL_SCHEMA_PROBE:-}\" == '2' ]]; then exit 27; fi\n"
        "elif [[ \"$*\" == *'MIGRATIONS[-1].version'* ]]; then\n"
        "  printf '%s\\n' \"${PREVIOUS_SCHEMA:-111}\"\n"
        "fi\n"
        if label == "docker"
        else ""
    )
    path.write_text(
        "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n"
        "set -euo pipefail\n"
        f"printf '%s %s\\n' '{label}' \"$*\" >> \"$RELEASE_TEST_LOG\"\n"
        + git_output
        + container_inspect_output
        + inspect_output
        + load_output
        + failure_output
        + schema_probe_output
        + running_services_output
        + network_inspect_output
        + runtime_value_output
        + backup_failure_output
        + python_failure_output,
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    path.chmod(0o755)


def _release_fixture(tmp_path: Path) -> tuple[Path, dict[str, str], Path]:
    binary_dir = tmp_path / "bin"
    binary_dir.mkdir()
    log = tmp_path / "release.log"
    docker = binary_dir / "docker"
    python = binary_dir / "python-fixture"
    backup = binary_dir / "backup-fixture"
    git = binary_dir / "git"
    _fake_executable(docker, "docker")
    _fake_executable(python, "python")
    _fake_executable(backup, "backup")
    _fake_executable(git, "git")
    data_root = tmp_path / "platform-data"
    data_root.mkdir()
    environment_file = tmp_path / "runtime env"
    environment_file.write_text("SAFE_ONLY=1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    environment = {
        **os.environ,
        "PATH": f"{binary_dir}:{os.environ.get('PATH', '')}",
        "PYTHON_BIN": str(python),
        "BACKUP_BIN": str(backup),
        "RELEASE_TEST_LOG": str(log),
        "RELEASE_DATA_ROOT": str(data_root),
        "IMAGE_REVISION": "b" * 40,
        "RELEASE_SOURCE_SHA": "b" * 40,
        "RELEASE_IMAGE_TAG": f"server-{'b' * 12}",
        "PREVIOUS_IMAGE_REFERENCE": "3d66-label-system:previous-running",
        "PREVIOUS_IMAGE_ID": f"sha256:{'e' * 64}",
        "LOADED_IMAGE_CONTRACT": (
            f"sha256:{'c' * 64}|linux|amd64|{'b' * 40}"
        ),
    }
    return environment_file, environment, log


def _run(
    script: str,
    *arguments: str,
    environment: dict[str, str],
) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    return subprocess.run(
        [str(ROOT / "deploy" / "server" / script), *arguments],
        cwd=ROOT,
        env=environment,
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )


def test_initial_release_orders_validation_migration_start_and_verify(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment_file, environment, log = _release_fixture(tmp_path)
    secret_marker = "must-not-be-printed"
    (tmp_path / "unread-secret").write_text(secret_marker, encoding="utf-8")

    completed = _run(
        "deploy.sh",
        str(environment_file),
        "--initial-import",
        str(tmp_path / "initial-backup"),
        environment=environment,
    )

    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stderr
    lines = log.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    joined = "\n".join(lines)
    assert lines[0].startswith("python -m app.server_runtime validate-env")
    assert "--database-mode initial-import" in lines[0]
    assert "config --quiet" in joined
    assert "app.server_backup provision-master-key" in joined
    assert joined.index("config --quiet") < joined.index("build")
    assert joined.index("provision-master-key") < joined.index("build")
    assert joined.index("prepare-import-target") < joined.index("import-snapshot")
    assert joined.index("import-snapshot") < joined.index("reconcile-snapshot")
    assert joined.index("reconcile-snapshot") < joined.index("up -d")
    assert next(
        index for index, line in enumerate(lines) if "reconcile-snapshot" in line
    ) < _line_position(lines, "backup ")
    assert _line_position(lines, "backup ") < next(
        index for index, line in enumerate(lines) if "up -d" in line
    )
    assert joined.index("up -d") < joined.index("verify-health")
    assert joined.index("verify-health") < joined.index("verify-operations")
    assert "--checks 2" in joined
    assert "--interval 35" in joined
    assert "--startup-timeout 180" in joined
    assert "--startup-interval 5" in joined
    assert secret_marker not in joined + completed.stdout + completed.stderr


def test_deploy_requires_an_explicit_database_mode(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    environment_file, environment, log = _release_fixture(tmp_path)

    completed = _run(
        "deploy.sh",
        str(environment_file),
        environment=environment,
    )

    assert completed.returncode != 0
    assert not log.exists()

def test_prebuilt_release_verifies_archive_and_image_before_quiescing(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment_file, environment, log = _release_fixture(tmp_path)
    environment["RUNNING_SERVICE_IDS"] = "old-app old-worker"
    archive = tmp_path / "reviewed-image.tar"
    archive.write_bytes(b"reviewed prebuilt image")
    archive_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(archive.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

    completed = _run(
        "deploy.sh",
        str(environment_file),
        "--existing-schema",
        str(tmp_path / "prebuilt-backup"),
        "--prebuilt-image",
        str(archive),
        "--prebuilt-sha256",
        archive_sha256,
        environment=environment,
    )

    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stderr
    lines = log.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    joined = "\n".join(lines)
    loaded = next(index for index, line in enumerate(lines) if "image load" in line)
    inspected = next(
        index
        for index, line in enumerate(lines)
        if index > loaded and "image inspect" in line
    )
    stopped = next(
        index for index, line in enumerate(lines) if "stop app worker-group" in line
    )
    assert loaded < inspected < stopped
    assert " build" not in joined
    assert "up -d --no-build" in joined


def test_prebuilt_release_rejects_archive_checksum_mismatch_before_loading(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment_file, environment, log = _release_fixture(tmp_path)
    environment["RUNNING_SERVICE_IDS"] = "old-app old-worker"

    joined = log.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if log.exists() else ""
    assert "image load" not in joined
    assert " build" not in joined
    assert "stop app worker-group" not in joined
    assert not any(line.startswith("backup ") for line in joined.splitlines())


def test_prebuilt_release_rejects_wrong_revision_before_quiescing(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment_file, environment, log = _release_fixture(tmp_path)
    environment["RUNNING_SERVICE_IDS"] = "old-app old-worker"
    environment["LOADED_IMAGE_CONTRACT"] = (
        f"sha256:{'c' * 64}|linux|amd64|{'d' * 40}"
    )
    archive = tmp_path / "wrong-revision.tar"
    archive.write_bytes(b"wrong revision")
    archive_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(archive.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

    completed = _run(
        "deploy.sh",
        str(environment_file),
        "--existing-schema",
        str(tmp_path / "wrong-revision-backup"),
        "--prebuilt-image",
        str(archive),
        "--prebuilt-sha256",
        archive_sha256,
        environment=environment,
    )

    assert completed.returncode != 0
    joined = log.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "image load" in joined
    assert "stop app worker-group" not in joined
    assert not any(line.startswith("backup ") for line in joined.splitlines())

    assert not any(line.startswith("backup ") for line in joined.splitlines())

def test_prebuilt_release_freezes_running_image_id_before_loading(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment_file, environment, log = _release_fixture(tmp_path)
    environment["RUNNING_SERVICE_IDS"] = "old-app old-worker"
    archive = tmp_path / "reviewed-image.tar"
    archive.write_bytes(b"reviewed prebuilt image")
    archive_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(archive.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

    completed = _run(
        "deploy.sh",
        str(environment_file),
        "--existing-schema",
        str(tmp_path / "image-id-backup"),
        "--prebuilt-image",
        str(archive),
        "--prebuilt-sha256",
        archive_sha256,
        environment=environment,
    )

    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stderr
    lines = log.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    loaded = next(index for index, line in enumerate(lines) if "image load" in line)
    frozen = next(
        index
        for index, line in enumerate(lines)
        if "inspect old-app --format {{.Image}}" in line
    )
    assert frozen < loaded
    assert str(archive) not in lines[loaded]
    assert "/tmp/3d66-label-system-prebuilt." in lines[loaded]

def test_deploy_rejects_relative_or_existing_backup_target(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment_file, environment, log = _release_fixture(tmp_path)
    existing = tmp_path / "existing"
    existing.mkdir()

    relative = _run(
        "deploy.sh",
        str(environment_file),
        "--existing-schema",
        "relative-backup",
        environment=environment,
    )
    occupied = _run(
        "deploy.sh",
        str(environment_file),
        "--existing-schema",
        str(existing),
        environment=environment,
    )

    assert relative.returncode != 0
    assert occupied.returncode != 0
    assert not log.exists()

def test_preserve_failure_mode_never_stops_new_services_for_automatic_rollback(tmp_path):
    environment_file, environment, log = _release_fixture(tmp_path)
    environment.update({
        'RUNNING_SERVICE_IDS':'old-app old-worker',
        'FAIL_DOCKER_MATCH':'up -d --no-build', 'FAIL_DOCKER_MATCH_ONCE':'1',
        'CURRENT_SCHEMA':'113', 'PREVIOUS_SCHEMA':'113',
        'LABEL_SYSTEM_RELEASE_FAILURE_MODE':'preserve',
    })
    completed = _run('deploy.sh', str(environment_file), '--existing-schema',
        str(tmp_path/'backup'), environment=environment)
    assert completed.returncode == 23
    joined = log.read_text()
    assert joined.count('up -d --no-build') == 1
    assert ' down' not in joined
    assert 'start app worker-group' not in joined
    assert 'preserve_current_services' in completed.stderr

    environment_file, environment, log = _release_fixture(tmp_path)
    environment['LABEL_SYSTEM_RELEASE_FAILURE_MODE'] = 'typo'
    completed = _run('deploy.sh', str(environment_file), '--existing-schema',
        str(tmp_path/'backup'), environment=environment)
    assert completed.returncode == 64
    assert not log.exists()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("failure", ["upgrade-schema", "up -d --no-build"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("current,previous,probe_fails,blocked", [
    ("112", "111", False, True),
    ("111", "111", False, False),
    ("111", "111", True, True),
    ("111", "111", 2, True),
    ("0", "111", False, True),
    ("unknown", "111", False, True),
    ("111", "unknown", False, True),
])
def test_release_failure_restores_old_services_only_with_proven_compatible_schema(
    tmp_path, failure, current, previous, probe_fails, blocked,
):
    environment_file, environment, log = _release_fixture(tmp_path)
    environment.update({
        "RUNNING_SERVICE_IDS": "old-app old-worker",
        "FAIL_DOCKER_MATCH": failure, "FAIL_DOCKER_MATCH_ONCE": "1",
        "CURRENT_SCHEMA": current, "PREVIOUS_SCHEMA": previous,
        "FAIL_SCHEMA_PROBE": str(int(probe_fails)),
    })
    completed = _run("deploy.sh", str(environment_file), "--existing-schema",
                     str(tmp_path / "backup"), environment=environment)
    assert completed.returncode == 23
    lines = log.read_text().splitlines()
    joined = "\n".join(lines)
    probes = [line for line in lines if "select max(version) from schema_migrations" in line]
    assert len(probes) == 1
    # Read via the exact Compose migration service, even when the app is stopped.
    assert "--env-file " + str(environment_file) in probes[0]
    assert "--profile migration run" in probes[0]
    assert "docker exec" not in probes[0]
    assert "--entrypoint python migration" in probes[0]
    if blocked:
        assert "start app worker-group" not in joined
        assert "image tag" not in joined
        assert " down" not in joined
        assert joined.count("up -d --no-build") == (failure == "up -d --no-build")
        assert "禁止恢复旧服务" in completed.stderr
        assert "前向恢复" in completed.stderr
    elif failure == "upgrade-schema":
        assert "start app worker-group" in joined
        assert "up -d --no-build" not in joined
    else:
        assert joined.count("up -d --no-build") == 2


def test_existing_release_quiesces_running_services_before_backup(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment_file, environment, log = _release_fixture(tmp_path)
    environment["RUNNING_SERVICE_IDS"] = "old-app old-worker"

    completed = _run(
        "deploy.sh",
        str(environment_file),
        "--existing-schema",
        str(tmp_path / "quiesced-backup"),
        environment=environment,
    )

    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stderr
    lines = log.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    probe = next(
        index
        for index, line in enumerate(lines)
        if "ps -q --status running app worker-group" in line
    )
    stopped = next(
        index
        for index, line in enumerate(lines)
        if "stop app worker-group" in line
    )
    backed_up = _line_position(lines, "backup ")
    upgraded = next(
        index for index, line in enumerate(lines) if "upgrade-schema" in line
    )
    assert probe < stopped < backed_up < upgraded


def test_existing_schema_release_upgrades_without_reimporting_snapshot(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment_file, environment, log = _release_fixture(tmp_path)

    completed = _run(
        "deploy.sh",
        str(environment_file),
        "--existing-schema",
        str(tmp_path / "existing-backup"),
        environment=environment,
    )

    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stderr
    lines = log.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    joined = "\n".join(lines)
    assert "--database-mode existing-schema" in joined
    assert "config --quiet" in joined
    assert joined.index("config --quiet") < joined.index("build")
    assert "upgrade-schema" in joined
    assert "provision-master-key" not in joined
    assert "prepare-import-target" not in joined
    assert "import-snapshot" not in joined
    assert "reconcile-snapshot" not in joined
    assert _line_position(lines, "backup ") < next(
        index for index, line in enumerate(lines) if "upgrade-schema" in line
    )
    assert joined.index("upgrade-schema") < joined.index("up -d")
    assert joined.index("up -d") < joined.index("verify-health")

def test_server_shell_entrypoints_have_strict_mode() -> None:
    for name in ("preflight.sh", "deploy.sh", "verify.sh", "rollback.sh"):
        content = (ROOT / "deploy" / "server" / name).read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
        assert "set -euo pipefail" in content

===== FILE backend/tests/test_server_runtime.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import io
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
from urllib.error import HTTPError

import pytest

from app.server_runtime import (
    ServerRuntimeError,
    fetch_health_payload,
    fetch_operational_metrics_text,
    parse_runtime_environment,
    validate_runtime_environment,
    verify_health_payload,
    verify_health_with_startup_wait,
    verify_live_build,
    verify_operational_metrics_text,
)


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def _runtime_environment(tmp_path: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    master_key = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(b"k" * 32).decode("ascii")
    secret_root = tmp_path / "secrets"
    secret_root.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    database_secret = secret_root / "database-url"
    migration_secret = secret_root / "migration-database-url"
    backup_secret = secret_root / "backup-database-url"
    master_key_secret = secret_root / "api-key-master-key"
    database_secret.write_text("placeholder-app-dsn", encoding="utf-8")
    migration_secret.write_text("placeholder-migration-dsn", encoding="utf-8")
    backup_secret.write_text("placeholder-backup-dsn", encoding="utf-8")
    master_key_secret.write_text(master_key, encoding="utf-8")
    database_secret.chmod(0o600)
    migration_secret.chmod(0o600)
    backup_secret.chmod(0o600)
    master_key_secret.chmod(0o600)
    nas = tmp_path / "nas"
    source = tmp_path / "migration" / "source"
    checkpoints = tmp_path / "migration" / "checkpoints"
    data_root = tmp_path / "platform-data"
    nas.mkdir()
    source.mkdir(parents=True)
    checkpoints.mkdir(parents=True)
    data_root.mkdir()
    metrics_secret = data_root / "secrets" / "operations-metrics.token"
    metrics_secret.parent.mkdir()
    metrics_secret.write_text("m" * 48, encoding="utf-8")
    metrics_secret.chmod(0o600)
    runtime_master_key = data_root / "secrets" / "master.key"
    runtime_master_key.write_text(master_key, encoding="utf-8")
    runtime_master_key.chmod(0o600)
    (source / "platform.sqlite3").write_bytes(b"sqlite fixture")
    return {
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_IMAGE_TAG": "candidate-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
        "LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA": "a" * 40,
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_BIND_ADDRESS": "127.0.0.1",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_PORT": "8081",
        "SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE": "true",
        "ALLOW_DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD": "false",
        "DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": str(database_secret),
        "MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": str(migration_secret),
        "BACKUP_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": str(backup_secret),
        "API_KEY_MASTER_KEY_SECRET_FILE": str(master_key_secret),
        "MYSQL_PLATFORM_SCHEMA": "labelsystem",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT": str(data_root),
        "NAS_MAPS_SOURCE": str(nas),
        "MIGRATION_SNAPSHOT_SOURCE_DIR": str(source),
        "MIGRATION_CHECKPOINT_DIR": str(checkpoints),
        "WORKER_DEPLOYMENT_MODE": "production",
        "WORKER_GROUP_SIZE": "32",
        "WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS": "6",
        "WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT": "193",
    }


def _write_environment(path: Path, environment: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    path.write_text(
        "\n".join(f"{key}={value}" for key, value in environment.items())
        + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )


def _healthy_payload(expected_workers: int = 33) -> dict[str, object]:
    workers = [
        {
            "worker_id": f"worker-{index}",
            "role": "evaluation",
            "capacity_eligible": True,
            "auxiliary_eligible": False,
            "active": True,
            "last_status": "idle",
            "readiness": "ready",
            "last_tick_at": "2026-08-30T01:00:00+00:00",
        }
        for index in range(expected_workers)
    ]
    workers[0]["last_status"] = "disabled"
    workers[0]["readiness"] = "blocked"
    workers.append({
        "worker_id": "auxiliary:worker-0",
        "role": "auxiliary",
        "capacity_eligible": False,
        "auxiliary_eligible": True,
        "auxiliary_active": True,
        "active": True,
        "last_status": "idle",
        "readiness": "ready",
        "last_tick_at": "2026-08-30T01:00:00+00:00",
    })
    workers.append({
        "worker_id": "optimization:worker-0",
        "role": "optimization",
        "capacity_eligible": False,
        "auxiliary_eligible": False,
        "optimization_eligible": True,
        "optimization_active": True,
        "active": True,
        "last_status": "idle",
        "readiness": "ready",
        "last_tick_at": "2026-08-30T01:00:00+00:00",
    })
    workers.append({
        "worker_id": "projection:worker-0",
        "role": "projection",
        "capacity_eligible": False,
        "auxiliary_eligible": False,
        "optimization_eligible": False,
        "projection_eligible": True,
        "projection_active": True,
        "active": True,
        "last_status": "idle",
        "readiness": "ready",
        "last_tick_at": "2026-08-30T01:00:00+00:00",
    })
    return {
        "status": "ready",
        "service": "3d66-label-system",
        "build": {"sha": "a" * 40},
        "database": {
            "status": "ok",
            "dialect": "mysql",
            "server_version": "8.4.11",
            "read_only": False,
            "claim_strategy": "for_update_skip_locked",
        },
        "workers": {
            "active_total_worker_count": expected_workers + 3,
            "active_worker_count": expected_workers,
            "active_evaluation_worker_count": expected_workers,
            "active_auxiliary_worker_count": 1,
            "active_optimization_worker_count": 1,
            "active_projection_worker_count": 1,
            "expected_worker_count": expected_workers,
            "missing_worker_count": 0,
            "expected_auxiliary_worker_count": 1,
            "missing_auxiliary_worker_count": 0,
            "expected_optimization_worker_count": 1,
            "missing_optimization_worker_count": 0,
            "expected_projection_worker_count": 1,
            "missing_projection_worker_count": 0,
            "worker_capacity_status": "ready",
            "workers": workers,
        },
    }

def test_runtime_paths_are_required_by_database_operation() -> None:
    root = Path("/must-not-be-opened")
    environment = {
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_IMAGE_TAG": "candidate-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
        "LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA": "a" * 40,
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_BIND_ADDRESS": "127.0.0.1",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_PORT": "8081",
        "SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE": "true",
        "ALLOW_DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD": "false",
        "DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": str(root / "database-url"),
        "BACKUP_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": str(root / "backup-database-url"),
        "MYSQL_PLATFORM_SCHEMA": "labelsystem",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT": str(root / "data"),
        "NAS_MAPS_SOURCE": str(root / "nas"),
        "WORKER_DEPLOYMENT_MODE": "production",
        "WORKER_GROUP_SIZE": "32",
        "WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS": "6",
        "WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT": "193",
    }

    runtime = validate_runtime_environment(environment, database_mode="runtime")
    existing = validate_runtime_environment(
        environment,
        database_mode="existing-schema",
    )
    initial = validate_runtime_environment(
        environment,
        database_mode="initial-import",
    )

    runtime_missing = {
        item["field"]
        for item in runtime["blockers"]
        if item["code"] == "required_value_missing"
    }
    existing_missing = {
        item["field"]
        for item in existing["blockers"]
        if item["code"] == "required_value_missing"
    }
    initial_missing = {
        item["field"]
        for item in initial["blockers"]
        if item["code"] == "required_value_missing"
    }
    assert "MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE" not in runtime_missing
    assert "MIGRATION_SNAPSHOT_SOURCE_DIR" not in runtime_missing
    assert "MIGRATION_CHECKPOINT_DIR" not in runtime_missing
    assert existing_missing == {"MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE"}
    assert initial_missing == {
        "API_KEY_MASTER_KEY_SECRET_FILE",
        "MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE",
        "MIGRATION_SNAPSHOT_SOURCE_DIR",
        "MIGRATION_CHECKPOINT_DIR",
    }


def test_runtime_master_key_requirement_switches_after_initial_import(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    target = (
        Path(environment["LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT"])
        / "secrets"
        / "master.key"
    )
    target.unlink()

    initial = validate_runtime_environment(
        environment,
        database_mode="initial-import",
    )
    runtime = validate_runtime_environment(environment, database_mode="runtime")

    assert initial["status"] == "ready"
    assert {
        (item["code"], item["field"]) for item in runtime["blockers"]
    } >= {("required_path_missing", "RUNTIME_MASTER_KEY_FILE")}


def test_runtime_rejects_an_invalid_existing_master_key(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    target = (
        Path(environment["LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT"])
        / "secrets"
        / "master.key"
    )
    target.write_text("not-a-file-aead-key", encoding="utf-8")

    report = validate_runtime_environment(environment, database_mode="runtime")

    assert {
        (item["code"], item["field"]) for item in report["blockers"]
    } >= {("master_key_invalid", "RUNTIME_MASTER_KEY_FILE")}

    [

            "shared-server-tag",

def test_runtime_environment_rejects_inline_secrets_without_echoing_them(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    secret_marker = "must-not-appear"
    environment["DATABASE_URL"] = f"mysql+pymysql://user:{secret_marker}@db/x"

    report = validate_runtime_environment(environment)
    serialized = json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False)

    assert "inline_secret_forbidden" in {
        item["code"] for item in report["blockers"]
    }
    assert secret_marker not in serialized


def test_runtime_environment_rejects_missing_and_relative_paths(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    environment["DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE"] = "relative/database-url"
    environment["NAS_MAPS_SOURCE"] = str(tmp_path / "missing-nas")
    environment["LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT"] = str(tmp_path / "missing-data")

    report = validate_runtime_environment(environment)
    codes = {item["code"] for item in report["blockers"]}

    assert "path_not_absolute" in codes
    assert "required_path_missing" in codes
    assert {
        item["field"]
        for item in report["blockers"]
        if item["code"] == "required_path_missing"
    } >= {"NAS_MAPS_SOURCE", "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT"}


def test_linux_runtime_rejects_paths_not_owned_by_fixed_container_identity(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr("app.server_runtime.sys.platform", "linux")
    original_stat = Path.stat

    def wrong_owner(path: Path, *args: object, **kwargs: object) -> object:
        metadata = original_stat(path, *args, **kwargs)
        return SimpleNamespace(
            st_mode=metadata.st_mode,
            st_uid=20002,
            st_gid=20002,
        )

    monkeypatch.setattr(Path, "stat", wrong_owner)

    report = validate_runtime_environment(environment)

    invalid_fields = {
        item["field"]
        for item in report["blockers"]
        if item["code"] == "runtime_path_owner_invalid"
    }
    assert invalid_fields >= {
        "DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE",
        "MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE",
        "BACKUP_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT",
        "MIGRATION_CHECKPOINT_DIR",
        "OPERATIONS_METRICS_TOKEN_FILE",
    }


def test_linux_runtime_rejects_unreadable_files_and_untraversable_directories(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    database_secret = Path(environment["DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE"])
    data_root = Path(environment["LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT"])
    secrets_dir = data_root / "secrets"
    monkeypatch.setattr("app.server_runtime.sys.platform", "linux")
    original_stat = Path.stat

    def runtime_owner(path: Path, *args: object, **kwargs: object) -> object:
        metadata = original_stat(path, *args, **kwargs)
        simulated_mode = {
            database_secret: 0o100200,
            data_root: 0o040600,
            secrets_dir: 0o040600,
        }.get(path, metadata.st_mode)
        return SimpleNamespace(
            st_mode=simulated_mode,
            st_uid=10001,
            st_gid=10001,
        )

    monkeypatch.setattr(Path, "stat", runtime_owner)

    report = validate_runtime_environment(environment)

    blocked = {
        (item["code"], item["field"]) for item in report["blockers"]
    }
    assert (
        "runtime_path_permissions_invalid",
        "DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE",
    ) in blocked
    assert (
        "runtime_path_permissions_invalid",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT",
    ) in blocked
    assert (
        "runtime_path_permissions_invalid",
        "RUNTIME_SECRETS_DIRECTORY",
    ) in blocked


def test_runtime_rejects_a_short_operations_metrics_token(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    token_file = (
        Path(environment["LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT"])
        / "secrets"
        / "operations-metrics.token"
    )
    token_file.write_text("too-short", encoding="utf-8")

    report = validate_runtime_environment(environment)

    assert {
        (item["code"], item["field"])
        for item in report["blockers"]
    } >= {
        (
            "operations_metrics_token_weak",
            "OPERATIONS_METRICS_TOKEN_FILE",
        )
    }

    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        fetch_operational_metrics_text(
            "http://127.0.0.1:1",
            token_file=token_file,
        )
    assert error.value.code == "operations_metrics_token_invalid"

    environment_file = tmp_path / "runtime.env"
    environment_file.write_text("A=1\nA=2\n", encoding="utf-8")

    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        parse_runtime_environment(environment_file)

    assert error.value.code == "environment_key_duplicate"

    environment_file = tmp_path / "runtime.env"
    environment_file.write_text(
        'DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE="/srv/label system/database-url"\n',
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    assert parse_runtime_environment(environment_file) == {
        "DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": "/srv/label system/database-url"
    }


def test_health_payload_requires_mysql_84_and_real_worker_loop_state() -> None:
    report = verify_health_payload(
        _healthy_payload(),
        expected_workers=33,
        expected_build_sha="a" * 40,
    )
    assert report == {
        "status": "ready",
        "build_sha": "a" * 40,
        "database_version": "8.4.11",
        "active_workers": 33,
        "expected_workers": 33,
    }

    starting = _healthy_payload()
    starting["workers"]["workers"][2]["last_status"] = "starting"  # type: ignore[index]
    starting["workers"]["workers"][2]["last_tick_at"] = None  # type: ignore[index]
    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_health_payload(starting, expected_workers=33)
    assert error.value.code == "worker_loop_not_ready"

    stalled_optimizer = _healthy_payload()
    stalled_optimizer["workers"]["workers"][-2][  # type: ignore[index]
        "optimization_active"
    ] = False
    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_health_payload(stalled_optimizer, expected_workers=33)
    assert error.value.code == "optimization_worker_snapshot_incomplete"

    stalled_projection = _healthy_payload()
    stalled_projection["workers"]["workers"][-1][  # type: ignore[index]
        "projection_active"
    ] = False
    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_health_payload(stalled_projection, expected_workers=33)
    assert error.value.code == "projection_worker_snapshot_incomplete"

    wrong_database = _healthy_payload()
    wrong_database["database"]["server_version"] = "8.0.42"  # type: ignore[index]
    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_health_payload(wrong_database, expected_workers=33)
    assert error.value.code == "mysql_version_unsupported"


def test_health_payload_rejects_a_different_or_missing_reviewed_build() -> None:
    wrong_build = _healthy_payload()
    wrong_build["build"] = {"sha": "b" * 40}

    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_health_payload(
            wrong_build,
            expected_workers=33,
            expected_build_sha="a" * 40,
        )
    assert error.value.code == "build_revision_mismatch"

    starting_wrong_build = _healthy_payload()
    starting_wrong_build["status"] = "starting"
    starting_wrong_build["build"] = {"sha": "b" * 40}
    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_health_payload(
            starting_wrong_build,
            expected_workers=33,
            expected_build_sha="a" * 40,
        )
    assert error.value.code == "build_revision_mismatch"

    missing_build = _healthy_payload()
    missing_build.pop("build")
    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_health_payload(
            missing_build,
            expected_workers=33,
            expected_build_sha="a" * 40,
        )
    assert error.value.code == "build_revision_mismatch"


def test_live_build_gate_rejects_wrong_candidate_before_readiness() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_live_build(
            {
                "status": "ok",
                "service": "3d66-label-system",
                "build": {"sha": "b" * 40},
            },
            expected_build_sha="a" * 40,
        )

    assert error.value.code == "build_revision_mismatch"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("status", "detail_code", "expected_code"),
    [
        (503, "worker_capacity_not_ready", "worker_capacity_not_ready"),
        (503, "database_not_ready", "database_not_ready"),
        (503, "production_security_not_ready", "production_security_not_ready"),
        (404, "worker_capacity_not_ready", "health_http_status_unexpected"),
    ],
)
def test_health_fetch_preserves_structured_http_failure_codes(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    status: int,
    detail_code: str,
    expected_code: str,
) -> None:
    body = json.dumps({"detail": {"code": detail_code}}).encode("utf-8")

    def fail(*_: object, **__: object) -> object:
        raise HTTPError(
            "http://127.0.0.1:8081/api/health/ready",
            status,
            "blocked",
            {},
            io.BytesIO(body),
        )

    monkeypatch.setattr("app.server_runtime.urlopen", fail)

    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        fetch_health_payload("http://127.0.0.1:8081", timeout=3)

    assert error.value.code == expected_code


def test_startup_wait_retries_transient_worker_readiness() -> None:
    now = [0.0]
    sleeps: list[float] = []
    payloads: list[object] = [
        ServerRuntimeError("health_request_failed", "starting"),
        {
            **_healthy_payload(),
            "status": "starting",
        },
        _healthy_payload(),
    ]

    def fetch(_: str, __: float) -> object:
        value = payloads.pop(0)
        if isinstance(value, Exception):
            raise value
        return value

    def sleep(seconds: float) -> None:
        sleeps.append(seconds)
        now[0] += seconds

    report = verify_health_with_startup_wait(
        "http://127.0.0.1:8081",
        expected_workers=33,
        expected_build_sha="a" * 40,
        startup_timeout=30,
        startup_interval=5,
        fetcher=fetch,
        sleeper=sleep,
        clock=lambda: now[0],
    )

    assert report["status"] == "ready"
    assert sleeps == [5, 5]


def test_startup_wait_does_not_retry_build_identity_failure() -> None:
    wrong_build = _healthy_payload()
    wrong_build["build"] = {"sha": "b" * 40}
    sleeps: list[float] = []

    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_health_with_startup_wait(
            "http://127.0.0.1:8081",
            expected_workers=33,
            expected_build_sha="a" * 40,
            startup_timeout=30,
            startup_interval=5,
            fetcher=lambda _, __: wrong_build,
            sleeper=sleeps.append,
            clock=lambda: 0,
        )

    assert error.value.code == "build_revision_mismatch"
    assert sleeps == []


def test_startup_wait_stops_at_the_bounded_deadline() -> None:
    now = [0.0]
    sleeps: list[float] = []
    attempts = [0]

    timeouts: list[float] = []

    def fetch(_: str, timeout: float) -> object:
        attempts[0] += 1
        timeouts.append(timeout)
        raise ServerRuntimeError("health_request_failed", "starting")

    def sleep(seconds: float) -> None:
        sleeps.append(seconds)
        now[0] += seconds

    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_health_with_startup_wait(
            "http://127.0.0.1:8081",
            expected_workers=33,
            expected_build_sha="a" * 40,
            startup_timeout=12,
            startup_interval=5,
            fetcher=fetch,
            sleeper=sleep,
            clock=lambda: now[0],
        )

    assert error.value.code == "health_request_failed"
    assert sleeps == [5, 5, 2]
    assert attempts == [3]
    assert timeouts == [10, 7, 2]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("startup_timeout", "startup_interval"),
    [(float("nan"), 5), (float("inf"), 5), (30, float("nan"))],
)
def test_startup_wait_rejects_non_finite_windows(
    startup_timeout: float,
    startup_interval: float,
) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_health_with_startup_wait(
            "http://127.0.0.1:8081",
            expected_workers=33,
            expected_build_sha="a" * 40,
            startup_timeout=startup_timeout,
            startup_interval=startup_interval,
        )

    assert error.value.code == "health_startup_window_invalid"


def test_release_metrics_gate_requires_complete_zero_critical_contract() -> None:
    healthy = "\n".join(
        (
            "label_system_workers_missing 0",
            "label_system_worker_loops_stale 0",
            "label_system_human_review_quota_configured 1",
            "label_system_mysql_metrics_available 1",
            "label_system_data_disk_metrics_available 1",
            "label_system_backup_available 1",
            "label_system_restore_available 1",
            "label_system_alerts_critical 0",
            "label_system_alerts_warning 2",
            "",
        )
    )

    assert verify_operational_metrics_text(healthy) == {
        "status": "ready",
        "critical_alerts": 0,
        "warning_alerts": 2,
    }

    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_operational_metrics_text(
            healthy.replace(
                "label_system_alerts_critical 0",
                "label_system_alerts_critical 1",
            )
        )
    assert error.value.code == "operations_critical"

    with pytest.raises(ServerRuntimeError) as error:
        verify_operational_metrics_text(
            healthy.replace("label_system_backup_available 1\n", "")
        )
    assert error.value.code == "operations_metrics_contract_incomplete"


def test_validate_env_cli_never_prints_inline_secret(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    secret_marker = "cli-must-not-appear"
    environment["MODEL_API_TOKEN"] = secret_marker
    environment_file = tmp_path / "runtime.env"
    _write_environment(environment_file, environment)

    completed = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            "-m",
            "app.server_runtime",
            "validate-env",
            "--env-file",
            str(environment_file),
            "--json",
        ],
        cwd=ROOT / "backend",
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        env={**os.environ, "PYTHONPATH": str(ROOT / "backend")},
    )

    assert completed.returncode == 2
    assert secret_marker not in completed.stdout
    assert secret_marker not in completed.stderr
    assert json.loads(completed.stdout)["status"] == "blocked"


def test_insecure_session_cookie_can_be_acknowledged_for_lan_http(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """内网明文 HTTP 部署：显式确认后 SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=false 不再是阻断项。"""

    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    environment["SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE"] = "false"
    blocked = validate_runtime_environment(environment, database_mode="runtime")
    assert ("session_cookie_insecure", "SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE") in {
        (item["code"], item["field"]) for item in blocked["blockers"]
    }
    environment["ALLOW_INSECURE_SESSION_COOKIE"] = "true"
    acknowledged = validate_runtime_environment(environment, database_mode="runtime")
    assert "session_cookie_insecure" not in {item["code"] for item in acknowledged["blockers"]}


def test_async_deployment_uses_physical_worker_count_and_bounded_slots(tmp_path):
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    environment.update(WORKER_DEPLOYMENT_MODE='async', WORKER_GROUP_SIZE='3',
        WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS='1', WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT='4',
        WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS='8', WORKER_RUNTIME_PHASE_THREADS='1')
    assert validate_runtime_environment(environment)['status'] == 'ready'
    environment['WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS'] = '208'
    assert validate_runtime_environment(environment)['status'] == 'blocked'
    environment['WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS'] = '0'
    assert validate_runtime_environment(environment)['status'] == 'blocked'

    ('32','32','1','1','ready'),
    ('32','0','1','1','blocked'),
    ('32','16','1','1','blocked'),
    ('32','32','0','1','blocked'),
    ('64','64','1','1','blocked'),
    ('32','32','1','2','blocked'),
])
def test_async_deployment_accepts_only_measured_expanded_profile(
        tmp_path, slots, validated, autotune, phase, expected):
    # 137: three processes, 32 waits each, one preparation thread. The final
    # 90-second isolated MySQL/native probe completed all 96 simultaneous calls.
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    environment.update(WORKER_DEPLOYMENT_MODE='async', WORKER_GROUP_SIZE='3',
        WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS='1', WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT='4',
        WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS=slots, WORKER_RUNTIME_VALIDATED_SLOTS=validated,
        WORKER_RUNTIME_AUTOTUNE=autotune, WORKER_RUNTIME_PHASE_THREADS=phase)
    assert validate_runtime_environment(environment)['status'] == expected
