@pytest.mark.parametrize('override,expected', [
    ({}, 'ready'),
    # The 2026-09-15 evidence covers exactly four preparation threads on the
    # 6 x 16 topology; neither the old single thread nor an untested count.
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_PHASE_THREADS': '1'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_PHASE_THREADS': '2'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_PHASE_THREADS': '8'}, 'blocked'),
    # Pool must cover phase_threads + 2, matching CapacityEnvelope.
    ({'DATABASE_POOL_SIZE': '5'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'DATABASE_POOL_SIZE': '6'}, 'ready'),
    ({'DATABASE_POOL_SIZE': ''}, 'blocked'),
    # 96 -> 192 measured negative, so the per-process ceiling does not move.
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS': '32'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_VALIDATED_SLOTS': '32'}, 'blocked'),
    # Everything the six-process profile already pinned stays pinned.
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_AUTOTUNE': '0'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION': '1'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_GROUP_SIZE': '3', 'WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT': '4'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT': '6'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_CAPACITY_PROFILE': 'new-server-16g-6x16-20260914'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_CAPACITY_PROFILE': 'unlimited'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_CAPACITY_PROFILE': ''}, 'blocked'),
])
def test_phase4_profile_moves_only_preparation_threads(tmp_path, override, expected):
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    environment.update(
        WORKER_DEPLOYMENT_MODE='async', WORKER_GROUP_SIZE='6',
        WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS='1', WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT='7',
        WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS='16', WORKER_RUNTIME_VALIDATED_SLOTS='16',
        WORKER_RUNTIME_AUTOTUNE='1', WORKER_RUNTIME_PHASE_THREADS='4',
        WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION='0', DATABASE_POOL_SIZE='8',
        WORKER_RUNTIME_CAPACITY_PROFILE='new-server-16g-6x16-phase4-20260915')
    environment.update(override)
    assert validate_runtime_environment(environment)['status'] == expected


def test_phase4_pool_blocker_names_the_pool_not_the_threads(tmp_path):
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    environment.update(
        WORKER_DEPLOYMENT_MODE='async', WORKER_GROUP_SIZE='6',
        WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS='1', WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT='7',
        WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS='16', WORKER_RUNTIME_VALIDATED_SLOTS='16',

        WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION='0', DATABASE_POOL_SIZE='4',
        WORKER_RUNTIME_CAPACITY_PROFILE='new-server-16g-6x16-phase4-20260915')
    codes = {item['code'] for item in validate_runtime_environment(environment)['blockers']}
    assert 'database_pool_below_phase_threads' in codes
    assert 'async_phase_threads_unverified' not in codes


@pytest.mark.parametrize('override,expected', [
    ({}, 'ready'),
    # 24 是这一档的上限，不是「随便往上」；validated 也必须跟着说 24。
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS': '16'}, 'ready'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS': '25'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS': '32'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_VALIDATED_SLOTS': '16'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_VALIDATED_SLOTS': '32'}, 'blocked'),
    # 准备线程沿用 phase4 的 4，连接池要求不变。
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_PHASE_THREADS': '1'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'DATABASE_POOL_SIZE': '5'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'DATABASE_POOL_SIZE': '6'}, 'ready'),
    # 旧两个档位的名字不能借 24 槽：它们的证据只覆盖 16。
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_CAPACITY_PROFILE': 'new-server-16g-6x16-phase4-20260915'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_CAPACITY_PROFILE': 'new-server-16g-6x16-20260914'}, 'blocked'),
    # 拓扑本身仍然钉死在 6 进程。
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_AUTOTUNE': '0'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION': '1'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_GROUP_SIZE': '3', 'WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT': '4'}, 'blocked'),
    ({'WORKER_GROUP_SIZE': '8', 'WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT': '9'}, 'blocked'),
])
def test_wide_6x24_profile_is_the_only_way_to_24_slots(tmp_path, override, expected):
    environment = _runtime_environment(tmp_path)
    environment.update(
        WORKER_DEPLOYMENT_MODE='async', WORKER_GROUP_SIZE='6',
        WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS='1', WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT='7',
        WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS='24', WORKER_RUNTIME_VALIDATED_SLOTS='24',
        WORKER_RUNTIME_AUTOTUNE='1', WORKER_RUNTIME_PHASE_THREADS='4',
        WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION='0', DATABASE_POOL_SIZE='6',
        WORKER_RUNTIME_CAPACITY_PROFILE='new-server-32g-6x24-phase4-20260917')
    environment.update(override)
    assert validate_runtime_environment(environment)['status'] == expected

===== FILE backend/tests/test_server_soak.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path


REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
SOAK_SCRIPT = REPO_ROOT / "scripts/integration/server_soak.py"
MOCK_SERVER = REPO_ROOT / "scripts/integration/mock_openai_server.py"


def _load_module(path: Path, name: str):
    existing = sys.modules.get(name)
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
    assert spec is not None and spec.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[name] = module
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def _passing_cycle(*, rss: int = 100_000_000) -> dict:
    return {
        "load": {
            "asset_count": 25,
            "counts": {
                "seeded": 25,
                "completed": 25,
                "missing_items": 0,
                "duplicate_claims": 0,
                "lease_recoveries": 0,
            },
            "latency_ms": {
                "claim_p50": 2.0,
                "claim_p95": 4.0,
                "claim_p99": 5.0,
            },
            "acceptance": {
                "duplicate_free": True,
                "zero_loss": True,
                "terminal_counts_reconcile": True,
                "passed": True,
            },
            "resources": {"peak_rss_bytes": rss},
            "database": {"growth_bytes": 1024},
            "secret_payload": "sk-test-must-not-appear",
        },
        "fault": {
            "all_passed": True,
            "cases": [
                {"key": "worker_kill", "passed": True, "evidence": {}},
                {
                    "key": "provider_429",
                    "passed": True,
                    "evidence": {"retry_after_seconds": 17},
                },
                {
                    "key": "outbox_target_pause",
                    "passed": True,
                    "evidence": {"retry_after_seconds": 60},
                },
                {"key": "application_restart", "passed": True, "evidence": {}},
            ],
            "database_url": (
                "mysql+pymysql://must-not-appear:must-not-appear@127.0.0.1/source"
            ),
        },
        "outbox_idempotency": {
            "passed": True,
            "same_event": True,
            "row_count": 1,
            "payload_hash": "a" * 64,
        },
        "observation": {
            "queue_oldest_age_seconds": 0.0,
            "outbox_oldest_age_seconds": 0.0,
            "queue_pending": 0,
            "outbox_pending": 1,
        },
        "resources": {"peak_rss_bytes": rss, "process_cpu_seconds": 0.5},
    }


def _provider_events() -> list[dict]:
    return [
        {"mode": "ok", "outcome": "ok", "passed": True, "elapsed_ms": 3.0},
        {"mode": "provider429", "outcome": "429", "passed": True, "elapsed_ms": 2.0},
        {"mode": "provider5xx", "outcome": "provider5xx", "passed": True, "elapsed_ms": 2.5},
        {"mode": "timeout", "outcome": "timeout", "passed": True, "elapsed_ms": 30.0},
    ]


def test_mock_provider_records_rate_limit_without_request_secrets(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    mock = _load_module(MOCK_SERVER, "server_soak_mock_provider")
    state = mock.State(tmp_path, timeout_seconds=0.05)
    (tmp_path / "mode.txt").write_text("provider429\n", encoding="utf-8")

    state.record(
        {
            "model": "offline-soak-model",
            "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "private payload"}],
            "authorization": "Bearer must-not-appear",
        }
    )

    audit = json.loads((tmp_path / "requests.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert audit["response_status"] == 429
    assert audit["usage_included"] is False
    assert "authorization" not in audit
    assert "private payload" not in json.dumps(audit)


def test_build_report_proves_soak_acceptance_and_redacts_raw_inputs() -> None:
    soak = _load_module(SOAK_SCRIPT, "server_soak_report")
    started = datetime(2026, 8, 30, 1, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    report = soak.build_report(
        cycles=[_passing_cycle(), _passing_cycle(rss=101_000_000)],
        provider_events=_provider_events(),
        started_at=started,
        finished_at=started + timedelta(minutes=10),
        requested_duration_seconds=600,
        cohort_size=25,
        worker_count=4,
        failure_schedule=("ok", "provider429", "provider5xx", "timeout"),
    )

    assert report["schema_version"] == "label-system-server-soak-v2"
    assert report["counts"] == {
        "cycles": 2,
        "seeded": 50,
        "completed": 50,
        "missing_items": 0,
        "duplicate_claims": 0,
    }
    assert report["failure_injection"]["counters"] == {
        "ok": 1,
        "429": 1,
        "provider5xx": 1,
        "timeout": 1,
    }
    assert report["acceptance"]["zero_loss"] is True
    assert report["acceptance"]["duplicate_free"] is True
    assert report["acceptance"]["bounded_retry"] is True
    assert report["acceptance"]["recovered_lease"] is True
    assert report["acceptance"]["idempotent_outbox"] is True
    assert report["acceptance"]["passed"] is True
    assert report["retries"] == {
        "provider_429_scheduled": 2,
        "outbox_backoffs_scheduled": 2,
        "maximum_retry_after_seconds": 60,
    }
    assert report["recoveries"] == {
        "expired_leases_recovered": 2,
        "application_connection_restarts": 2,
        "outbox_idempotency_checks": 2,
    }
    assert len(report["evidence_hash"]) == 64

    serialized = json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False)
    assert "sk-test-must-not-appear" not in serialized
    assert "mysql+pymysql" not in serialized
    assert "database_url" not in serialized
    assert "secret_payload" not in serialized


def test_report_fails_closed_when_failure_injection_or_recovery_is_missing() -> None:
    soak = _load_module(SOAK_SCRIPT, "server_soak_fail_closed")
    cycle = _passing_cycle()
    cycle["fault"]["cases"] = [
        case for case in cycle["fault"]["cases"] if case["key"] != "worker_kill"
    ]
    started = datetime(2026, 8, 30, 1, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    report = soak.build_report(
        cycles=[cycle],
        provider_events=[_provider_events()[0]],
        started_at=started,
        finished_at=started + timedelta(seconds=30),
        requested_duration_seconds=30,
        cohort_size=25,
        worker_count=4,
        failure_schedule=("ok", "provider429", "provider5xx", "timeout"),
    )

    assert report["acceptance"]["recovered_lease"] is False
    assert report["acceptance"]["failure_schedule_covered"] is False
    assert report["acceptance"]["passed"] is False


def test_report_cannot_pass_when_failure_schedule_omits_mandatory_modes() -> None:
    soak = _load_module(SOAK_SCRIPT, "server_soak_mandatory_failures")
    started = datetime(2026, 8, 30, 1, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    report = soak.build_report(
        cycles=[_passing_cycle()],
        provider_events=[_provider_events()[0]],
        started_at=started,
        finished_at=started + timedelta(seconds=30),
        requested_duration_seconds=30,
        cohort_size=25,
        worker_count=4,
        failure_schedule=("ok",),
    )

    assert report["acceptance"]["mandatory_failure_modes_covered"] is False
    assert report["acceptance"]["passed"] is False


def test_peak_memory_assessment_allows_bounded_warmup_but_rejects_growth() -> None:
    soak = _load_module(SOAK_SCRIPT, "server_soak_memory_assessment")
    mib = 1024 * 1024

    bounded = soak.assess_peak_memory(
        [150 * mib, 180 * mib, 205 * mib, 220 * mib, 224 * mib, 228 * mib]
    )
    growing = soak.assess_peak_memory(
        [150 * mib, 180 * mib, 205 * mib, 225 * mib, 245 * mib, 255 * mib]
    )
    over_limit = soak.assess_peak_memory(
        [150 * mib, 220 * mib, 250 * mib, 257 * mib, 257 * mib]
    )

    assert bounded["passed"] is True
    assert bounded["tail_growth_bytes"] == 23 * mib
    assert growing["passed"] is False
    assert growing["tail_growth_bytes"] == 50 * mib
    assert over_limit["passed"] is False
    assert over_limit["maximum_peak_bytes"] == 257 * mib


def test_existing_v1_report_can_only_reassess_memory_from_recorded_samples() -> None:
    soak = _load_module(SOAK_SCRIPT, "server_soak_report_reassessment")
    started = datetime(2026, 8, 30, 1, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    mib = 1024 * 1024
    report = soak.build_report(
        cycles=[
            _passing_cycle(rss=value * mib)
            for value in (150, 180, 205, 220, 224, 228)
        ],
        provider_events=_provider_events(),
        started_at=started,
        finished_at=started + timedelta(minutes=10),
        requested_duration_seconds=600,
        cohort_size=25,
        worker_count=4,
        failure_schedule=("ok", "provider429", "provider5xx", "timeout"),
    )
    legacy = json.loads(json.dumps(report))
    legacy["schema_version"] = "label-system-server-soak-v1"
    legacy["resources"].pop("memory_assessment", None)
    legacy["acceptance"]["stable_memory"] = False
    legacy["acceptance"]["passed"] = False

    reassessed = soak.reassess_report_memory(legacy)

    assert reassessed["schema_version"] == "label-system-server-soak-v2"
    assert reassessed["counts"] == legacy["counts"]
    assert reassessed["failure_injection"] == legacy["failure_injection"]
    assert reassessed["acceptance"]["stable_memory"] is True
    assert reassessed["acceptance"]["passed"] is True
    assert reassessed["reassessment"]["source_evidence_hash"] == (
        legacy["evidence_hash"]
    )

    legacy["acceptance"]["zero_loss"] = False
    legacy["acceptance"]["passed"] = False
    still_failed = soak.reassess_report_memory(legacy)
    assert still_failed["acceptance"]["zero_loss"] is False
    assert still_failed["acceptance"]["passed"] is False


def test_failure_schedule_parser_is_strict_and_deduplicates() -> None:
    soak = _load_module(SOAK_SCRIPT, "server_soak_schedule")
    assert soak.parse_failure_schedule("ok, provider429,ok,timeout") == (
        "ok",
        "provider429",
        "timeout",
    )

    for value in ("", "ok,unknown", "provider5xx,,timeout"):
        try:
            soak.parse_failure_schedule(value)
        except ValueError:
            pass
        else:
            raise AssertionError(f"schedule should fail: {value!r}")

)


def _context() -> tuple[object, Session, object, object, object]:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    field_contract = create_field_demand_contract(
        db,
        contract_key="3d-search",
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        consumer_key="search",
        owner="tpeng-3d",
        fields=[
            {"field_key": "style", "source_path": "semantic.style", "required": True, "data_type": "string"},
            {"field_key": "quality", "source_path": "quality.score", "required": True, "data_type": "number"},
        ],
        thresholds={"accuracy": 0.9, "recall": 0.9},
        status="active",
        created_by="admin",
    )
    target = create_shadow_projection_target(
        db,
        target_key="3d-shadow-unified",
        adapter_key="fixture-shadow",
        connection_locator="target-registry:3d-shadow",
        secret_reference="secret-ref:3d-shadow-writer",
        schema_name="labellab_shadow",
        table_name="asset_dimension_shadow",
        environment="shadow",
        shadow_only=True,
        owner="tpeng-3d",
        schema_fingerprint="e" * 64,
        status="active",
        created_by="admin",
    )
    projection_contract = create_contract_version(
        db,
        contract_key="3d-shadow-unified",
        target_role="unified_dimension",
        table_name="asset_dimension_shadow",
        environment="shadow",
        primary_key=["content_key"],
        field_mappings={
            "content_key": "content_key",
            "category_key": "category_key",
            "style": "semantic.style",
            "quality_score": "quality.score",
            "label_version": "$label.version",
            "asset_version": "provenance.asset_sha256",
            "mechanism_version": "provenance.strategy_bundle_id",
            "model_version": "provenance.model_id",
        },
        input_versions={"label_schema_version": "published-label-v1"},
        mode="snapshot",
        idempotency_key_template="{table_name}:{content_key}:{label_version}",
        checkpoint={"kind": "published_label_id"},
        reconciliation={"checks": ["row_count", "payload_hash", "version"]},
        rollback={"strategy": "batch_delete"},
        owner="tpeng-3d",
        status="active",
        created_by="admin",
        adapter_key="fixture-shadow",
        target_key=target.target_key,
        write_policy="shadow_only",
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        field_contract_id=field_contract.id,
        max_batch_size=500,
    )
    _published(db, content_key="3d:1001", category_key="model_3d_su", style="modern")
    _published(db, content_key="space:2001", category_key="space_image", style="other")
    db.commit()
    return engine, db, field_contract, target, projection_contract


def _published(db: Session, *, content_key: str, category_key: str, style: str) -> None:
    release = LabelRelease(
        release_key=f"release:{content_key}",
        content_key=content_key,
        category_key=category_key,
        label_schema_version="published-label-v1",
        label_payload_json="{}",
        payload_hash="a" * 64,
        status="published",
        requested_by="admin",
        approved_by="admin",
        approved_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        published_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )
    db.add(release)
    db.flush()
    payload = {
        "content_key": content_key,
        "category_key": category_key,
        "semantic": {"style": style},
        "quality": {"score": 90},
        "provenance": {
            "asset_sha256": "b" * 64,
            "strategy_bundle_id": 7,
            "model_id": "3d-quality-model-v1",
        },
        "candidate_mechanism": {"must_not_project": True},
        "raw_response": "must-not-project",
    }
    db.add(
        PublishedLabel(
            release_id=release.id,
            content_key=content_key,
            category_key=category_key,
            version=1,
            label_schema_version="published-label-v1",
            label_payload_json=json.dumps(payload),
            payload_hash="c" * 64,
            status="published",
            published_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )
    )


def test_shadow_run_rejects_more_than_500_rows() -> None:
    engine, db, field_contract, target, contract = _context()
    try:
        run = enqueue_shadow_projection_run(
            db,
            projection_contract=contract,
            field_contract=field_contract,
            target=target,
            max_rows=501,
            actor="admin",
        )
        assert run.status == "blocked"
        assert run.error_code == "CANARY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED"
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_shadow_manifest_contains_only_published_matching_category() -> None:
    engine, db, field_contract, target, contract = _context()
    try:
        run = enqueue_shadow_projection_run(db, projection_contract=contract, field_contract=field_contract, target=target, max_rows=10, actor="admin")
        manifest = build_shadow_manifest(db, run=run)
        assert manifest.row_count == 1
        assert manifest.rows[0]["category_key"] == "model_3d_su"
        serialized = json.dumps(manifest.rows)
        assert "space:2001" not in serialized
        assert "candidate_mechanism" not in serialized
        assert "raw_response" not in serialized
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_shadow_manifest_rejects_non_positive_mechanism_version() -> None:
    engine, db, field_contract, target, contract = _context()
    try:
        label = db.scalar(
            select(PublishedLabel).where(PublishedLabel.category_key == "model_3d_su")
        )
        payload = json.loads(label.label_payload_json)
        payload["provenance"]["strategy_bundle_id"] = 0
        label.label_payload_json = json.dumps(payload)
        run = enqueue_shadow_projection_run(
            db,
            projection_contract=contract,
            field_contract=field_contract,
            target=target,
            max_rows=10,
            actor="admin",
        )

        with pytest.raises(ShadowProjectionError, match="机制"):
            build_shadow_manifest(db, run=run)
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_shadow_target_must_be_shadow_only_and_least_privileged() -> None:
    engine, db, field_contract, target, contract = _context()
    adapter = FixtureShadowProjectionAdapter(
        shadow_only=True,
        least_privileged=False,
        schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint,
    )
    try:
        run = enqueue_shadow_projection_run(db, projection_contract=contract, field_contract=field_contract, target=target, max_rows=10, actor="admin")
        shadow_projection_worker_tick(db, "worker-a", adapter_resolver=lambda _target: adapter)
        assert run.status == "blocked"
        assert run.error_code == "SHADOW_PERMISSION_OVERBROAD"
        assert adapter.rows == []
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_crash_after_apply_retries_without_duplicate_rows() -> None:
    engine, db, field_contract, target, contract = _context()
    adapter = FixtureShadowProjectionAdapter(
        shadow_only=True,
        least_privileged=True,
        schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint,
        transient_after_apply=1,
    )
    try:
        run = enqueue_shadow_projection_run(db, projection_contract=contract, field_contract=field_contract, target=target, max_rows=10, actor="admin")
        shadow_projection_worker_tick(db, "worker-a", adapter_resolver=lambda _target: adapter)
        assert run.status == "queued"
        assert adapter.rows and len(adapter.rows) == 1
        run.retry_after = None
        shadow_projection_worker_tick(db, "worker-a", adapter_resolver=lambda _target: adapter)
        assert run.status == "succeeded"
        assert len(adapter.rows) == 1
        assert json.loads(run.checkpoint_json)["reconciled"] is True
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_unexpected_adapter_failure_rolls_back_worker_state_and_recovers() -> None:
    engine, db, field_contract, target, contract = _context()
    adapter = FixtureShadowProjectionAdapter(
        shadow_only=True,
        least_privileged=True,
        schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint,
    )

    class UnexpectedOnceAdapter:
        def __init__(self) -> None:
            self.should_fail = True

        def verify_shadow_target(self) -> ShadowSafetyEvidence:
            return adapter.verify_shadow_target()

        def apply_batch(
            self, *, batch_id: str, rows: list[dict[str, object]]
        ) -> None:
            adapter.apply_batch(batch_id=batch_id, rows=rows)
            if self.should_fail:
                self.should_fail = False
                raise RuntimeError("unexpected adapter failure after apply")

        def read_back(self, *, batch_id: str) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
            return adapter.read_back(batch_id=batch_id)

        def rollback_batch(self, *, batch_id: str) -> int:
            return adapter.rollback_batch(batch_id=batch_id)

    unexpected_adapter = UnexpectedOnceAdapter()
    try:
        run = enqueue_shadow_projection_run(
            db,
            projection_contract=contract,
            field_contract=field_contract,
            target=target,
            max_rows=10,
            actor="admin",
        )
        db.commit()
        run_id = run.id
        db.close()

        with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as failing_db:
            with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="unexpected adapter failure"):
                shadow_projection_worker_tick(
                    failing_db,
                    "worker-a",
                    adapter_resolver=lambda _target: unexpected_adapter,
                )
            failing_db.rollback()

        assert len(adapter.rows) == 1
        with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as check_db:
            stored = check_db.get(ShadowProjectionRun, run_id)
            assert stored.status == "queued"
            assert check_db.scalar(select(func.count(ShadowProjectionLease.id))) == 0

        with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as retry_db:
            recovered = shadow_projection_worker_tick(
                retry_db,
                "worker-a",
                adapter_resolver=lambda _target: unexpected_adapter,
            )
            retry_db.commit()
            assert recovered.status == "succeeded"
        assert len(adapter.rows) == 1
    finally:
        if db.is_active:
            db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_target_lease_allows_only_one_writer() -> None:
    engine, db, field_contract, target, contract = _context()
    adapter = FixtureShadowProjectionAdapter(shadow_only=True, least_privileged=True, schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint)
    try:
        run = enqueue_shadow_projection_run(db, projection_contract=contract, field_contract=field_contract, target=target, max_rows=10, actor="admin")
        db.add(ShadowProjectionLease(target_id=target.id, worker_id="worker-other", expires_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=1)))
        db.flush()
        assert shadow_projection_worker_tick(db, "worker-a", adapter_resolver=lambda _target: adapter) is None
        assert run.status == "queued"
        assert adapter.rows == []
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_three_transient_failures_open_target_circuit() -> None:
    engine, db, field_contract, target, contract = _context()
    adapter = FixtureShadowProjectionAdapter(
        shadow_only=True,
        least_privileged=True,
        schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint,
        transient_before_apply=3,
    )
    try:
        run = enqueue_shadow_projection_run(db, projection_contract=contract, field_contract=field_contract, target=target, max_rows=10, actor="admin")
        for _ in range(3):
            run.retry_after = None
            shadow_projection_worker_tick(db, "worker-a", adapter_resolver=lambda _target: adapter)
        assert run.status == "blocked"
        assert run.error_code == "PROJECTION_CIRCUIT_OPEN"
        assert target.circuit_opened_at is not None
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_hash_drift_blocks_checkpoint_and_batch_can_be_rolled_back() -> None:
    engine, db, field_contract, target, contract = _context()
    adapter = FixtureShadowProjectionAdapter(
        shadow_only=True,
        least_privileged=True,
        schema_fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint,
        corrupt_readback=True,
    )
    try:
        run = enqueue_shadow_projection_run(db, projection_contract=contract, field_contract=field_contract, target=target, max_rows=10, actor="admin")
        shadow_projection_worker_tick(db, "worker-a", adapter_resolver=lambda _target: adapter)
        assert run.status == "blocked"
        assert run.error_code == "PROJECTION_HASH_DRIFT"
        assert json.loads(run.checkpoint_json).get("reconciled") is not True
        rollback_shadow_projection_run(db, run=run, adapter=adapter, actor="admin")
        assert run.status == "rolled_back"
        assert adapter.rows == []
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ShadowProjectionRun.id))) == 1
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_sql_shadow_adapter_upserts_idempotently_and_rolls_back_batch() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql("""
            CREATE TABLE asset_dimension_shadow (
                batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                content_key TEXT NOT NULL,
                payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
                PRIMARY KEY (batch_id, content_key)
            )
        """)
    adapter = SqlShadowProjectionAdapter(
        connection_factory=engine.begin,
        table_name="asset_dimension_shadow",
        evidence=ShadowSafetyEvidence(
            shadow_only=True,
            least_privileged=True,
            schema_fingerprint="e" * 64,
        ),
    )
    try:
        adapter.apply_batch(
            batch_id="shadow-batch-1",
            rows=[{"content_key": "3d:1001", "quality_score": 90}],
        )
        adapter.apply_batch(
            batch_id="shadow-batch-1",
            rows=[{"content_key": "3d:1001", "quality_score": 91}],
        )

        assert adapter.read_back(batch_id="shadow-batch-1") == [
            {"content_key": "3d:1001", "quality_score": 91}
        ]
        assert adapter.rollback_batch(batch_id="shadow-batch-1") == 1
        assert adapter.read_back(batch_id="shadow-batch-1") == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@contextmanager
def _api_context() -> Iterator[tuple[TestClient, sessionmaker[Session]]]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(
        username="shadow-admin",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="影子投影管理员",
        role="admin",
        is_admin=True,
    )

    def override_db() -> Iterator[Session]:
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    try:
        with TestClient(app) as client:
            yield client, sessions
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def _target_payload() -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "target_key": "3d-shadow-unified",
        "adapter_key": "sql-shadow",
        "connection_locator": "target-registry:3d-shadow",
        "secret_reference": "secret-ref:3d-shadow-writer",
        "schema_name": "labellab_shadow",
        "table_name": "asset_dimension_shadow",
        "environment": "shadow",
        "shadow_only": True,
        "owner": "tpeng-3d",
        "schema_fingerprint": "e" * 64,
        "status": "active",
    }


def _shadow_contract_payload(*, field_contract_id: int) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "contract_key": "3d-shadow-unified",
        "target_role": "unified_dimension",
        "table_name": "asset_dimension_shadow",
        "environment": "shadow",
        "adapter_key": "sql-shadow",
        "target_key": "3d-shadow-unified",
        "write_policy": "shadow_only",
        "category_key": "model_3d_su",
        "field_contract_id": field_contract_id,
        "max_batch_size": 500,
        "primary_key": ["content_key"],
        "field_mappings": {
            "content_key": "content_key",
            "category_key": "category_key",
            "style": "semantic.style",
            "quality_score": "quality.score",
            "label_version": "$label.version",
            "asset_version": "provenance.asset_sha256",
            "mechanism_version": "provenance.strategy_bundle_id",
            "model_version": "provenance.model_id",
        },
        "input_versions": {"label_schema_version": "published-label-v1"},
        "mode": "snapshot",
        "idempotency_key_template": "{table_name}:{content_key}:{label_version}",
        "checkpoint": {"kind": "published_label_id"},
        "reconciliation": {"checks": ["row_count", "payload_hash", "version"]},
        "rollback": {"strategy": "batch_delete"},
        "owner": "tpeng-3d",
        "status": "active",
    }


def test_shadow_projection_admin_apis_redact_secret_and_enqueue_run() -> None:
    with _api_context() as (client, sessions):
        with sessions() as db:
            field_contract = create_field_demand_contract(
                db,
                contract_key="3d-search",
                category_key="model_3d_su",
                consumer_key="search",
                owner="tpeng-3d",
                fields=[
                    {"field_key": "style", "source_path": "semantic.style", "required": True, "data_type": "string"},
                    {"field_key": "quality", "source_path": "quality.score", "required": True, "data_type": "number"},
                ],
                thresholds={"accuracy": 0.9, "recall": 0.9},
                status="active",
                created_by="admin",
            )
            db.commit()
            field_contract_id = field_contract.id

        target_response = client.post(
            "/api/shadow-projection-targets", json=_target_payload()
        )
        assert target_response.status_code == 200, target_response.text
        target = target_response.json()
        assert target["connection_locator"] == "target-registry:3d-shadow"
        assert target["secret_status"] == "unresolved"
        assert "secret_reference" not in target
        assert "3d-shadow-writer" not in target_response.text

        contract_response = client.post(
            "/api/projection-contracts",
            json=_shadow_contract_payload(field_contract_id=field_contract_id),
        )
        assert contract_response.status_code == 200, contract_response.text
        contract = contract_response.json()
        assert contract["environment"] == "shadow"
        assert contract["write_policy"] == "shadow_only"

        run_response = client.post(
            "/api/shadow-projection-runs",
            json={
                "projection_contract_id": contract["id"],
                "field_contract_id": field_contract_id,
                "target_id": target["id"],
                "max_rows": 500,
            },
        )
        assert run_response.status_code == 200, run_response.text
        run = run_response.json()
        assert run["status"] == "queued"
        assert run["max_rows"] == 500
        assert run["target"]["secret_status"] == "unresolved"
        assert "secret_reference" not in run_response.text

        listed = client.get("/api/shadow-projection-runs")
        detail = client.get(f"/api/shadow-projection-runs/{run['id']}")
        assert listed.status_code == 200
        assert [item["id"] for item in listed.json()["items"]] == [run["id"]]
        assert detail.status_code == 200
        assert detail.json()["batch_id"] == run["batch_id"]

        with sessions() as db:
            stored_run = db.get(ShadowProjectionRun, run["id"])
            stored_target = stored_run.target
            stored_run.status = "blocked"
            stored_run.error_code = "PROJECTION_CIRCUIT_OPEN"
            stored_target.consecutive_failures = 3
            stored_target.circuit_opened_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            db.commit()

        retried = client.post(
            f"/api/shadow-projection-runs/{run['id']}/retry"
        )
        assert retried.status_code == 200, retried.text
        assert retried.json()["status"] == "queued"
        assert retried.json()["error_code"] == ""
        assert retried.json()["checkpoint"]["resume"]["circuit_reviewed"] is True


def test_shadow_projection_retry_rejects_non_transient_blocker() -> None:
    engine, db, field_contract, target, contract = _context()
    try:
        run = enqueue_shadow_projection_run(
            db,
            projection_contract=contract,
            field_contract=field_contract,
            target=target,
            max_rows=10,
            actor="admin",
        )
        run.status = "blocked"
        run.error_code = "PROJECTION_HASH_DRIFT"
        with pytest.raises(ShadowProjectionError, match="不是可重试"):
            retry_shadow_projection_run(db, run=run, actor="admin")
        assert run.status == "blocked"
        assert run.error_code == "PROJECTION_HASH_DRIFT"
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_worker_loop_isolates_shadow_projection_tick_failure(monkeypatch) -> None:
    from app import worker

    calls = {"shadow": 0, "continue": 0}
    statuses: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

    @contextmanager
    def fake_session_scope() -> Iterator[object]:
        yield object()

    async def fake_process_one(**_kwargs: object) -> bool:
        return False

    def fail_shadow_tick(*_args: object, **_kwargs: object) -> None:
        calls["shadow"] += 1
        raise RuntimeError("shadow tick failed")

    def should_continue() -> bool:
        calls["continue"] += 1
        return calls["continue"] == 1

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "init_database", lambda: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", fake_session_scope)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "seed_defaults", lambda _db: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "touch_automation_worker_status", lambda *_args, **_kwargs: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "record_automation_worker_status",
        lambda _db, **kwargs: statuses.append(
            (str(kwargs["status"]), str(kwargs["readiness"]))
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "process_one", fake_process_one)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "optimization_worker_tick", lambda _worker_id: {"status": "idle"})
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "shadow_projection_worker_tick", fail_shadow_tick, raising=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker.time, "sleep", lambda _seconds: None)

    worker.run_forever(
        poll_seconds=0,
        should_continue=should_continue,
        initialize_database=False,
        consume_jobs=False,
        run_auxiliary_ticks=False,
        run_optimization_ticks=False,
        run_shadow_ticks=True,
        worker_role="projection",
    )

    assert calls["shadow"] == 1
    assert statuses == [("error", "blocked")]


def test_dedicated_optimization_worker_error_is_not_overwritten_by_idle(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    from app import worker

    calls = {"continue": 0}
    statuses: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

    @contextmanager
    def fake_session_scope() -> Iterator[object]:
        yield object()

    def should_continue() -> bool:
        calls["continue"] += 1
        return calls["continue"] == 1

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", fake_session_scope)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "touch_automation_worker_status",
        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: None,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "record_automation_worker_status",
        lambda _db, **kwargs: statuses.append(
            (str(kwargs["status"]), str(kwargs["readiness"]))
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "optimization_worker_tick",
        lambda _worker_id: {"status": "worker_error"},
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker.time, "sleep", lambda _seconds: None)

    worker.run_forever(
        poll_seconds=0,
        should_continue=should_continue,
        initialize_database=False,
        consume_jobs=False,
        run_auxiliary_ticks=False,
        run_optimization_ticks=True,
        run_shadow_ticks=False,
        worker_role="optimization",
    )

    assert statuses == [("error", "blocked")]


def test_auxiliary_error_survives_poll_before_next_real_tick(monkeypatch) -> None:
    from app import worker

    calls = {"continue": 0, "recovery": 0}
    statuses: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    clock = iter((0.0, 1.0))

    @contextmanager
    def fake_session_scope() -> Iterator[object]:
        yield object()

    def should_continue() -> bool:
        calls["continue"] += 1
        return calls["continue"] <= 2

    def fail_recovery() -> None:
        calls["recovery"] += 1
        raise RuntimeError("auxiliary recovery failed")

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", fake_session_scope)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "touch_automation_worker_status",
        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: None,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "record_automation_worker_status",
        lambda _db, **kwargs: statuses.append(
            (str(kwargs["status"]), str(kwargs["readiness"]))
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "recover_runtime_once", fail_recovery)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "dispatch_due_incremental_batches",
        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: [],
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker.time, "sleep", lambda _seconds: None)

    worker.run_forever(
        poll_seconds=0,
        should_continue=should_continue,
        initialize_database=False,
        consume_jobs=False,
        run_auxiliary_ticks=True,
        run_optimization_ticks=False,
        run_shadow_ticks=False,
        worker_role="auxiliary",
        auxiliary_tick_seconds=5,
        monotonic_clock=lambda: next(clock),
    )

    assert calls == {"continue": 3, "recovery": 1}
    assert statuses == [("error", "blocked")]


def test_auxiliary_leader_scans_only_at_the_configured_interval(monkeypatch) -> None:
    from app import worker

    calls = {
        "continue": 0,
        "recovery": 0,
        "incremental": 0,
        "optimization": 0,
        "shadow": 0,
    }
    evaluation_calls: list[bool] = []
    clock = iter((0.0, 1.0, 6.0))

    @contextmanager
    def fake_session_scope() -> Iterator[object]:
        yield object()

    async def fake_process_one(**kwargs: object) -> bool:
        evaluation_calls.append(bool(kwargs.get("run_recovery")))
        return True

    def should_continue() -> bool:
        calls["continue"] += 1
        return calls["continue"] <= 3

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", fake_session_scope)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "touch_automation_worker_status",
        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: None,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "process_one", fake_process_one)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "recover_runtime_once",
        lambda: calls.__setitem__("recovery", calls["recovery"] + 1),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "dispatch_due_incremental_batches",
        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: calls.__setitem__(
            "incremental", calls["incremental"] + 1
        )
        or [],
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "optimization_worker_tick",
        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: calls.__setitem__(
            "optimization", calls["optimization"] + 1
        )
        or {"status": "idle"},
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "shadow_projection_worker_tick",
        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: calls.__setitem__(
            "shadow", calls["shadow"] + 1
        ),
    )
    worker.run_forever(
        poll_seconds=0,
        should_continue=should_continue,
        initialize_database=False,
        run_auxiliary_ticks=True,
        consume_jobs=False,
        register_worker_status=False,
        auxiliary_tick_seconds=5,
        monotonic_clock=lambda: next(clock),
    )

    assert calls == {
        "continue": 4,
        "recovery": 2,
        "incremental": 2,
        "optimization": 2,
        "shadow": 2,
    }
    assert evaluation_calls == []

def test_evaluation_worker_group_can_skip_auxiliary_queue_scans(monkeypatch) -> None:
    from app import worker

    calls = {"continue": 0, "optimization": 0, "shadow": 0}

    @contextmanager
    def fake_session_scope() -> Iterator[object]:
        yield object()

    async def fake_process_one(**_kwargs: object) -> bool:
        return False

    def should_continue() -> bool:
        calls["continue"] += 1
        return calls["continue"] == 1

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", fake_session_scope)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,

        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: None,
    )

        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: None,
    )

        ),
