    assert calls["shadow"] == 0

===== FILE backend/tests/test_shadow_projection_resolver.py =====
"""影子投影运行时适配器解析器的回归。

背景：影子投影链路的机制此前已完整（合同、清单、租约、幂等写、读回比对、回滚、
重试），唯一断点是 ``resolve_configured_shadow_projection_adapter`` 直接抛
``SHADOW_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE``，而它正是 worker 与 API 唯一使用的解析器。
本文件锁定接上之后的行为。

重点锁三件容易被悄悄写坏的事：

1. **写入真的落库。** 可写连接工厂必须与 ``engine.begin`` 同语义（退出即提交）。
   若只 connect/close，SQLAlchemy 2.0 会隐式回滚，写入静默丢失且不报错——
   读回比对会拿到空结果却看不出原因。
2. **schema 指纹从活库实算，不是把登记值回传。** 回传会让
   ``SHADOW_SCHEMA_DRIFT`` 门禁自证同一、形同废除。
3. **安全证据不是硬编码的。** 最小权限走实测；不是影子目标、不是 sql 适配器、
   表不存在，都必须拒绝而不是放行。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base
from app.external_datasources import (
    ExternalDatasourceError,
    compute_live_schema_fingerprint,
    reset_engine_cache,
    verify_least_privilege,
)
from app.models import ShadowProjectionTarget
from app.shadow_projection import (
    ShadowProjectionError,
    create_shadow_projection_target,
    resolve_configured_shadow_projection_adapter,
)

#: 影子表 DDL。列名由 SqlShadowProjectionAdapter.apply_batch 决定。
#: ``UNIQUE (batch_id, content_key)`` 是必需的：适配器靠 ``ON CONFLICT`` 做幂等
#: upsert（重试同一批次不产生重复行），缺这个约束 SQLite 会直接报
#: 「ON CONFLICT clause does not match any PRIMARY KEY or UNIQUE constraint」。
#: 目标表建表时必须带上，这也是投影目标 DDL 的一条硬要求。
_SHADOW_DDL = """
CREATE TABLE shadow_kg_tags (
    batch_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    content_key TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
    UNIQUE (batch_id, content_key)
)
"""


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _clean_engine_cache():
    reset_engine_cache()
    yield
    reset_engine_cache()


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _non_production_env(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    """SQLite 无账号权限模型，最小权限必然实测为不收敛。

    显式声明本地环境让解析器放行——这正是 ``_PRIVILEGE_WAIVER_ENVS`` 的用途。
    独立用例会单独验证生产环境下没有放行口。
    """
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV", "test")


@pytest.fixture
def db() -> Session:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    session = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        yield session
    finally:
        session.close()
        engine.dispose()


#: 影子目标登记行里存的**逻辑引用**。库里不准存 DSN
#: （见 shadow_projection._required 与 external_datasources.resolve_locator_to_dsn），
#: 真实连接串只配在部署侧环境变量里。
_LOCATOR = "kg_tags_shadow"
_LOCATOR_ENV = "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_KG_TAGS_SHADOW"


@pytest.fixture
def live_db(tmp_path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> str:
    """建一个真实存在的外部库（含影子表），把 DSN 配到部署侧环境变量。

    返回**逻辑引用**而不是 DSN——这正是登记行里能存的东西。
    """
    path = tmp_path / "downstream.db"
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{path}")
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(text(_SHADOW_DDL))
    engine.dispose()
    reset_engine_cache()
    monkeypatch.setenv(_LOCATOR_ENV, f"sqlite:///{path}")
    return _LOCATOR


@pytest.fixture
def live_db_dsn(live_db: str) -> str:
    """同一个库的真实 DSN，供测试自己开连接复查用。"""
    import os

    return os.environ[_LOCATOR_ENV]


def _register(
    db: Session,
    dsn: str,
    *,
    fingerprint: str,
    table_name: str = "shadow_kg_tags",
    adapter_key: str = "sql",
    target_key: str = "kg-tags-shadow",
):
    return create_shadow_projection_target(
        db,
        target_key=target_key,
        adapter_key=adapter_key,
        connection_locator=dsn,
        # SQLite 不使用口令认证，但登记要求该字段非空，故给占位引用。
        secret_reference="local-sqlite-no-password",
        schema_name="main",
        table_name=table_name,
        environment="shadow",
        shadow_only=True,
        owner="标签中台",
        schema_fingerprint=fingerprint,
        status="active",
        created_by="tester",
    )


# --- 1. 写入真的落库 -------------------------------------------------------


def test_resolved_adapter_actually_persists_rows(
    db: Session, live_db: str, live_db_dsn: str
) -> None:
    """解析出的适配器写入后，数据必须在**新连接**里读得到。

    用新 engine 复查，而不是复用适配器自己的连接——只有这样才能发现「事务未提交、
    关闭时被隐式回滚」这类静默丢失。
    """
    fingerprint = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
        live_db, None, table_name="shadow_kg_tags"
    )
    target = _register(db, live_db, fingerprint=fingerprint)
    adapter = resolve_configured_shadow_projection_adapter(target)

    # 注意 apply_batch 存的是**整行**的规范化 JSON（canonical_json(row)），
    # payload_hash 也由它自己按整行算（_hash(row)），并不取行里的同名字段。
    row = {"content_key": "ll-100001", "tag": "北欧风", "confidence": 0.91}
    adapter.apply_batch(batch_id="batch-001", rows=[row])

    # 关键：另开一个 engine 复查，确认已提交。
    reset_engine_cache()
    verifier = create_engine(live_db_dsn)
    with verifier.connect() as connection:
        persisted = connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT content_key, payload_json, payload_hash FROM shadow_kg_tags"
                " WHERE batch_id = :b"
            ),
            {"b": "batch-001"},
        ).all()
    verifier.dispose()

    assert len(persisted) == 1, "写入未落库——事务很可能被隐式回滚了"
    assert persisted[0].content_key == "ll-100001"
    assert json.loads(persisted[0].payload_json) == row
    assert persisted[0].payload_hash, "缺少内容指纹，读回比对无从做起"


def test_resolved_adapter_read_back_sees_written_rows(
    db: Session, live_db: str
) -> None:
    """适配器自己的读回比对也要拿到刚写的行——这是投影校验的基础。"""
    fingerprint = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
        live_db, None, table_name="shadow_kg_tags"
    )
    adapter = resolve_configured_shadow_projection_adapter(
        _register(db, live_db, fingerprint=fingerprint)
    )
    row = {"content_key": "ll-100002", "tag": "侘寂风"}
    adapter.apply_batch(batch_id="batch-002", rows=[row])
    # read_back 反序列化的就是整行，可以逐字段比对。
    assert adapter.read_back(batch_id="batch-002") == [row]


def test_resolved_adapter_rollback_removes_batch(db: Session, live_db: str) -> None:
    fingerprint = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
        live_db, None, table_name="shadow_kg_tags"
    )
    adapter = resolve_configured_shadow_projection_adapter(
        _register(db, live_db, fingerprint=fingerprint)
    )
    adapter.apply_batch(
        batch_id="batch-003",
        rows=[{"content_key": "ll-100003", "tag": "工业风"}],
    )
    assert adapter.read_back(batch_id="batch-003") != []
    adapter.rollback_batch(batch_id="batch-003")
    assert adapter.read_back(batch_id="batch-003") == []


# --- 2. 指纹从活库实算 -----------------------------------------------------


def test_evidence_fingerprint_comes_from_live_schema(
    db: Session, live_db: str
) -> None:
    """登记一个**错误**的指纹，证据里必须是活库真值，而不是登记值。

    若解析器把登记值原样回传，这个断言会失败——那种实现会让
    SHADOW_SCHEMA_DRIFT 门禁永远比对相等、彻底失效。
    """
    wrong_fingerprint = "0" * 64
    target = _register(db, live_db, fingerprint=wrong_fingerprint)
    adapter = resolve_configured_shadow_projection_adapter(target)

    live = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(live_db, None, table_name="shadow_kg_tags")
    evidence = adapter.verify_shadow_target()
    assert evidence.schema_fingerprint == live
    assert evidence.schema_fingerprint != wrong_fingerprint


def test_live_fingerprint_changes_when_schema_drifts(
    live_db: str, live_db_dsn: str
) -> None:
    """表结构变了指纹必须变，否则门禁拦不住漂移。"""
    before = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
        live_db, None, table_name="shadow_kg_tags"
    )
    engine = create_engine(live_db_dsn)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(text("ALTER TABLE shadow_kg_tags ADD COLUMN extra TEXT"))
    engine.dispose()
    reset_engine_cache()
    after = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(live_db, None, table_name="shadow_kg_tags")
    assert before != after


# --- 3. 安全证据不是硬编码 -------------------------------------------------


def test_sqlite_is_not_claimed_least_privileged(live_db: str) -> None:
    """SQLite 无权限模型，实测必须判为不收敛并给出原因（fail-closed）。"""
    收敛, 原因 = verify_least_privilege(
        live_db, None, table_name="shadow_kg_tags"
    )
    assert 收敛 is False
    assert "SQLite" in 原因


def test_production_env_has_no_privilege_waiver(
    db: Session, live_db: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    """生产环境下最小权限实测不通过就必须拒绝解析，没有放行口。"""
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV", "production")
    fingerprint = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
        live_db, None, table_name="shadow_kg_tags"
    )
    target = _register(db, live_db, fingerprint=fingerprint)
    adapter = resolve_configured_shadow_projection_adapter(target)
    # 解析本身不阻断，但证据必须诚实地报告未收敛，交由投影门禁拦截。
    assert adapter.verify_shadow_target().least_privileged is False


def test_unset_deploy_env_defaults_to_production(
    db: Session, live_db: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    """未声明部署环境时按最严处理，不能默认放行。"""
    monkeypatch.delenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV", raising=False)
    fingerprint = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
        live_db, None, table_name="shadow_kg_tags"
    )
    target = _register(db, live_db, fingerprint=fingerprint)
    assert (
        resolve_configured_shadow_projection_adapter(target)
        .verify_shadow_target()
        .least_privileged
        is False
    )


# --- 4. 拒绝不该解析的目标 -------------------------------------------------


def test_non_sql_adapter_key_is_rejected(db: Session, live_db: str) -> None:
    fingerprint = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
        live_db, None, table_name="shadow_kg_tags"
    )
    target = _register(db, live_db, fingerprint=fingerprint, adapter_key="fixture")
    with pytest.raises(ShadowProjectionError) as excinfo:
        resolve_configured_shadow_projection_adapter(target)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "SHADOW_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE"


def test_missing_table_is_rejected(db: Session, live_db: str) -> None:
    """登记的表在目标库里不存在时必须拒绝，而不是解析出一个必然写失败的适配器。"""
    fingerprint = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
        live_db, None, table_name="shadow_kg_tags"
    )
    target = _register(
        db, live_db, fingerprint=fingerprint, table_name="not_created_yet"
    )
    with pytest.raises(ShadowProjectionError) as excinfo:
        resolve_configured_shadow_projection_adapter(target)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "SHADOW_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("environment", "shadow_only"),
    [("production", False), ("production", True), ("shadow", False)],
)
def test_non_shadow_target_is_rejected(
    live_db: str, environment: str, shadow_only: bool
) -> None:
    """解析器必须自己复核环境与 shadow_only，不盲信传进来的目标对象。

    数据库层已有 CHECK 约束 ``ck_shadow_projection_target_environment`` 挡住
    非影子行落库（试图 UPDATE 成 production 会直接 IntegrityError），所以这里
    用**未落库**的对象来测——解析器是第二道防线，任何绕过登记路径构造出来的
    目标都不能换到可写适配器。
    """
    target = ShadowProjectionTarget(
        target_key="tampered",
        adapter_key="sql",
        connection_locator=live_db,
        secret_reference="local-sqlite-no-password",
        schema_name="main",
        table_name="shadow_kg_tags",
        environment=environment,
        shadow_only=shadow_only,
        owner="标签中台",
        schema_fingerprint="0" * 64,
        status="active",
        created_by="tester",
    )
    with pytest.raises(ShadowProjectionError) as excinfo:
        resolve_configured_shadow_projection_adapter(target)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "SHADOW_TARGET_UNREGISTERED"


# --- 5. 连接层自身的安全边界 -----------------------------------------------


def test_dsn_in_locator_is_rejected() -> None:
    """把 DSN 塞进 locator 必须被拒——库里只准存逻辑引用。

    这是 ``shadow_projection._required`` 已有不变式的连接层对应实现：DSN 与口令
    不得跟着数据行走。
    """
    with pytest.raises(ExternalDatasourceError) as excinfo:
        compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
            "mysql+pymysql://user:secret@host/db", None, table_name="t"
        )
    assert excinfo.value.code == "DATASOURCE_LOCATOR_NOT_LOGICAL"


def test_unconfigured_locator_fails_closed() -> None:
    """逻辑引用没有对应配置时必须报错并指明该设哪个变量，不能猜或回退默认库。"""
    with pytest.raises(ExternalDatasourceError) as excinfo:
        compute_live_schema_fingerprint("never_configured", None, table_name="t")
    assert excinfo.value.code == "DATASOURCE_NOT_CONFIGURED"
    assert "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_NEVER_CONFIGURED" in excinfo.value.message


def test_inline_password_in_deployed_dsn_is_rejected(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """部署侧 DSN 也不得内联口令——口令只能走 secret 引用。"""
    monkeypatch.setenv(
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_PG_TARGET", "postgresql+psycopg://u:pw@h/db"
    )
    with pytest.raises(ExternalDatasourceError) as excinfo:
        compute_live_schema_fingerprint("pg_target", None, table_name="t")
    # 未装 psycopg 时会先在驱动检查处被拦，两者都是合法的拒绝原因。
    assert excinfo.value.code in {
        "DATASOURCE_LOCATOR_HAS_SECRET",
        "DATASOURCE_DRIVER_MISSING",
    }


def test_table_name_injection_is_rejected(live_db: str) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ExternalDatasourceError) as excinfo:
        verify_least_privilege(live_db, None, table_name='x"; DROP TABLE y;--')
    assert excinfo.value.code == "DATASOURCE_TABLE_INVALID"


def test_disallowed_scheme_is_rejected(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    """部署侧配了不在白名单里的库类型时必须拒绝。"""
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_ORA_TARGET", "oracle://h/db")
    with pytest.raises(ExternalDatasourceError) as excinfo:
        compute_live_schema_fingerprint("ora_target", None, table_name="t")
    assert excinfo.value.code == "DATASOURCE_SCHEME_NOT_ALLOWED"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_snapshot_dedup_probe.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import importlib.util
import io
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from app.audit import canonical_json


SCRIPT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "probe-snapshot-dedup.py"
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("snapshot_dedup_probe", SCRIPT)
assert spec and spec.loader
probe = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
sys.modules[spec.name] = probe
spec.loader.exec_module(probe)


def _snapshot(case_id: int) -> dict:
    return {
        "schema_version": "production-run-snapshot-v1",
        "workflow": {
            "workflow_definition_id": 11,
            "workflow_version_id": 12,
            "version": "v3",
            "canonical_hash": "a" * 64,
            "manifest": {"nodes": [{"key": "source", "config": {"description": "机制" * 1000}}]},
        },
        "scripts": [{"script_key": "fixture", "version": "v1", "artifact_sha256": "b" * 64}],
        "runtime_context": {
            "input_manifest": {"case_id": case_id, "url": "sensitive fixture"},
            "nullable": None,
            "enabled": True,
            "disabled": False,
            "integer": 7,
            "decimal": 7.0,
            "array": [None, False, 1, {"x": "y"}],
        },
        "custom_missing_from_other_rows": {"status": "kept"},
    }


def test_probe_canonicalization_matches_existing_freeze_function():
    snapshot = _snapshot(1)
    assert probe.canonical_bytes(snapshot) == canonical_json(snapshot).encode("utf-8")


def test_repeated_mechanism_different_runtime_context_is_lossless_and_input_unchanged():
    first = _snapshot(1)
    second = _snapshot(2)
    second.pop("custom_missing_from_other_rows")
    raw_first = probe.canonical_bytes(first).decode("utf-8")
    raw_second = probe.canonical_bytes(second).decode("utf-8")
    rows = [
        {"id": 1, "snapshot_json": raw_first, "snapshot_hash": hashlib.sha256(raw_first.encode()).hexdigest()},
        {"id": 2, "snapshot_json": raw_second, "snapshot_hash": hashlib.sha256(raw_second.encode()).hexdigest()},
    ]
    before = json.dumps(rows, sort_keys=True)
    result = probe.probe_rows(iter(rows))
    assert json.dumps(rows, sort_keys=True) == before
    assert result["rows"] == result["canonical_hash_equal_rows"] == 2
    assert result["source_bytes_equal_rows"] == 2
    assert result["stored_hash_checked_rows"] == 2
    assert result["component_occurrences"] == 4
    assert result["unique_components"] == 2
    assert result["canonical_payload_byte_difference"] > 0
    assert "sensitive fixture" not in json.dumps(result)


def test_noncanonical_source_is_semantically_restored_but_not_byte_equal():
    snapshot = _snapshot(1)
    raw = json.dumps(snapshot, ensure_ascii=True, indent=2)
    result = probe.probe_rows([{"id": "one", "snapshot_json": raw}])
    assert result["canonical_hash_equal_rows"] == 1
    assert result["source_bytes_equal_rows"] == 0
    assert result["stored_hash_checked_rows"] == 0


def test_optional_v2_arrays_nulls_unknown_fields_and_missing_keys_roundtrip():
    source = {
        "workflow": {"manifest": {"steps": []}},
        "scripts": [],
        "prompts": [None, {"system_prompt": "x"}],
        "models": [{"p": 1.0}],
        "rules": [],
        "nodes": [{"enabled": True}],
        "runtime_context": {"all": [None, False, 0, 0.0, {}, []]},
        "unexpected": {"nested": [1, 2, 3]},
    }
    envelope, components = probe.split_snapshot(source)
    assert "runtime_context" in envelope["context"]
    assert set(envelope["refs"]) == {"workflow", "scripts", "prompts", "models", "rules", "nodes"}
    assert probe.rebuild_snapshot(envelope, components) == source
    assert probe.canonical_bytes(probe.rebuild_snapshot(envelope, components)) == probe.canonical_bytes(source)
    assert probe.probe_rows([{"id": 1, "snapshot_json": "{}"}])["unique_components"] == 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "raw",
    [
        "[]", "null", '{"workflow":1,"workflow":2}', '{"x":NaN}',
        '{"x":1e400}', '{"x":1e-1000}', '{"x":1.234567890123456789}',
        '{"x":"\\ud800"}', "{",
    ],
)
def test_invalid_or_ambiguous_snapshots_are_rejected(raw):
    with pytest.raises(probe.ProbeError):
        probe.probe_rows([{"id": 1, "snapshot_json": raw}])


def test_missing_changed_or_wrong_type_component_is_rejected():
    envelope, components = probe.split_snapshot(_snapshot(1))
    workflow_hash = envelope["refs"]["workflow"]
    for changed in (
        {},
        {**components, workflow_hash: b"{}"},
        {**components, workflow_hash: b"[]"},
        {**components, workflow_hash: b"\xff"},
    ):
        with pytest.raises(probe.ProbeError):
            probe.rebuild_snapshot(envelope, changed)
    with pytest.raises(probe.ProbeError):
        probe.rebuild_snapshot({"context": {"workflow": {}}, "refs": envelope["refs"]}, components)


def test_stored_hash_mismatch_and_all_limits_refuse_input():
    row = {"id": 1, "snapshot_json": '{"workflow":{}}', "snapshot_hash": "0" * 64}
    with pytest.raises(probe.ProbeError, match="stored snapshot hash mismatch"):
        probe.probe_rows([row])
    row.pop("snapshot_hash")
    for limits, rows in [
        (probe.Limits(max_rows=1), [row, row]),
        (probe.Limits(max_snapshot_bytes=3), [row]),
        (probe.Limits(max_total_input_bytes=3), [row]),
        (probe.Limits(max_unique_bytes=1), [row]),
    ]:
        with pytest.raises(probe.ProbeError):
            probe.probe_rows(rows, limits=limits)


def test_jsonl_has_bounded_lines_and_rejects_malformed_input():
    valid = json.dumps({"id": 1, "snapshot_json": "{}"}) + "\n"
    assert probe.probe_rows(probe.jsonl_rows(io.StringIO(valid)))["rows"] == 1
    with pytest.raises(probe.ProbeError):
        probe.probe_rows(probe.jsonl_rows(io.StringIO(valid), limits=probe.Limits(max_line_bytes=8)))
    with pytest.raises(probe.ProbeError):
        probe.probe_rows(probe.jsonl_rows(io.StringIO('{"id": 1,\n')))

===== FILE backend/tests/test_snapshot_import_common.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, MetaData, String, Table

from app.snapshot_import_common import (
    SnapshotImportError,
    source_batch,
    source_schema_compatibility_version,
    validate_source_schema,
)


def _compatibility_metadata() -> MetaData:
    metadata = MetaData()
    for table_name in (
        "users",
        "workflow_snapshot_blobs",
        "runtime_outbox_events",
    ):
        Table(table_name, metadata, Column("id", Integer, primary_key=True))
    return metadata


def _legacy_connection(*, migration_name: str) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    connection = sqlite3.connect(":memory:")
    connection.execute("CREATE TABLE users (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)")
    connection.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE schema_migrations ("
        "version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL)"
    )
    connection.execute(
        "INSERT INTO schema_migrations(version, name) VALUES (82, ?)",
        (migration_name,),
    )
    return connection


def test_legacy_nas_v82_uses_v81_structural_compatibility() -> None:
    connection = _legacy_connection(
        migration_name="scope_nas_source_uri_to_category"
    )
    try:
        assert source_schema_compatibility_version(connection) == 81
        assert validate_source_schema(
            connection,
            _compatibility_metadata(),
        ) == {"users": {"id"}}
        assert connection.execute(
            "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
        ).fetchone()[0] == 82
    finally:
        connection.close()


def test_current_v82_still_requires_runtime_foundation_tables() -> None:
    connection = _legacy_connection(migration_name="add_runtime_foundation")
    try:
        assert source_schema_compatibility_version(connection) == 82
        with pytest.raises(
            SnapshotImportError,
            match="runtime_outbox_events|workflow_snapshot_blobs",
        ):
            validate_source_schema(connection, _compatibility_metadata())
    finally:
        connection.close()


def test_v91_import_normalizes_legacy_incremental_package_like_direct_upgrade() -> None:
    metadata = MetaData()
    table = Table(
        "material_packages",
        metadata,
        Column("id", Integer, primary_key=True),
        Column("source", String),
        Column("status", String),
        Column("incremental_event_id", Integer),
        Column("incremental_snapshot_hash", String),
        Column("incremental_lane_key", String),
        Column("incremental_batch_size", Integer),
        Column("incremental_due_at", String),
        Column("incremental_retry_at", String),
        Column("incremental_failure_count", Integer),
    )
    connection = sqlite3.connect(":memory:")
    connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    connection.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE material_packages ("
        "id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, source TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL)"
    )
    connection.execute(
        "INSERT INTO material_packages(id, source, status) "
        "VALUES (1, 'production_import', 'active')"
    )
    try:
        rows = source_batch(
            connection,
            table,
            last_primary_key=None,
            batch_size=10,
            source_columns={"id", "source", "status"},
            source_schema_version=91,
        )
    finally:
        connection.close()

    assert rows == [
        {
            "id": 1,
            "source": "production_import",
            "status": "incremental_legacy",
            "incremental_event_id": None,
            "incremental_snapshot_hash": None,
            "incremental_lane_key": None,
            "incremental_batch_size": None,
            "incremental_due_at": None,
            "incremental_retry_at": None,
            "incremental_failure_count": 0,
        }
    ]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_snapshot_inline_nullable_migration.py =====
"""Deterministic schema-128 checks; no production connection is used."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect

from app import models  # noqa: F401 - register model metadata
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.migrations import mysql_baseline
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import (
    UPGRADE_SOURCE_VERSIONS,
    UPGRADE_TARGET_VERSIONS,
    mysql_metadata,
)
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS
from app.migrations.snapshot_inline_nullable import SnapshotInlineNullableError, migrate


def _old_schema(connection) -> None:
    connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "CREATE TABLE production_runs ("
        "id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL, "
        "snapshot_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL, snapshot_blob_id INTEGER)"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "CREATE TABLE run_children (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, run_id INTEGER NOT NULL "
        "REFERENCES production_runs(id))"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "CREATE TABLE run_audit (run_id INTEGER NOT NULL)"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "CREATE INDEX ix_runs_hash ON production_runs(snapshot_hash)"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "CREATE TRIGGER trg_runs_update AFTER UPDATE OF snapshot_json "
        "ON production_runs BEGIN INSERT INTO run_audit(run_id) VALUES (NEW.id); END"
    )


def _inline_required(connection) -> bool:
    return bool(next(row[3] for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "PRAGMA table_info(production_runs)"
    ) if row[1] == "snapshot_json"))


def test_v128_preserves_raw_snapshot_and_inbound_schema() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    raw_text = '{ "标题": "原文", "n": 1 }'
    digest = hashlib.sha256(raw_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    try:
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            _old_schema(connection)
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO production_runs VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (7, raw_text, digest, 17),
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO run_children VALUES (1, 7)")
            connection.commit()
            before = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT id, snapshot_json, snapshot_hash, snapshot_blob_id FROM production_runs"
            ).one()
            migrate(connection)
            assert not _inline_required(connection)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT id, snapshot_json, snapshot_hash, snapshot_blob_id FROM production_runs"
            ).one() == before
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_key_check").all() == []
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "PRAGMA foreign_key_list(run_children)"
            ).one()[2] == "production_runs"
            schema_names = {row[0] for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type IN ('index','trigger') "
                "AND tbl_name='production_runs'"
            )}
            assert {"ix_runs_hash", "trg_runs_update"} <= schema_names
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "UPDATE production_runs SET snapshot_json=NULL WHERE id=7"
            )
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql("SELECT run_id FROM run_audit").all() == [(7,)]
            migrate(connection)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT snapshot_json FROM production_runs WHERE id=7"
            ).scalar_one() is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_v128_preexisting_shadow_is_not_deleted() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    try:
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            _old_schema(connection)
            connection.exec_driver_sql("CREATE TABLE production_runs_v128_new (marker TEXT)")
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO production_runs_v128_new VALUES ('keep')"
            )
            connection.commit()
            with pytest.raises(SnapshotInlineNullableError, match="shadow"):
                migrate(connection)
            assert _inline_required(connection)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT marker FROM production_runs_v128_new"
            ).scalar_one() == "keep"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_v128_failed_foreign_key_check_rolls_back_original_table() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    try:
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            _old_schema(connection)
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO production_runs VALUES (7, '{}', 'hash', NULL)"
            )
            connection.commit()
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=OFF")
            connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO run_children VALUES (1, 999)")
            connection.commit()
            connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
            with pytest.raises(SnapshotInlineNullableError, match="foreign-key check"):
                migrate(connection)
            assert _inline_required(connection)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT snapshot_json FROM production_runs WHERE id=7"
            ).scalar_one() == "{}"
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE name='ix_runs_hash'"
            ).scalar_one() == "ix_runs_hash"
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_keys").scalar_one() == 1
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE name='production_runs_v128_new'"
            ).scalar_one_or_none() is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_v128_partial_fixture_and_new_database() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    try:
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            migrate(connection)
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            assert inspect(connection).get_columns("production_runs")
            assert not _inline_required(connection)
            migrate(connection)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_v128_version_and_mysql_upgrade_gate() -> None:
    v128 = next(migration for migration in MIGRATIONS if migration.version == 128)
    assert v128.name == "snapshot_inline_nullable"
    assert 127 in UPGRADE_SOURCE_VERSIONS
    assert 128 in UPGRADE_TARGET_VERSIONS
    mysql_column = mysql_metadata().tables["production_runs"].c.snapshot_json
    assert type(mysql_column.type).__name__ == "LONGTEXT"
    assert mysql_column.type.collation == "utf8mb4_0900_as_cs"
    assert mysql_column.nullable


def test_v127_ledger_upgrades_once_to_v128() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    try:
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            _old_schema(connection)
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, "
                "name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL)"
            )
            for migration in (item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version < 128):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO schema_migrations VALUES (?, ?)",
                    (migration.version, migration.name),
                )
            connection.commit()
            run_migrations(connection)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
            ).scalar_one() == MIGRATIONS[-1].version
            assert not _inline_required(connection)
            run_migrations(connection)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM schema_migrations WHERE version=128"
            ).scalar_one() == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_version_gate_rejects_old_image_and_old_schema(monkeypatch) -> None:
    # Exercise the version comparison without a MySQL connection.
    monkeypatch.setattr(mysql_baseline, "_require_mysql", lambda _: None)
    connection = Mock()
    connection.exec_driver_sql.return_value.scalar_one_or_none.return_value = 128
    with pytest.raises(mysql_baseline.MySQLBaselineError, match="期望 127，实际 128"):
        mysql_baseline.upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=127)
    connection.exec_driver_sql.return_value.scalar_one_or_none.return_value = 127
    with pytest.raises(mysql_baseline.MySQLBaselineError, match="期望 128，实际 127"):
        mysql_baseline.assert_mysql_schema_version(connection, expected=128)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_snapshot_shadow_archive.py =====
"""Offline shadow archive round trips and fail-closed checks."""

import gzip
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import stat
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest


SCRIPT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "snapshot-shadow-archive.py"
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("snapshot_shadow_archive", SCRIPT)
archive = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
sys.modules[spec.name] = archive
spec.loader.exec_module(archive)


def private_path(tmp_path):
    directory = tmp_path / "private"
    directory.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    directory.chmod(0o700)
    return directory / "archive.sqlite"


def row(identifier, context, *, raw=None):
    snapshot = {
        "workflow": {"name": "same", "version": 1},
        "scripts": [{"id": "script", "content": "shared"}],
        "prompts": [], "models": [], "rules": [], "nodes": [],
        "runtime_context": context,
    }
    source = raw if raw is not None else archive.probe.canonical_bytes(snapshot).decode("utf-8")
    return {"id": identifier, "snapshot_json": source,
            "snapshot_hash": archive.probe._hash(archive.probe.canonical_bytes(snapshot))}


def test_persistent_dedup_reopen_and_exact_contexts(tmp_path):
    path = private_path(tmp_path)
    rows = [row(1, {"run": "A"}), row(2, {"run": "B"})]
    originals = [dict(item) for item in rows]
    result = archive.build_archive(rows, path)
    assert rows == originals
    assert result["rows"] == 2
    assert result["unique_components"] == 3
    assert stat.S_IMODE(path.stat().st_mode) == 0o600
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as conn:
        assert conn.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM components").fetchone()[0] == 3
        assert conn.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM runs").fetchone()[0] == 2
    assert archive.load_row(path, 1) == rows[0]
    assert archive.load_row(path, 2) == rows[1]
    assert archive.verify_archive(path) == result


def test_noncanonical_source_gets_explicit_raw_override(tmp_path):
    path = private_path(tmp_path)
    snapshot = json.loads(row("x", {"run": 1})["snapshot_json"])
    source = json.dumps(snapshot, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    item = row("x", {"run": 1}, raw=source)
    archive.build_archive([item], path)
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as conn:
        assert conn.execute("SELECT raw_override IS NOT NULL FROM runs").fetchone()[0] == 1
    assert archive.load_row(path, "x") == item
    assert archive.verify_archive(path)["rows"] == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("damage", ["missing", "changed", "wrong_hash", "bad_envelope", "bad_source", "same_length_raw", "unknown_ref", "zip_bomb"])
def test_damage_is_rejected(tmp_path, damage):
    path = private_path(tmp_path)
    snapshot = json.loads(row("x", {"a": 1})["snapshot_json"])
    item = row("x", {"a": 1}, raw=json.dumps(snapshot, indent=2))
    archive.build_archive([item], path)
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as conn:
        if damage == "missing":
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM components WHERE digest=(SELECT digest FROM components LIMIT 1)")
        elif damage == "changed":
            conn.execute("UPDATE components SET payload=? WHERE digest=(SELECT digest FROM components LIMIT 1)",
                         (gzip.compress(b"{}"),))
        elif damage == "wrong_hash":
            conn.execute("UPDATE runs SET snapshot_hash=?", ("0" * 64,))
        elif damage == "bad_envelope":
            conn.execute("UPDATE runs SET envelope=?", (gzip.compress(b"{}"),))
        elif damage == "unknown_ref":
            packed = gzip.decompress(conn.execute("SELECT envelope FROM runs").fetchone()[0])
            envelope = json.loads(packed)
            envelope["refs"]["unrecognized"] = "0" * 64
            encoded = archive.probe.canonical_bytes(envelope)
            conn.execute("UPDATE runs SET envelope=?, envelope_size=?", (gzip.compress(encoded), len(encoded)))
        elif damage == "zip_bomb":
            conn.execute("UPDATE runs SET envelope=?", (gzip.compress(b"x" * 5_000_000),))
        elif damage == "same_length_raw":
            original = gzip.decompress(conn.execute("SELECT raw_override FROM runs").fetchone()[0]).decode("utf-8")
            changed = original.replace('": ', '" :', 1)
            assert changed != original and len(changed.encode("utf-8")) == len(original.encode("utf-8"))
            assert archive.probe.canonical_bytes(archive.probe._parse_json(changed)) == archive.probe.canonical_bytes(archive.probe._parse_json(original))
            conn.execute("UPDATE runs SET raw_override=?", (gzip.compress(changed.encode("utf-8")),))
        else:
            conn.execute("UPDATE runs SET raw_override=?", (gzip.compress(b"{}"),))
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError):
        archive.verify_archive(path)
    if damage == "same_length_raw":
        with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError, match="source byte hash"):
            archive.load_row(path, "x")


def test_limits_and_midstream_failure_leave_only_incomplete_archive(tmp_path):
    first = row(1, {"run": 1})

    def breaks():
        yield first
        raise RuntimeError("source interrupted")

    path = private_path(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        archive.build_archive(breaks(), path)
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError, match="incomplete"):
        archive.verify_archive(path)
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as conn:
        assert conn.execute("SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key='state'").fetchone()[0] == "incomplete"
    path2 = path.parent / "small.sqlite"
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError):
        archive.build_archive([first], path2, limits=archive.Limits(max_snapshot_bytes=10))
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError):
        archive.verify_archive(path2)
    path3 = path.parent / "rows.sqlite"
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError, match="row limit"):
        archive.build_archive([first, row(2, {"run": 2})], path3,
                              limits=archive.Limits(max_rows=1))
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError):
        archive.verify_archive(path3)
    path4 = path.parent / "total.sqlite"
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError, match="total input"):
        archive.build_archive([first], path4,
                              limits=archive.Limits(max_total_input_bytes=10))
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError):
        archive.verify_archive(path4)
    path5 = path.parent / "unique.sqlite"
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError, match="unique component"):
        archive.build_archive([first], path5,
                              limits=archive.Limits(max_unique_bytes=1))
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError):
        archive.verify_archive(path5)


def test_bad_input_hash_and_duplicate_ids_fail_closed(tmp_path):
    path = private_path(tmp_path)
    bad = row(1, {"run": 1})
    bad["snapshot_hash"] = "0" * 64
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError, match="hash"):
        archive.build_archive([bad], path)
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError):
        archive.verify_archive(path)
    path2 = path.parent / "duplicate.sqlite"
    one = row(1, {"run": 1})
    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
        archive.build_archive([one, one], path2)
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError):
        archive.verify_archive(path2)


def test_path_permissions_symlink_and_overwrite(tmp_path):
    public = tmp_path / "public"
    public.mkdir(mode=0o755)
    public.chmod(0o755)
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError):
        archive.build_archive([], public / "archive.sqlite")
    path = private_path(tmp_path)
    archive.build_archive([], path)
    original = path.read_bytes()
    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        archive.build_archive([], path)
    assert path.read_bytes() == original
    link = path.parent / "link.sqlite"
    link.symlink_to(path)
    with pytest.raises((FileExistsError, archive.ArchiveError)):
        archive.build_archive([], link)
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError):
        archive.verify_archive(link)
    path.chmod(0o644)
    with pytest.raises(archive.ArchiveError):
        archive.verify_archive(path)


def test_stdin_cli_create_and_verify_without_row_output(tmp_path):
    path = private_path(tmp_path)
    item = row(9, {"run": "private synthetic value"})
    result = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, str(SCRIPT), "create", str(path)],
        input=json.dumps(item) + "\n", text=True, capture_output=True, check=False,
    )
    assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr
    assert "private synthetic value" not in result.stdout + result.stderr
    assert json.loads(result.stdout)["rows"] == 1
    verify = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, str(SCRIPT), "verify", str(path)],
        text=True, capture_output=True, check=False,
    )
    assert verify.returncode == 0, verify.stderr
    assert json.loads(verify.stdout)["rows"] == 1

import pytest

from app.source_identity_probe import SourceIdentityProbeError, build_three_d_su_identity_probe


def test_probe_contains_count_null_duplicate_and_res_id_queries() -> None:
    bundle = build_three_d_su_identity_probe("aliyun_3d66_dw.dim_res_info_union")
    assert set(bundle.queries) == {"scope", "nulls", "duplicates", "res_id_conflicts"}
    assert "res_type IN (1, 6)" in bundle.queries["scope"]
    assert "GROUP BY res_type, ll_id" in bundle.queries["duplicates"]
    assert "COUNT(DISTINCT res_id)" in bundle.queries["res_id_conflicts"]
    assert len(bundle.probe_hash) == 64


def test_probe_hash_is_stable() -> None:
    first = build_three_d_su_identity_probe("aliyun_3d66_dw.dim_res_info_union")
    second = build_three_d_su_identity_probe("aliyun_3d66_dw.dim_res_info_union")
    assert first.probe_hash == second.probe_hash


@pytest.mark.parametrize("table", ["x;DROP TABLE y", "x y", "`secret`", "x/../y"])
def test_probe_rejects_unsafe_identifiers(table: str) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(SourceIdentityProbeError, match="表名"):
        build_three_d_su_identity_probe(table)

from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from app.readonly_sources import SqlReadOnlySourceAdapter, SourceRow, FixtureReadOnlySourceAdapter, poll_upstream_source
from test_readonly_sources import db, sessions, source_contract


def test_change_cursor_reads_low_id_updates_and_delete():
    engine = create_engine('sqlite://')
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        conn.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE changes (seq INTEGER, content_id TEXT, source_version TEXT, category_key TEXT, occurred_at TEXT, event_type TEXT)')
        conn.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO changes VALUES (1,'99','v1','space_image','2026-09-01','content.created'),(2,'1','v2','space_image','2026-09-02','content.updated'),(3,'1','v3','space_image','2026-09-03','content.deleted')")
    @contextmanager
    def factory():
        with engine.connect() as conn:
            conn.exec_driver_sql('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
            yield conn
    adapter = SqlReadOnlySourceAdapter(connection_factory=factory, table_name='changes', field_mappings={x:x for x in ['content_id','source_version','category_key','occurred_at','event_type']} | {'change_cursor':'seq'}, schema_fingerprint='f'*64, cursor_definition={'mode':'change','field':'change_cursor'})
    first = adapter.fetch_page(cursor=None, limit=1)
    second = adapter.fetch_page(cursor=first.next_cursor, limit=2)
    assert [(r.content_id,r.event_type) for r in second.rows] == [('1','content.updated'),('1','content.deleted')]
    assert second.next_cursor.values == {'change_cursor':3}

def test_replay_conflict_and_audit(db, source_contract):

    from app.models import UpstreamReadRun, AuditEvent
    from app.readonly_sources import ReadOnlySourceError
    run = UpstreamReadRun(source_contract_id=source_contract.id, source_contract_hash=source_contract.contract_hash, category_key=source_contract.category_key, requested_limit=1, status='succeeded', actor='admin')
    db.add(run); db.flush()
    row = quarantine_source_row(db, contract=source_contract, run=run, payload={'content_id':'recover','source_version':'v1','category_key':'space_image','occurred_at':'2026-09-01'}, error=ValueError())

    with pytest.raises(ReadOnlySourceError, match='状态'):
        replay_source_row(db, row_id=row.id, source_contract_id=source_contract.id, category_key='space_image', expected_lock_version=1, reason='again', actor='admin')
    assert db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.category=='source_recovery')) is not None

def test_mysql_uses_dialect_quotes_and_bound_text():
    from sqlalchemy.dialects.mysql import dialect as mysql_dialect
    from sqlalchemy.sql.elements import TextClause
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    class Connection:
        dialect = mysql_dialect()
        def exec_driver_sql(self, sql):
            assert sql == 'SELECT @@transaction_read_only'
            return SimpleNamespace(scalar_one=lambda:1)
        def execute(self, statement, params):
            assert isinstance(statement, TextClause)
            compiled = str(statement.compile(dialect=self.dialect))
            assert '`order`' in compiled and '%s' in compiled
            assert params == {'limit':1}
            return SimpleNamespace(mappings=lambda:SimpleNamespace(all=lambda:[]))
    @contextmanager
    def factory():
        yield Connection()
    adapter=SqlReadOnlySourceAdapter(connection_factory=factory,table_name='order',field_mappings={x:x for x in ['content_id','source_version','category_key','occurred_at']},schema_fingerprint='f'*64)
    assert adapter.fetch_page(cursor=None,limit=1).rows == ()

def test_replay_checks_scope_reason_version_and_busy(db, source_contract):
    from app.source_recovery import quarantine_source_row, replay_source_row
    from app.models import UpstreamReadRun
    from app.readonly_sources import ReadOnlySourceError
    run=UpstreamReadRun(source_contract_id=source_contract.id,source_contract_hash=source_contract.contract_hash,category_key='space_image',requested_limit=1,status='running',actor='admin')
    db.add(run); db.flush()
    row=quarantine_source_row(db,contract=source_contract,run=run,payload={'content_id':'a','source_version':'v1','category_key':'space_image','occurred_at':'2026-09-01'},error=ValueError())
    args=dict(row_id=row.id,source_contract_id=source_contract.id,category_key='space_image',expected_lock_version=1,reason='restore',actor='admin')
    for overrides,code in [({'reason':' '},'SOURCE_RECOVERY_REASON_REQUIRED'),({'category_key':'wrong'},'SOURCE_RECOVERY_SCOPE_MISMATCH'),({},'SOURCE_RECOVERY_BUSY')]:
        with pytest.raises(ReadOnlySourceError) as exc:
            replay_source_row(db,**(args|overrides))
        assert exc.value.code == code
    assert row.attempt_count == 0
